| **Dragino S31-LB (design and manufacturer examples)** | One SHT31 physical probe provides **temperature and humidity** while the S31-LB node sends reports. Who is physical observing unit, who is network participant, and what is only a declared possible alarm/setting? | Claim Dragino is SAPIENT/WoT/SSN conformant, or create multiple physical devices from two fields/one Node type. |
| **AirGradient Open Air Max (design and distinct published device/cloud examples)** | Multiple physical sensing modules and **two PMS5003s** report through local electronics and possible cloud services. What if a cloud record is processed or mediated? What is source-backed raw electrode versus calibrated value? | Assume example cloud data are an authenticated WoT Digital Twin, current hardware state or validated ozone/nitrogen dioxide measurement. |
| **Campbell CR1000X + ClimaVue 50 (reference design and unjoined published examples)** | ClimaVue senses while the **CR1000X** may process/carry forward/aggregate. How distinguish **Observer, method, reporter, status provider and Host**? | Treat logger PanelTemp as environmental air, untested historic program as installed, or publisher MQTT status as fielded ClimaVue Observation. |
| **Bennett's Point (two actual selected 2024 station summaries + separate equipment history)** | Persistent station **acebpmet**, changing EE181 serials, logger averaging and GOES/CDMO publishing require separate **Observer/Host, result, source time, equipment-use association**. Two historical summary rows have a nominal **derived interval** and original parameter QA; original resultTime is absent. | Pretend station rows themselves contain serials, exact replacement minute or resultTime; treat four server Observation resources as four independent NOAA measurements; silently select station-led/probe-led DataStream binding. |

### 18.5 Consequences for the three OPEN Step 10 boundaries

**A — physical equipment primary.** OMS directly distinguishes the **individual Observer** from reusable methods; SSN models Systems/Sensors, but permits non-hardware observers, while SAPIENT Node may be software/fusion. WoT proxies can use new IDs. A purely physical-root model risks losing **who actually performed or reported an event**. Yet none of these authors requires a distinct physical System for each observation or network message. **A stays OPEN.**

**B — observing/reporting arrangement primary.** OMS gives the strongest **Observer + ObservingProcedure + Observation + feature/property/result/time** semantics, and SSN gives related ontology links. SAPIENT distinguishes **capability versus selected state versus Task/TaskAck**, while WoT supplies Property/Action/Event affordances and intermediaries. This improves an arrangement-oriented model but also creates **multiple noninterchangeable participants**: physical sensor, logger, reporting Node, proxy/digital twin, publisher and actual observer. No reviewed source settles **CSAPI DataStream.system ownership**. **B stays OPEN.**

**C — continuing installation/station primary.** OMS's **Host** can describe an actual environmental monitoring facility and its **Deployment** can express Observer-to-Host association. The SSN 2017 ontology includes hosting/deployment; the 2026 draft proposes an Asset-centered improvement. This is useful for Bennett's station continuity, but **cannot be imposed on uninstalled reference products**. A Host can be an organizational survey, not just a physical enclosure. **C stays OPEN.**

**Additional donor-led possibility, not a chosen fourth chassis:** one could keep distinct **equipment, observing activity, host/installation and reporting/mediation referents**, with relationships and source authority explicitly qualified, rather than picking a unique universal object that all fields attach to. Such a flexible model could also become vague and untestable. **Step 15 must evaluate this possibility against specific source evidence and acceptance tests before any choice.**

### 18.6 Open issues and exact stopping point

- **SAPIENT published/schema conflicts remain OPEN:** printed **Registration Table 15 field 9 versus pinned protobuf field 10**, and **message-content timestamp versus sent timestamp**. The protobuf fields were directly read in this step. No corrigendum, wire layout or temporal interpretation was invented.
- **OMS average/time remains OPEN for Bennett:** the published station's fixed-EST stamp and 15-minute means permit a nominal **derived interval** but not a genuine independently evidenced **resultTime**; earlier 52°North/pygeoapi scalar JSON and archival-context bridge limitations remain unchanged.
- **W3C SSN source edition remains qualified:** the **2017 Recommendation** and **26 October 2026 Working Draft (“2023 Edition”)** differ in vocabulary and status. The exact 26 Oct draft was identified via W3C publication/index and text excerpts, but direct whole-page retrieval failed. In particular **draft OMS/CSAPI alignments and draft non-normative capabilities** are **not** published CSAPI Profile obligations.
- **WoT TD/Architecture referent scope remains qualified:** TD and Thing Model describe interactions; proxies/twins may cache or re-identify, but these are **supported architecture patterns**, not evidence our four manufacturers expose WoT interfaces.
- **Broader donor corpus not silently closed:** this is four targeted lineages with companion editions, **not** 182 independently evaluated standards. Preserve uncertainty and the source-specific identity/physical/temporal meaning gaps.

**Step 14 delivered:** original standard review with edition/authority boundaries, **thirteen source-owned findings**, explicit non-equivalences, four case comparisons, competing-frame consequences and open semantic decisions. No candidate DIM/Profile text, accepted chassis, Glaux code, cloud deployment, live CSAPI server, existing controlled corpus, PITON or original source record was modified. **Gate C stays OPEN pending Step 15.**

**Historical Step 14 handoff; Step 15 now complete in [§19](#19-step-15--phase-c-comparison-bounded-working-subject-and-gate-c):** — review **A/B/C and the additional multi-referent/management/provenance possibility** against our four source-native cases and **the actual original-donor coverage from Steps 11–14**. Either state a **bounded working subject/structure with exclusions and overturn conditions** or preserve an honestly unresolved choice; perform Gate C review. Do not build the integrated DIM or start the CSAPI Profile/Glaux implementation in Step 15.


## 19. Step 15 — Phase C comparison, bounded working subject and Gate C

**Status: Step 15 COMPLETE, 2 November 2026. Gate C: REACHED WITH A BOUNDED WORKING SUBJECT AND EXPLICIT LIMITATIONS.** This is a decision about the **working approach for the first integrated candidate Domain Information Model (DIM)** in Phase D, **not** the adoption of a final class, formal ontology, Profile requirement, vendor standard, or conformance result. Inputs: the four original source arrangements [§§9–12](#9-step-05--dragino-s31-lb-source-native-reference-system), their neutral comparison [§13](#13-step-09--four-source-native-arrangements-compared-without-choosing-a-chassis), the three competing boundaries [§14](#14-step-10--three-competing-ways-to-bound-the-whole-sensing-system-not-a-chassis-decision), and the original targeted donor reviews [§15 AAS/OPC](#15-step-11--original-aas-opc-ua-devices-and-pa-dim-donor-native-whole-system-questions), [§16 ISA/Open Group SOSA](#16-step-12--isa-60-and-the-open-group-sosa-physical-functional-visible-and-managed-relationships), [§17 IEEE/SensorML](#17-step-13--ieee-1451-transducerchannel-evidence-and-sensorml-30-processsystem-semantics), and [§18 SAPIENT/OMS/SSN/WoT](#18-step-14--sapient-v2-oms-30-w3cogc-sosa-ssn-and-web-of-things-observation-action-time-and-representation). No independently new field data or normative cross-standard mappings were acquired here.

### 19.1 Plain-language decision: what is the whole we are describing?

**No single manufactured enclosure, observation stream/function, or permanent monitoring station is the universal whole.** Dragino describes a radio node plus an external probe; AirGradient describes a multi-device assembled monitor; Campbell describes a separate logger and weather instrument working together; Bennett documents an actual persistent station with replaceable probes. None can be forced into one of those three exclusive roots without fabricating a unit, suppressing a real relationship, or confusing a reporter with its physical source.

**Working subject:** a **source-grounded, bounded, equipment-realized sensing System/arrangement**, whose defining purpose includes sensing/observing and whose relevant equipment, observing/processing, communications, power, configuration, management, reporting and life-cycle relations can be described **only as the source evidence warrants**. The source must establish **which subject is in focus** and its standing: **reference product/arrangement**, **actual identified individual equipment**, or **actual identified station/installation**. **A reference arrangement is not an authenticated fielded System instance**. This is a provisional description of our focus, **not a minted domain name or adopted superclass**; retain the original provisional “system and sensor and equipment” description until justified.

**Selected working approach D — a declared focal subject with separately identifiable, typed, evidence-qualified relationships.** A description focuses on one source-declared whole or reference arrangement and preserves other physical units, observing roles, hosts, processors, managers, publishers/proxies and source records **as distinct, only when supported**. It does **not** require every focus to instantiate all of those views; it does **not** define a generic graph of everything; and it **does not** assert a physical system from an API resource ID. This is the **preferred structural method for drafting**, subject to falsification in Steps 16–24, rather than choosing an irrevocable single DIM root.

### 19.2 Fair comparative disposition of the alternatives

| Alternative | Strongest case/source evidence | Failure when forced as the exclusive root | Step 15 result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **A: one physical equipment unit or assembly** | Dragino node/external probe, AirGradient physical multi-sensor product; AAS asset/type/instance, OPC UA Devices/PA-DIM equipment information, IEEE module/channel. | Campbell has **two separately manufactured devices**, not a single proven installed assembly. Bennett station identity continues when an EE181 physical probe changes. Reporting and proxy identities remain outside a single device. | **Useful physical-equipment viewpoint**, not the sole whole-System referent. |
| **B: observation/functional reporting arrangement** | Campbell sensor → logger transformation; Bennett sensed value → logger average → GOES/CDMO published summary; SensorML processes, OMS Observer/Procedure, SAPIENT capability/action, IEEE channel. | One AirGradient device supports many channels; defining a whole per value fragments common firmware, battery, management and physical history. Stream/publisher identity does not necessarily identify the physical observer. | **Necessary roles and processing relationships**, not a universal System root per stream. |
| **C: permanent station/installation** | Bennett **acebpmet** stable across the 16 April 2024 probe swap; OMS Host/Deployment and SensorML Deployment. | Dragino, AirGradient and Campbell are **manufacturer reference cases**, not authenticated deployments. A station-first model would fabricate sites and persistent unit identities; OMS Host may also be an organizational grouping. | **Valid when the source establishes a continuing station or Host**, not mandatory for every product. |
| **D: source-bounded focal System with distinct typed related subjects** | All four cases can be described at their actual evidence level; AAS separates represented asset from shell, ISA visibility from physical member, SOSA physical from functional and host, OMS Observer from Host, WoT publisher/proxy from underlying Thing. | Could become an untestable “model everything” graph or a hollow generic facade **unless the focus, inclusion rule, identities, evidence authority, exclusions and falsification tests are enforced**. | **SELECT AS BOUNDED WORKING STRUCTURAL APPROACH**, not adopted DIM classes, Profile obligations or interoperability proof. |

**Why D rather than an unexamined compromise?** A/B/C each misdescribe at least one of four actual source cases if made universally primary. D is chosen **only insofar as it keeps one explicitly justified focus and preserves each other role as an independently described referent or association with source/provenance**. A description fails if an integrator cannot answer what the focus is or which evidence supports its asserted relationships. No standards body defines D as an accepted cross-standard ontology; **this is our falsifiable synthesis**, not donor-authored equivalence.

### 19.3 Four-case screen and the exact evidence boundaries

| Case | Source-supported focus under D | Required distinctions and honest unknowns | Fit standing |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Dragino S31-LB** — [§9](#9-step-05--dragino-s31-lb-source-native-reference-system) | Manufacturer **reference** node with one external cabled **SHT31** measuring both temperature and humidity. | Radio node is not the external probe; two values are not two physical probes. FPort payload types, capacity and control possibilities do not establish installed serial, active settings, command result or live history. External LoRaWAN gateway not manufactured inside node. | **Fits documented reference scope**; **NOT TESTED for an individual**. |
| **AirGradient Open Air Max** — [§10](#10-step-06--airgradient-open-air-max-source-native-reference-system) | Manufacturer **reference** gas-equipped assembled monitor **O-M-1PPSTON-CE**, physical main/sensor modules and **two PMS5003s**. | Raw gas electrode vs verified concentration; PM channel count vs physical device count; cellular batches, Wi-Fi example and separate cloud examples; firmware/hardware editions, battery/panel support vs current condition. No authenticated installed unit. | **Fits documented reference scope**; **NOT TESTED for a fielded/calibrated instance**. |
| **CR1000X + first-generation ClimaVue 50** — [§11](#11-step-07--campbell-cr1000x-and-first-generation-climavue-50-source-native-arrangement) | **Reference two-device pairing**, *not* a newly minted identified weather station. | Separately connected integrated ClimaVue and programmable CR1000X; fourteen aR7! quantities are not hardware count. Logger PanelTemp is not ambient; program-transformed or carried-forward values are not fresh direct sensor readings; 2019 program is not joined to 2026 publisher logger-status example. | **Fits reference pairing only**; **NOT TESTED for an installed station**. |
| **Bennett's Point** — [§12](#12-step-08--bennetts-point-actual-station-time-scoped-probes-and-historical-temperature-reports) | **Actual historic continuing station acebpmet**, logger **13916**, TX321 transmitter, with identifiable serial-numbered EE181 probes serving a temporal role. | EE181 serial **222016000109CE** used before **16 Apr 2024**, later **21281600077814**; same station continues. Only **two actual selected NOAA temperature means** underlie **four test-server representations**. Probe attribution/nominal UTC windows are **DERIVED**, and exact swap minute, native resultTime and probe-specific spatial geometry remain **UNKNOWN**. | **Fits bounded actual station history**, **NOT TESTED as a complete operating System or final DataStream ownership rule**. |

**These are analytical fit checks, not software test PASS results.** The permitted inference is that D can describe the documented arrangements **without needing to invent the other case's missing identity or state**. We have *not* constructed a connected DIM schema, mapped the original four sources through a common profile-aware client, or independently confirmed vendor conformity with any donor.

### 19.4 Fair donor comparison: native contributions, not votes

| Original target reviewed in Steps 11–14 | Distinction that challenged the exclusive roots | Equivalences not adopted |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **AAS Part 1 v3.2; OPC UA Devices 1.05.0 and PA-DIM 1.02.0** — [§15](#15-step-11--original-aas-opc-ua-devices-and-pa-dim-donor-native-whole-system-questions) | **Asset/type/instance versus Shell representation**, independently described/submanaged entities; **connected-to** versus contained-in; online versus offline state; equipment signals and condition. | Asset Shell = hardware; manufacturer Model = OPC DeviceType; PA-DIM Signal = one physical sensor; configured value = current state. |
| **ISA 6.0; The Open Group SOSA RA 1.1 / separate DSDM 1.0** — [§16](#16-step-12--isa-60-and-the-open-group-sosa-physical-functional-visible-and-managed-relationships) | Software/network participant may represent **opaque/virtual** hardware members; whole multi-element **SOSA Sensor** versus **Host Platform**; physical/software Component, functional Module, Product/implementation, System Manager vs Task Manager. | Visible member = installed sensor; host = part of Sensor; defense SOSA's five-modality Sensor = universal DIM superclass; W3C SOSA = Open Group SOSA. |
| **IEEE 1451 public 2024-linked resources and approved SensorML 3.0** — [§17](#17-step-13--ieee-1451-transducerchannel-evidence-and-sensorml-30-processsystem-semantics) | TIM/module/channel metadata, quantities/timing/calibration; **physical versus computational process**, process type/individual, components, outputs, methods, configuration, history, Deployment. | Channel = chip = CSAPI DataStream; TEDS published range = measured value; SensorML process description = executed observation; public Playground labels = uninspected normative IEEE SHALLs. |
| **BSI SAPIENT v2 and original OMS 3.0** — [§18](#18-step-14--sapient-v2-oms-30-w3cogc-sosa-ssn-and-web-of-things-observation-action-time-and-representation) | SAPIENT **Node sender** vs physical equipment and supported/current/task/ack/outcome; OMS **Observer, Procedure, feature, Host, Deployment, phenomenonTime, resultTime**. | node_id = physical serial; content/send timestamp conflict resolved; host = manufactured sensor; observer = publisher; average timestamp = known result availability. |
| **W3C/OGC SSN 2017 Recommendation / 2026 Working Draft; W3C WoT TD/Architecture 1.1** — [§18](#18-step-14--sapient-v2-oms-30-w3cogc-sosa-ssn-and-web-of-things-observation-action-time-and-representation) | Ontology Systems/Sensors and deployment, proposed draft asset/OMS alignment; **Thing Model/Thing Description, affordances, Intermediary/Digital Twin proxy and cached report context**. | A Working Draft = published API normative rule; description/proxy ID = physical serial; cached measurement = fresh source reading or completed physical action. |

**What is actually covered:** original, targeted excerpts and qualified companion evidence in four donor iterations—not a complete audit of **182 donor artifacts** or a vote across standards. Source-authored requirements and aliases do not become our DIM axioms merely by being useful. The actual **BSI/protobuf registration tag 9/10 and timestamp source conflicts**, **2026 SSN draft status and direct-page access ceiling**, **full IEEE 1451.0-2024 normative access ceiling**, and **Open Group SOSA distinct editions** remain explicit unresolved evidence constraints. Donors were permitted to add **management/representation, mediated visibility, transducer-channel, observing/time, action and proxy** distinctions beyond the original five candidate headings. No late donor was dismissed for failing to supply a complete chassis.

### 19.5 Bounded inclusion, exclusion and falsification rules for the draft

The following constrain **future research interpretation**, not final DIM class definitions or mandatory Profile attributes.

1. **State the focus and evidence standing.** Distinguish product or reference arrangement from a real individually identified physical unit and a source-identified station. **No real System instance is inferred from a manufacturer PDF, mock serial, publisher code example or API placeholder.**
2. **State inclusion by original evidence and relevance.** Include source-described sensing hardware, processor/logger, functional/communication/power equipment, software/firmware, control, quality/condition, maintenance and provenance as **linked roles and descriptions when relevant to this bounded sensing focus**. Do not require any such facet merely because a standards catalog or UI tab lists it.
3. **Do not collapse relations.** Physical constituent, separately connected, hosted/deployed, implements/performs, observes, computes/averages, published/reported/proxied by, managed by and served in a role during an interval are **different questions**. No mandatory DIM edge names or cardinalities are adopted here.
4. **Distinguish identity domains.** Manufacturer model, actual physical serial, platform/station ID, SAPIENT node UUID, IEEE transducer/channel ID, SDI-12 address, CSAPI resource ID, Thing/description/proxy identifier and information-record ID are **not self-evidently the same individual**. The provider/source of a value is not necessarily the observer.
5. **Separate capability, actual configuration, activity and report.** A specification declares what can happen. An installed setting/condition requires operating evidence. A sent Task, accepted Task, completed outcome and sensor Observation are different source propositions, as are environmental record QA and hardware health.
6. **Treat missing history and time honestly.** Bennett's two selected averages are bounded historical evidence, and their nominal 15-minute intervals/probe attributions are project derived. Source data do **not** identify exact probe replacement minute, independently measured probe coordinates, true resultTime or complete running system configuration.
7. **Keep model, Profile and client work separate.** The DIM describes meanings and relations without choosing a JSON encoding, CSAPI System vs DataStream owner, SensorML class for every constituent, W3C draft namespace or Glaux UI layout. Later Profile and implementation work must demonstrate actual meaning preservation. **A successful one-case backend readback does not prove cross-vendor interoperability.**

**First-draft primary-subject exclusions:** generic enterprise IT, whole industrial plants unrelated to sensing, a complete mission/asset-management enterprise, actuator-only systems, entirely virtual Things, standalone software or human survey campaigns, and all possible security/maintenance infrastructures **are not automatically equal primary focal Systems in this scoped draft**. Their **real relationships to sensing equipment** still deserve representation where the source supports them. These exclusions are bounded **release-scope choices**, not claims those domains are unimportant or impossible or that OMS/SSN human/software observers are invalid.

**Overturn or restrict D if:** (a) a fielded source demands another referent and D cannot preserve it without fabricated hardware; (b) a simpler donor-founded structure accurately fits all four cases with fewer unsupported assumptions; (c) the integrated model creates circular System identities or converts a documented reference pairing into a fielded station; (d) the semantics are so generic that independent producer/observer/host/equipment interpretation is impossible; or (e) a source→DIM→Profile→client test demonstrably loses critical original configuration, observation provenance/time or hardware-state meaning. **Failure is grounds to revise D, not to relabel a source to fit it.**

**Naming guard:** retain provisional **system and sensor and equipment** language. This working frame is **not** a renamed “managed physical sensing system,” the incumbent five F-01–F-05 slots, a power-first taxonomy, a copy of SOSA's defense Sensor class, or a fixed UI category list.

### 19.6 Gate C outcome and accountability

| Gate C question | Verification at Step 15 | Decision |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Are A, B, C and donor-emergent D evaluated rather than treated as synonymous? | §19.2 identifies the strengths, specific four-case failure modes and statuses of each. | **MET — bounded working D selected, A/B/C retained as distinct viewpoints.** |
| Can the working subject be described across the four evidence cases without false units or invented fields? | §19.3 states the source-backed focus for each and expressly **NOT TESTED** fielded scopes for the three reference cases. | **MET for documentary/historical source scope, NOT TESTED for full operational conformance.** |
| Did donors have a fair opportunity to challenge previous concepts? | §19.4 follows actual original source editions and contributions, including newly important roles absent from F-01–F-05; it records source access/version limitations. | **MET for the targeted reviews, NOT a completed 182-source evaluation.** |
| Are naming, scope, relationship boundaries and exclusions sufficient to begin a connected DIM draft? | §19.5 defines source focal scope, relation/identity distinctions, bounded exclusions and explicit reversal conditions. | **MET for commencing Phase D only.** |
| Is the integrated DIM, CSAPI Profile, shared Glaux interpreter or whole-operating-system dataset finished? | No. The 52°North time/QA stream issue and several authentic-data gaps remain. | **NOT TESTED / OUTSIDE GATE C.** |

**GATE C DECISION: REACHED WITH A BOUNDED WORKING SUBJECT AND EXPLICIT LIMITATIONS, 2 NOVEMBER 2026.** The evidence supports drafting a **source-bounded, equipment-realized sensing System focus with explicit relationships**, but not adopting a final root class or assuming A/B/C are mandatory modules. The working approach can and **must be falsified** in later steps. The actual CSAPI **station-led versus probe-led DataStream** relationship, IEEE normative details, draft SSN ontology migration, metadata/firmware evidence and full operating-unit provenance remain **OPEN**.

**Step 15 changes:** this cumulative workbook section and the existing execution index/tracker, plus a short **Phase C PITON v2.2** for recovery. Original donor/source reports and frozen case sheets, earlier PITONs, preexisting candidate DIM/Profile, application branch, Cloudflare deployment and live Oracle/52°North server are **unchanged**.

**Historical Gate C handoff after Step 15 (Step 16 now completed in [§20](#20-step-16--individual-identity-kind-source-record-and-digital-description)):** begin Phase D with **individual System versus product/kind versus source record versus digital description/proxy and identifier scope**. Ground the *first actual draft definitions/relations* in **at least two distinct reference/history cases**, preserve unverified identities, and leave component/channel, observing/time, commands and integrated diagram work to their later numbered steps. **Do not start Step 16 as part of this Step 15 turn.**

## 20. Step 16 — Individual identity, kind, source record and digital description

**Status: Step 16 COMPLETE, 2 November 2026. Phase D initiated; Gate D NOT YET REACHED.** The actual [candidate DIM now contains a bounded Step 16 identity section](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-16--described-subject-individual-kind-evidence-and-digital-representation). This workbook entry records the evidence and a small conceptual falsification check, **not** a second new model, a CSAPI Profile update, a full integrated diagram or an assertion that the old F-01–F-05 design was already adopted. Gate C's [working focal subject and evidence restrictions](#19-step-15--phase-c-comparison-bounded-working-subject-and-gate-c) continue to control what can honestly be said.

### 20.1 In ordinary language — why this is a real modeling distinction

**A real piece of equipment is not its catalogue name, and neither is its digital record.** Bennett's Point offers a clear example: **EE181** names a product kind, two different **serial numbers** identify two physical probes, **acebpmet** identifies the *continuing monitoring station*, and the source NOAA **ATemp** rows identify particular historical station-temperature reports but **do not name the probes**. Our probe attribution uses separate dated equipment-use records and is therefore **DERIVED**, not an original source field. A digital API can show a representation of each subject, but its local resource identifier is **not automatically the corresponding physical serial or station code**. [Bennett original case and qualifying sources (§12)](#12-step-08--bennetts-point-actual-station-time-scoped-probes-and-historical-temperature-reports); [pinned selected source record](../Candidate_Specification/examples/bennetts-point-ee181.json).

**Dragino demonstrates the negative case:** we know a manufacturer-specified **S31-LB node and external SHT31 probe** and a decoder that emits the **Node_type** label, but we have **no authenticated real S31-LB physical unit or EUI**. Neither a product kind nor a decoder field establishes an actual fielded individual. **The same is true of AirGradient's published placeholder serial/0.0 coordinates and Campbell's example loggerID or ClimaVue SN-yyyyyyyy**. Thus a useful DIM must describe reusable designs and connected reference arrangements **without manufacturing station/unit objects to fill a UI**. [Dragino source sheet](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Dragino_S31_LB.md); [AirGradient source sheet](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/AirGradient_Open_Air_Max.md); [Campbell source sheet](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Campbell_CR1000X_ClimaVue50.md).

### 20.2 Candidate identity and representation meanings — linked to the actual DIM draft

| Source-driven question | Initial **candidate meaning** now in DIM | Its evidence/identity guard |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **What are we describing?** | One **source-declared focal subject**, which may be a real individual sensing System/equipment unit or station, **or** a **manufacturer reference product/arrangement** at design level. The focus does not mandate a new superclass. | A reference design is **not** automatically a real individual; one case can mention several distinct subjects. |
| **What kind of equipment?** | **Product kind/model description** or source-described reference role; type and physical individual have different referents. A reference **pairing** need not be a single manufactured model. | Same model ≠ same physical unit. Original model label/suffix/version matters, but does not prove installed hardware/options. |
| **Which real individual?** | **Source-attested individual equipment unit or continuing station/System** with identity within the native issuing authority/context. Its continuity and its components' identities are separable. | **EE181 serial 21281600077814** has two 2024-or-earlier recorded service periods; it is still one identified unit, not two individuals because time appears twice. Station acebpmet continues when a different EE181 takes its role. |
| **What is the evidential source?** | **Original source record/assertion** with publisher, native key, edition, reported time/scope and evidence standing; a source report may describe a product or an individual. | A NOAA archive row has StationCode/DatetimeStamp/ATemp/flag, **not a probe serial**; the **project's temporal join is a derived relationship**. |
| **Whose digital identity are we seeing?** | **Digital description/resource** about a subject and a possibly different **network intermediary/reporter/proxy**, whose identifier may refer to that digital participant or representation. | SAPIENT sender UUID, ISA visible/opaque Component ID, AAS Shell ID, WoT proxy/TD ID and local CSAPI resource IDs **cannot be merged with hardware serials without evidence**. |
| **When can identifiers count as referring to one subject?** | Evidence-backed **identifier assertion** specifies referent, issuer, scheme/context, original value and scope/time/edition where known; optional alias or represents relationship needs original support. | Same text, matching a model name, network address, local URL or a repeated field label **does not prove alias or real-world sameness**. No one-to-one ID rule or mandatory serial requirement was adopted. |

These are **candidate meaning distinctions**, not six imposed DIM classes, universal relationship names, JSON fields or a normative ontology. The [direct Step 16 definitions, relation propositions and four-case tests](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-16--described-subject-individual-kind-evidence-and-digital-representation) are the model deliverable. Earlier candidate [components/channels, observed subject and settings](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#components-and-channels) remain **preserved legacy research**, not revalidated as part of this step.

### 20.3 Explicit source-to-candidate tests and counterexamples

| Test | Original case evidence | What the candidate can say **without inventing anything** | Assessment |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **One model, two physical units** | Bennett's Point EE181 **222016000109CE** and **21281600077814**; original dated operator instrument history. | Two **individually identified pieces of equipment** sharing model EE181, associated with **one identified continuing station acebpmet**. | **SOURCE_GROUNDED CONCEPTUAL FIT**, not a verified physical-identity resolution service. |
| **One individual, multiple historical use periods** | EE181 **21281600077814** served at Bennett twice, before and again after the 16 April 2024 replacement. | One physical individual with two described service periods; the source's end-day precision **does not invent an exact replacement instant**. | **SOURCE_GROUNDED CONCEPTUAL FIT**, time-scoped association semantics deferred to Step 22. |
| **Model and design without a real individual** | Dragino S31-LB technical manual/decoder; AirGradient product/hardware and separate cloud examples. | Detailed reusable product/reference description; **individual identity UNKNOWN** and placeholder example IDs explicitly non-identifying. | **SOURCE_GROUNDED CONCEPTUAL FIT**, real-unit realization **NOT TESTED**. |
| **Two source-described products, no fabricated combined station** | Campbell first-generation ClimaVue 50 and CR1000X reference wiring; no acquired serial/installed program; separate published MQTT logger-status example. | A **documented reference arrangement** involving two distinct product kinds; no real paired station asserted. | **SOURCE_GROUNDED CONCEPTUAL FIT**, installed pairing **NOT TESTED**. |
| **Source measurement count versus API representation count** | Bennett two authentic selected NOAA rows; previous live 52°North example has two scalar and two contextual representations. | One source row remains one *original source result* even if multiple API descriptions/resources carry it. Source-probe serial attribution remains **DERIVED**. | **SOURCE_GROUNDED NON-EQUIVALENCE**; final API structure/DataStream ownership **OPEN**. |
| **Digital participant is not the physical equipment** | AAS v3.2 separately identifies asset and Shell; WoT Architecture proxies may use distinct IDs; SAPIENT Node is message participant. | Identify **represented subject** and **reporting/description role** separately; record missing physical identity links as **UNKNOWN**. | **DONOR-SUPPORTED DISTINCTION**, no verified physical-to-digital join across our four vendors. |

**Negative checks:** No data-sheet-only individual, no literal **SN-yyyyyyyy** instance, no AirGradient **0/0** station, no asserted physical equivalence for a **SAPIENT node_id**, no doubled Bennett probe from two service intervals, no original NOAA serial field invented, and no four source measurements inferred from four server resources. This is a **reasoning/documentary check only**, not a compiler, runtime, ontology consistency engine, source-to-API transformation, or Glaux test.

### 20.4 Relevant donor authority and remaining decisions

- **AAS v3.2**: asset/type/instance and distinct digital Shell/representation; **OPC UA Devices 1.05.0**: manufacturer Model versus structural DeviceType and source-scoped identity; [§15](#15-step-11--original-aas-opc-ua-devices-and-pa-dim-donor-native-whole-system-questions). Neither supplies an automatic one-to-one identity equivalence across systems.
- **SensorML 3.0**: general/specific process description, typeOf and real physical/process subject semantics; **OMS 3.0**: individually identifiable Observer versus reusable procedure and physical/organizational Host; [§17](#17-step-13--ieee-1451-transducerchannel-evidence-and-sensorml-30-processsystem-semantics), [§18](#18-step-14--sapient-v2-oms-30-w3cogc-sosa-ssn-and-web-of-things-observation-action-time-and-representation). **A sensor process and its catalog description are not the same as a real executed observation.**
- **SAPIENT v2 / ISA 6.0 / WoT 1.1**: network participant/proxy/representation and possible mediation separated from source physical performer; [§16](#16-step-12--isa-60-and-the-open-group-sosa-physical-functional-visible-and-managed-relationships), [§18](#18-step-14--sapient-v2-oms-30-w3cogc-sosa-ssn-and-web-of-things-observation-action-time-and-representation). Published 2026 SSN Working Draft is not a new Recommendation and the SAPIENT timestamp/tag discrepancies remain OPEN.
- **CSAPI Part 1 and the older candidate Profile**: the old [profile unit/kind/Deployment mapping](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#product-model-or-individual-unit) is a useful **earlier hypothesis, not adopted or revalidated in Step 16**. Digital API/resource identity and physical source identity cannot be equated merely by a name or a URL. **Profile mapping work belongs to Steps 25–31**.

**OPEN:** general identifier-scheme rules and cross-provider alias verification; whether one service resource describes a station, probe, interface participant or product kind; actual installed vendor serials/configuration for Dragino, AirGradient and Campbell; full temporal role/Deployment semantics and particular probe location; channels and physical constituents; observation producer/reporting/stream ownership; current status and actions; source-time/derived-time authority; and normative mapping in a future CSAPI Profile. **All four case evidence ceilings from §19 remain in force.** No completeness claims for 182 donors, complete individual operation, a cross-system DIM/client test or a final overall domain name.

### 20.5 Step 16 exit and next single iteration

**Completed:** an actual candidate DIM section with **meaningful subject/identity/kind/source/digital-representation distinctions**, evidence-qualified relationship propositions and a four-case falsification screen; earlier candidate sections retained without silent adoption. This workbook contains the verification/checkpoint. **No original source/controlled corpus edit, CSAPI Profile SHALL, channel/part ontology, cross-source UUID mapping, Glaux code, Cloudflare deployment or live CSAPI server mutation was performed.** **Gate D remains OPEN** until Steps 16–24 are integrated and challenged.

**Historical handoff after Step 16 (Step 17 completed in [§21](#21-step-17--physical-constituents-connected-equipment-functions-and-hosting)):** develop the physical **constituent versus independently connected equipment, aggregate/whole, functional realization and host/associated-equipment** relationship distinctions. Use concrete **Dragino external cable**, **AirGradient physical module** and **Campbell separate logger** counterexamples. Keep source design versus installed identities distinct, and **do not** advance to Step 18 channels, update the CSAPI Profile or implement a shared client as part of Step 17.


## 21. Step 17 — Physical constituents, connected equipment, functions and hosting

**Status: Step 17 COMPLETE, 2 November 2026. Gate D OPEN; Step 18 NEXT.** The [actual candidate DIM — Step 17 physical/functional relationship section](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-17--physical-constituent-external-connection-functional-realization-and-hosting) is the **single model deliverable**. This workbook entry documents the positive and negative evidence checks. We used source-native equipment arrangements from [§§9–12](#9-step-05--dragino-s31-lb-source-native-reference-system), plus the independently reviewed **OPC UA Devices, AAS, SensorML, The Open Group SOSA, ISA and OMS** donor distinctions in §§15–18. These materials are not combined into a new universal mandatory relation ontology.

### 21.1 The physical distinction in plain language

**A component is not defined by how many data values it reports. A connection is not the same as physical containment. A functional role is not necessarily one physical item. A station is not merely a permanently assembled product.**

The original examples are deliberately different: **Dragino** sells a radio/node product with an **external three-metre SHT31 probe**; **AirGradient** describes a manufactured enclosure and main circuit board with **two directly attached PMS5003 sensors and several plug-in sensing boards**; **Campbell** documents **two separate products** joined over **SDI-12**; and **Bennett's Point** records a continuing *operational station* with **two different serialized EE181 probes used during different periods**. If we give all those connections one unqualified “has part” arrow, we erase source meaning or claim a fielded installation that was never observed.

The physical and functional distinctions may **coexist** rather than compete. AirGradient's **SHT4x module** is a documented physical product constituent, communicates through I²C, and serves a measurement role; those are not the same proposition. Dragino's probe can be **supplied as part of the reference product** and **external to the node's housing**. Campbell's data logger can **acquire and process the weather sensor's outputs** without the ClimaVue being manufactured inside the logger. Such statements require the original source and its design-versus-individual standing.

### 21.2 Relationship questions now recorded in the candidate DIM

| Candidate physical/System relationship | Source evidence condition and example | Non-equivalence we explicitly reject |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Physical constituent of a documented assembly** | Identified *manufactured physical build*: AirGradient **main PCB, plug-in modules, PM1/PM2 PMS5003 devices** in the gas-equipped reference design. [Pinned original hardware README](https://github.com/Open-Air-Foundation/pcb-openair-max-main/blob/89aacf5013fc0ffe4b988a2e3c5a227bce3c4983/README.md). | “Is an output,” “uses same interface,” “is physically inside the housing” and “was a real installed serial-numbered unit” are not automatic consequences of one another. |
| **Externally situated but supplied in a manufacturer reference** | Manufacturer documents **S31-LB radio node with one three-metre external SHT31**. [Original §1.8](https://wiki.dragino.com/docs/LoRaWAN-End-Node/temperature-humidity-sensors/s31-lb/). | The sensor is **not the S31B internally mounted variant**, but external does not prove separately managed or absent from the manufacturer's described equipment package. |
| **Separate equipment connected by physical/data interface** | **Campbell CR1000X and first-generation ClimaVue 50** are independent described products, physically wired via **SDI-12 signal, 12 V, ground/shield** in the manufacturer's reference setup. [Campbell original wiring](https://help.campbellsci.com/climavue50/climavue50/installation/wiring.htm). | A source-described wire does **not** prove a ClimaVue is an internal CR1000X part, one actual installed station, or a successful command/data exchange. |
| **Functional implementation/processing role** | **ClimaVue weather sensing** versus **CR1000X acquisition/program/aggregation**; AirGradient sensing modules versus main-board reporting; Dragino cabled probe versus radio node. [Source-native §11](#11-step-07--campbell-cr1000x-and-first-generation-climavue-50-source-native-arrangement). | A function is not another physical chip/part or independently identified instrument solely because it appears in a process hierarchy. **No output/channel ontology or observation ownership decided in this step.** |
| **Host, station membership, mounting and historical use** | Actual historical **Bennett's Point acebpmet** station, **CR1000X logger 13916**, **TX321 transmitter 2335**, two time-scoped **EE181 probe serials**. [Operator source and history §12](#12-step-08--bennetts-point-actual-station-time-scoped-probes-and-historical-temperature-reports). | One station ID ≠ all component serials, permanent membership, known probe-specific coordinates or exact 16 April 2024 changeover. **Temporal relationship design belongs to Step 22.** |
| **Support, communication or management** | AirGradient solar/battery/CE electronics can be part of a design; LoRaWAN gateway, external carrier, data center and station archives may be outside the sensing assembly. ISA CIC and Open Group SOSA System Manager distinguish exposed/managed participants. | Shared power, network membership, management/visibility or remote data publishing is **not automatically** physical constituent membership. |

**Source authority matters:** the AirGradient two-PMS5003 construction is a **SOURCE_FACT at design scope**, not inspection of two real instruments. Campbell's published wiring is **SOURCE_FACT at reference scope**, not a verified installed connection. Bennett's serialized probes and station are **actual historical operator records**, but source-day precision does not give exact installation instants or full deployed component inventories. Missing values remain **UNKNOWN**.

### 21.3 Source-donor checks rather than a copied standard taxonomy

The [original OPC UA Devices v1.05.0 §5.5](https://reference.opcfoundation.org/specs/OPC-10000-100/5.5) explicitly defines **ConnectsTo** as a **symmetric NonHierarchical network-topology relation**; this is independent of the Device Model's physical/software component structure and **Device Integration Host**. [AAS Part 1 v3.2](https://industrialdigitaltwin.io/aas-specifications/IDTA-01001/v3.2/spec-metamodel/submodel-elements.html) distinguishes an Entity in a composite asset representation from its management granularity, not from an externally authenticated physical serial. [SensorML 3.0 §§7.1–7.2, 8.4–8.6](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-000/23-000.html) can represent **physical and computational processes and their connections**, but that process graph cannot be assumed to be the same as the equipment's physical assembly or ownership tree.

The **Open Group SOSA** Sensor–Host Platform and functional Module–implementation relationships, **ISA** visible/opaque/virtual interface-member relationships, and **OMS** Observer–Host–Deployment concepts supply further **different meanings of hosting, participation and management**. See source-specific qualified research [§16](#16-step-12--isa-60-and-the-open-group-sosa-physical-functional-visible-and-managed-relationships) and [§18](#18-step-14--sapient-v2-oms-30-w3cogc-sosa-ssn-and-web-of-things-observation-action-time-and-representation). They **do not jointly publish a single mandatory DIM “part-of” rule**. A source declaring visibility, status or hosting cannot be promoted to physical membership by analogy.

### 21.4 Negative checks and the Step 17 decision boundary

- **Dragino:** reject “the external SHT31 is inside the S31-LB radio housing” and “two reported properties imply two separately installed probes”; retain **manufacturer reference package/external cabling** without assigning a verified field-unit serial.
- **AirGradient:** reject “every PM value is a distinct physical sensor” and “the directly connected PMS5003s are independent daughterboards”; preserve the **two direct PM device sockets versus plug-in sensor boards** and the distinction between physical product and cloud/service.
- **Campbell:** reject “ClimaVue is an intrinsic logger component” and “the 2019 example program and 2026 MQTT logger status establish a joined deployed station”; preserve **two physical product roles connected at design level**.
- **Bennett:** reject “both EE181 serials were permanently part of the station at once,” “station position is per-probe geometry,” and “a dated equipment change gives an exact changeover time”; keep **one continuing station and actual serial-specific historical use evidence**, with interval semantics still pending.

**What Step 17 establishes:** an evidence-qualified **distinction among physical assembly constituents, external supplied/probe arrangements, independent connected equipment, functional realization, hosting/station membership and support/management**. It is an **implementation-independent candidate model distinction**, not finalized UML relation types, required multiplicities, CSAPI resource bindings, executed conformance tests or successful Glaux UI interpretation. The actual [candidate DIM section](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-17--physical-constituent-external-connection-functional-realization-and-hosting) contains the more formal working text and four-case table. Earlier component/channel text remains preserved as **prior draft**, not silently approved by this Step 17.

**Historical handoff after Step 17 (Step 18 now complete in [§22](#22-step-18--interfaces-channels-outputs-fields-properties-and-reporting-provenance)):** examine **physical sensing implementation, interface, addressed channel, reporting output and field, observed property and reporting/producer** using Dragino, AirGradient and Campbell counterexamples. Do not perform observation/time/quality Step 19 or a CSAPI Profile/client/server implementation as part of Step 18. **Gate D remains OPEN** until the integrated Step 24 falsification check.


## 22. Step 18 — Interfaces, channels, outputs, fields, properties and reporting provenance

**Status: Step 18 COMPLETE, 2 November 2026. Phase D Gate D OPEN; Step 19 NEXT.** The primary output is the [new Step 18 section of the actual candidate DIM](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-18--interfaces-addressable-channels-reported-outputs-fields-properties-and-reporters), added after the [Step 16 identity draft](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-16--described-subject-individual-kind-evidence-and-digital-representation) and [Step 17 physical/functional relations](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-17--physical-constituent-external-connection-functional-realization-and-hosting). This workbook section records the checked source-to-meaning distinctions and negative cases. **The result is documentary/conceptual, not executed software or a completed standard Profile.** Earlier candidate components/channel text is preserved rather than silently approved.

### 22.1 The result in ordinary language

The underlying physical sensing equipment, an interface it uses, a logical address in that interface, a published response, an individual result field, and the property that field describes are **six different questions**. A seventh question is **who actually reported or processed the value**, which may not be the physical sensor itself. The original four cases prove that a model which collapses these questions into a generic “sensor child” would miscount devices, lose measurement meaning or claim an unfounded producer.

**Dragino:** One external SHT31 probe supplies **two environmental properties** while the S31-LB radio node packages those values with other node metadata in a **FPort-2** message. FPort-3 history and FPort-5 device status are different report forms. **LoRaWAN FPort number is not an independently addressable IEEE transducer channel**, and battery voltage in a message is not another outside-air sensor. [Source-native §9](#9-step-05--dragino-s31-lb-source-native-reference-system).

**AirGradient:** Two physical PMS5003 particulate sensing units attach to **PM1 and PM2 board connectors**. Separately, reported **pm01, pm25, pm10** refer to measured particulate mass fractions; the **PM1 socket label is not the PM1.0 property**, and a PM2 socket is not PM2.5. Distinct Wi-Fi, cellular and cloud examples have different schemas and reporting provenance. The corpus does **not** establish which PM physical unit supplied a specific example **pm25** value or whether the firmware combined them. [Pinned physical design](https://github.com/Open-Air-Foundation/pcb-openair-max-main/blob/89aacf5013fc0ffe4b988a2e3c5a227bce3c4983/README.md); [pinned firmware example](https://github.com/Open-Air-Foundation/firmware-openair-max/blob/84e479a415d81151e7a81aea9a2f7449a15e98cc/README.md); [source-native §10](#10-step-06--airgradient-open-air-max-source-native-reference-system).

**Campbell:** One integrated ClimaVue 50 has one relevant SDI-12 device-addressing context and **14 named/ordered quantities within its aR7! response**; those 14 positions are not fourteen physical units or independently addressed IEEE channels. The separate CR1000X may acquire or transform them and has its own publisher-documented **logger-only Status** field list. **ClimaVue ambient temperature, humidity-sensor temperature and logger PanelTemp are different properties/subjects despite shared °C**. No actual weather response or matching installed logger program has been acquired. [Sensor table 8-1 in 08/2024 original](https://s.campbellsci.com/documents/us/manuals/climavue50.pdf); [source-native §11](#11-step-07--campbell-cr1000x-and-first-generation-climavue-50-source-native-arrangement).

**Bennett's Point:** The reserve documents EE181 sensing units, CR1000X averaging and station/GOES/CDMO reporting as distinct roles. The selected real NOAA **ATemp** fields are **15-minute station-temperature summaries**, not source rows containing probe serials or verified per-sample sensor data. Their probe associations come from a separate equipment-use history as **DERIVED** attribution, and the original station has **two** selected measurements, even though the experimental server exposes **four** Observation representations. [Source-native §12](#12-step-08--bennetts-point-actual-station-time-scoped-probes-and-historical-temperature-reports).

### 22.2 One comparison table with exact meanings and limits

| Question | Dragino S31-LB | AirGradient Open Air Max | Campbell ClimaVue + CR1000X | Bennett's Point |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Physical sensing implementation** | One external SHT31 | Two PMS5003s plus other physical sensing modules | One integrated ClimaVue, separate CR1000X logger | Distinct serial-numbered EE181 units in dated service |
| **Physical or logical access** | Source-specified cabled probe; S31-LB LoRaWAN message interface | UART PM1/PM2 connectors; I²C/UART module interfaces; Wi-Fi/cellular reports | SDI-12 bus/interface at sensor; separate logger status API | Station source archive; sensor/logger native details incomplete |
| **Channel/address evidence** | No native SHT31/IEEE addressed channel acquired; LoRaWAN FPort is a report discriminator | PM1/PM2 are physical connector paths, not proven IEEE addressed channels | SDI-12 **sensor-device** address default **0**, not active; response positions are not separate bus addresses | No original per-probe address/channel in selected CSV |
| **Original output/schema** | FPort-2 current/formatted, FPort-3 history, FPort-5 status | Wi-Fi 17-key annotated sample; cellular **1 + 3×17 = 52** integers; separate cloud forms | **aR7!** 14-value sensor response; **logger-only** Status example has 31 fields | Published NOAA station ATemp plus parameter-specific F_ATemp; 15-minute summary |
| **Meaning of a selected field** | TempC_SHT31 in °C vs Hum_SHT31 in % RH; BatV is node voltage | pm25 is PM2.5 quantity, not physical PM2 board; tvocRaw/noxRaw and electrode volts are raw; measure4 unresolved | Air temp °C vs humidity-sensor temp °C vs logger PanelTemp °C; ClimaVue RH source fraction | ATemp is ambient air-temperature 15-minute mean; F_ATemp is source QA, not probe health |
| **Who reports/mediates?** | S31-LB node reports probe-originating values | Main-board firmware reports; cloud forms separately describe API values | ClimaVue sensor responds; separate CR1000X can acquire/transform; logger Status is separate | Physical probe senses, CR1000X makes station summary, GOES/CDMO publishes |
| **Evidence ceiling** | Manufacturer design and decoded example, no real unit | Design and unjoined publisher examples, no field-level original PM1/PM2 provenance | Documented reference pairing and unrelated examples, no installed pair | Historical actual source rows and equipment history, inferred per-row probe association |

**Critical negative tests, read literally:** (1) **physical probe count ≠ output field count**; (2) physical **PM1/PM2 socket labels ≠ PM1.0/PM2.5 mass-fraction fields**; (3) **device address ≠ response-field position ≠ IEEE transducer channel**; (4) one component's **temperature ≠ environmental air temperature** merely because units match; (5) **reporting or publishing System ≠ actual sensing element** without source-verified attribution; (6) one manufacturer's device/cellular/cloud example cannot be silently joined into one observed run; (7) a **CSAPI DataStream is not automatically the same thing as any native field, socket, FPort or transducer**. These are **REJECTED_EQUIVALENCE** findings for later source-fidelity tests.

### 22.3 Primary donor authority without invented cross-standard aliases

[SensorML 3.0, OGC 23-000 §§7.1–7.2 and 8.2.9](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-000/23-000.html) distinguishes **physical or computational process** from **inputs, outputs, parameters, and their connected process descriptions**, and can use typed SWE Common quantities/records. The [official IEEE 1451.0-2024 publisher scope](https://standards.ieee.org/ieee/1451.0/11001/) and [2024-linked Playground TransducerChannel TEDS](https://iml.ubi.pt/playground/docs/ieee1451/teds/transducer-channel-teds/) separately establish a rich **addressable channel-level metadata** precedent. **We did not examine complete licensed IEEE 2024 field/cardinality SHALLs**, and **no case source claims IEEE 1451 conformance**. A field called “channel” or “PM2” is not, simply by vocabulary, an IEEE TransducerChannel.

The [approved OGC API – Connected Systems Part 2 v1.0, §§9.1–9.2](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) **specifies that a DataStream is produced by one output of an observation System**, that observations in the stream are produced by the **same System**, and that they share the **same observed properties and result schema**. Thus a future Profile author **cannot omit producing-System semantics or arbitrarily merge unrelated result schemas**. But the existence of that standard rule alone **does not prove which physical, functional or station System is the correct producer for a Bennett summary**, nor does it require one stream for every physical transducer/field. That actual mapping was tested differently in the prior offline vs live experiment, and remains a **Phase E decision**.

[OMS 3.0 §§8.2 and 8.4–8.8](../Primary_Sources/OGC_20-082r4_OMS_3.0.0.md) distinguishes **Observer, ObservingProcedure, observed feature/property, result and reporter/Host context**. Actual observer/procedure/time/quality is explicitly deferred to **Step 19**; this Step 18 uses the source conceptual distinctions without claiming a particular observed activity occurred in the three manufacturer design examples. ISA's mediated/opaque members, SAPIENT's Node sender, and WoT proxy/twin roles add **possible differing reporter identities**, not proof of actual producer equivalence. [Prior original-donor review §§15–18](#15-step-11--original-aas-opc-ua-devices-and-pa-dim-donor-native-whole-system-questions).

### 22.4 What the candidate DIM now says, and what it still does not

The [candidate DIM Step 18](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-18--interfaces-addressable-channels-reported-outputs-fields-properties-and-reporters) records **seven candidate meaning distinctions** and the source-evidence requirements for relating them: physical sensing implementation, interface, source-verified addressed channel, output/report form, field/position, represented property and reporting/processing participant. These **are not seven adopted classes or compulsory associations**. The source-qualified relationship propositions must not become encoding keys, UI tabs or one-per-field API resource obligations merely because they are named in a table.

**Open and unavailable:** exact physical-to-field provenance for AirGradient's two PM units, actual native-channel identity for the Dragino probe, applied Campbell SDI-12 address and paired response values, Bennett's actual per-probe bus addressing, independently evidenced Logger/Observer/Procedure role in each source observation, genuine resultTime, exact stream-ownership relation, and generic client behavior. Source examples are **not** real performed actions or calibrated observations. All manufacturer case claims stay at **design/example** scope; only Bennett provides narrow actual historical measurements. No completeness claim is made for **182 independently reviewed standards**.

**Completed in this iteration:** one bounded, source-qualified **candidate DIM Step 18 section** and this cumulative workbook verification; prior identity and physical relations were preserved, old candidate component/output wording remains **historical**, and no candidate CSAPI Profile, Glaux code, source fixture, server, Cloudflare deployment or original dataset was changed. **Gate D remains OPEN** pending Steps 19–24 and the integrated source-falsification check.

**Historical Step 18 handoff (Step 19 now completed in [§23](#23-step-19--observing-capabilities-performed-activity-and-result-provenance)):** use the source roles now distinguished to examine **supported observing method versus performed observation, actual Observer, featureOfInterest, observed result and time/quality semantics**, with Bennett's actual source mean as a counterexample. Preserve true **resultTime UNKNOWN**; do not preempt later configuration, control, time-scoped equipment or Profile/client work.


## 23. Step 19 — Observing capabilities, performed activity and result provenance

**Status: Step 19 COMPLETE, 2 November 2026. Step 20 NEXT. Gate D OPEN.** The actual [Candidate DIM Step 19](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-19--observing-capability-performed-observation-subject-result-time-and-quality) now defines the candidate meanings and relationships for **supported observing capability, reusable method, actual performed Observation, identifiable Observer, observed subject/property, result, phenomenonTime, resultTime, validTime, parameter-specific result quality, and sensing/processing/reporting provenance**. This workbook record verifies the source checks and leaves an explicit OMS conformance/data gap. This was **conceptual/documentary model development**, not a running sensor/CSAPI conformance experiment or new field data acquisition.

### 23.1 What Step 19 adds, in ordinary language

**An instrument's ability to measure temperature, a method for making a temperature measurement, and a particular temperature observation are different facts.** The [OGC Observations, Measurements and Samples (OMS) 3.0 original §§8.2, 8.4–8.6](https://docs.ogc.org/as/20-082r4/20-082r4.html) distinguishes an act of Observation using an ObservingProcedure from a description of a reusable method and the identifiable Observer that implements it. The property belongs to the **featureOfInterest**, not necessarily the hardware used for sensing. An Observer may be a physical sensor or a computational actor; the instrument that first senses the medium, the logger that produces an average, and the publisher of that average are **not necessarily the same source-identified participant**.

For the three **manufacturer-only reference** cases, source documents tell us what can be produced or what a response schema looks like. **They do not give an authenticated completed Observation from an identified installed device**. The original **Bennett's Point 2024 archive**, however, contains two **actual selected historical station-temperature summaries**, corroborated by operator equipment-use and station reporting descriptions. The source row is evidence of an Observation/result and can be carried through different API representations; **it is not a new independent physical act each time a server re-encodes it**.

### 23.2 Bennett's Point: source report versus derived association

| Source element and evidence standing | **15 April 2024 at 12:00 fixed EST** | **18 April 2024 at 12:00 fixed EST** |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Original source archive row | **NOAA CSV line 10130**, station **acebpmet** | **NOAA CSV line 10418**, station **acebpmet** |
| Original observed result | **ATemp 29.1 °C**, 15-minute air-temperature mean | **ATemp 27.3 °C**, 15-minute air-temperature mean |
| Original per-parameter QA | **F_ATemp = "<0> "**, passed **initial** checks, including one literal trailing space | **F_ATemp = "<0> "**, same limited interpretation |
| Original station timestamp | **04/15/2024 12:00**, fixed **EST UTC−05 year-round** | **04/18/2024 12:00**, fixed EST |
| Project-derived nominal phenomenonTime period | **16:45–17:00 UTC, 15 April**, preceding station stamp | **16:45–17:00 UTC, 18 April**, preceding station stamp |
| Separate source equipment role, joined by station/date/role (**DERIVED attribution**) | **EE181 serial 222016000109CE** in source-described service period | **EE181 serial 21281600077814** in later source-described service period |
| Exact physical source identity directly in original CSV? | **NO.** Serial appears only in separate operator history | **NO.** Serial appears only in separate operator history |
| Independently established native resultTime? | **UNKNOWN**. No actual availability instant in source | **UNKNOWN**. No actual availability instant in source |
| Individual probe geometry, actual 5-second inputs and executed full program? | **UNKNOWN** at per-row level | **UNKNOWN** at per-row level |

The [preserved two-row source evidence file](../Candidate_Specification/examples/bennetts-point-ee181.json) records the original field strings and the **31 October archive snapshot fingerprint**. We **did not re-download the original annual CSV** in Step 19. The source's **DatetimeStamp** does not itself contain a duration or a resultTime; original NOAA/ACE documentation says the readings are **15-minute averages** with the summary associated with the preceding interval. Our nominal **period and EST→UTC conversion are DERIVED**, not new native CSV fields or proof of each 5-second sample timestamp. The 16 April multi-instrument maintenance window is **not an exact EE181 swap timestamp**.

**Four different referents must survive:** (1) **environmental air** as the observed medium in the vicinity of the station, not the logger panel; (2) **air temperature** as the observable characteristic; (3) the **15-minute mean** as the original station-reported result, including its source QA; and (4) the **physical sensing/aggregation/publication chain**, with **serial attribution derived from separate dated equipment-use records**. The source does **not** identify an individual air parcel or provide a complete OMS Observer/ObservingProcedure instance for each result. The operator describes the EE181 sensing role, CR1000X **5-second input/15-minute aggregation**, GOES transfer and NOAA/CDMO publication, but not the exact per-row executed program or original result availability instant.

**Negative quality-control example retained:** archive CSV line **10322**, **17 April 2024 12:00 EST**, has **F_ATemp = "<-3> [GPR] (CSM)"**, rejected during a logger operating-system update; its numeric temperature, if present, **does not make it a third accepted observation**. The selected **"<0> "** initial-QA flag does **not** certify calibration, probe health or all other parameter fields; **F_Record** is a separate record-level value. The 29.1 versus 27.3 °C difference across two separate dates **cannot be attributed causally** to the probe change.

### 23.3 Four-case conceptual falsification

| Source case | What is actually supported | Shortcut that the DIM now rejects |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Dragino S31-LB** — [§9](#9-step-05--dragino-s31-lb-source-native-reference-system) | External SHT31 can report temperature/humidity with documented LoRaWAN sampling/report structure and illustrative decoder messages; no authenticated deployed unit. | Manufacturer examples are actual Observations with an identified physical Observer, measured source time, resultTime or current unit QA. |
| **AirGradient Open Air Max** — [§10](#10-step-06--airgradient-open-air-max-source-native-reference-system) | Multi-module sensor design and separate cellular/Wi-Fi/cloud example payloads with raw gas fields and different schema. | Different example message families are one verified measured run, physical sensor attribution is known for every field, or raw electrodes are calibrated air concentrations. |
| **Campbell ClimaVue 50 + CR1000X** — [§11](#11-step-07--campbell-cr1000x-and-first-generation-climavue-50-source-native-arrangement) | One weather instrument's 14 return positions and a distinct logger's programmable calculations/carry-forward possibilities; separate logger-only Status. | One schema slot or example program proves a completed environmental Observation from a fielded integrated station; **PanelTemp** is outside air; a returned value necessarily means a new sample. |
| **Bennett's Point** — [§12](#12-step-08--bennetts-point-actual-station-time-scoped-probes-and-historical-temperature-reports) | Two real source station air-temperature means; individual serials and station use periods are source facts but per-result association is derived; value and parameter-specific original QA preserved. | Four API representations mean four original source measurements; source CSV names the probe; original timestamp is native period and genuine resultTime; **F_ATemp flag = hardware health**. |

**Conceptual fit only:** these distinctions can accommodate the four source cases without inventing actual measurement acts for reference designs or falsely duplicating historical activity. **No observation-oriented DIM encoding, reasoner/test suite, generic Glaux renderer or third-party client was run**. The eventual source-to-DIM→Profile→client comparison must test whether a producer can communicate these distinctions consistently.

### 23.4 Original standards and a mandatory-data gap

**OMS 3.0, OGC 20-082r4**: The [original published specification](https://docs.ogc.org/as/20-082r4/20-082r4.html) **§8.2.2** defines the observation act; **§8.2.3** defines **phenomenonTime**, including **an interval for an average**; **§8.2.4** defines **resultTime** as when the result became available and requires **exactly one**; **§8.2.5** separately describes **validTime**; **§§8.2.6–.10** address feature/property/result/procedure/Observer; **§8.5.2 Note 5** says reusable procedure describes sensor type/method rather than an individual sensor; **§8.6.2** defines individual Observer. **OMS Abstract Observation Core §9.2.5** defines **resultQuality**, which complements generic procedure reliability. OMS **§8.2.16** requires an appropriate unit for measurable results. Requirements belong to their **original OMS requirements classes**, not to newly adopted DIM SHALLs.

**Explicit gap with real downstream consequences:** A fully conforming conceptual OMS Observation **requires resultTime**, whereas the original Bennett's Point two-row evidence does **not** provide an independently evidenced instant of result availability. **Do not supply the station's measurement-end timestamp, GOES transmit slot, the NOAA archive publication date or the current server time as though original resultTime were known.** The source-to-Profile mapping must resolve a truthful basis for any required resultTime **or record why that complete conformance claim cannot currently be made**. This is not repaired by a convenient missing-value filler. The actual datum remains **UNKNOWN**.

**SensorML 3.0, OGC 23-000**, [approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-000/23-000.html) describes **physical/computational processes, inputs/outputs, parameters and ProcessMethod**, with configured processes distinguishable from supported reference definitions. **W3C/OGC SOSA-SSN 2017 Recommendation**, [original](https://www.w3.org/TR/2017/REC-vocab-ssn-20171019/), keeps sensor, Observation, procedure, feature and temporal relations distinct. The **2026 SSN Working Draft** remains separately qualified and **The Open Group SOSA** is unrelated by acronym. [CSAPI Part 2](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) has a producing-System association for a DataStream, but **Step 19 does not select probe/logger/station producer ownership** for Bennett. This is a genuine choice for Phase E, not an automatic implication of OMS Observer.

### 23.5 Stop boundary and next authorized iteration

**What changed in this iteration:** one new [candidate DIM Step 19 observation/quality section](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-19--observing-capability-performed-observation-subject-result-time-and-quality) plus **this cumulative, source-linked case verification**; corresponding plan and README status were updated. Previous Step 16–18 work and earlier historical candidate text were preserved. Original frozen evidence, prior PITON, candidate CSAPI Profile, Glaux app, Oracle/52°North server and Cloudflare deployment were **not** changed. This step supplies **candidate meanings, not a new normative ontology**.

**Gate D remains OPEN**, pending the configuration/status, control/action, physical history and cross-cutting work of Steps 20–23 and the integrated falsification check at Step 24.

**Historical Step 19 handoff (Step 20 is now complete in [§24](#24-step-20--configuration-operating-mode-reported-status-and-condition)):** compare **supported choices/defaults** with **selected/effective configuration, observed state, diagnostics and modes**, using at least two real source arrangements and preserving the uncertain boundary between **Condition and Configuration** where appropriate. **Do not** begin Step 21 command/outcome, Step 22 Deployment/replacement, or any CSAPI Profile, client or server work in the same iteration.


## 24. Step 20 — Configuration, operating mode, reported status and condition

**Status: Step 20 COMPLETE, 2 November 2026. Step 21 NEXT; Phase D Gate D OPEN.** The [Candidate DIM Step 20 section](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-20--configuration-operating-state-and-condition) is the **primary model output**: separate candidate meanings for design support, source defaults, desired/offline configuration, reported effective settings, modes, operational states, diagnostics, equipment condition and Observation-result quality. It is not a new health taxonomy or a generic “power System” framework. This workbook entry records the case-specific checks, original donor pedigree and limits.

### 24.1 Ordinary-language lesson: what can it do, what was chosen, what did it report?

A product's specification can describe **available choices** and **factory/source defaults**, a management system may contain **desired settings**, and a real device could return an **applied setting** or **reported state** at a particular time. **Those are four different claims**, potentially from four different sources. A voltage or error counter is **diagnostic evidence** about a particular component; deciding it establishes “healthy” generally needs a source's actual rule, measurement scope and temporal validity. **An environmental observation's QA flag qualifies its result—not automatically the device's health.**

The source cases here are asymmetric. **Dragino, AirGradient and Campbell** each have substantial manufacturer-defined options, reporting formats and diagnostics, **but lack an authenticated fielded unit/report joined to those documentation passages**. **Bennett's Point** offers genuine 2024 history of deployed equipment, maintenance and station temperature result quality, **not a full effective-configuration/status feed**. A shared profile-aware client must be capable of showing “supports,” “default in this documentation,” “example report,” “historically recorded,” “last reported” and “unknown” distinctly, rather than presenting them all as “currently configured and healthy.”

### 24.2 Four-case evidence matrix — source facts versus unsupported inference

| Case | Supported choices and documented defaults (source and scope) | Status/diagnostic facts (source and scope) | Rejected inference |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Dragino S31-LB** — [sheet C, power/operations and controls](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Dragino_S31_LB.md#2-power-and-operating-behavior), [manual §§1.4, 2.1, 3.3](https://wiki.dragino.com/docs/LoRaWAN-End-Node/temperature-humidity-sensors/s31-lb/) | **Default uplink 20 minutes**, supported Deep Sleep/Working/IDLE, **AT+TDC** interval control and example **60000 ms**, alarm threshold syntax, optional 5-V output. No actual selected unit configuration. | FPort-5 *defined* status reply can contain firmware, region and **battery voltage** (scalar 2.885/2.889 V examples), and FPort-2 settings reply can contain threshold values. **No real original unit reply was obtained.** | **AT+TDC 60000 ms** = the documented **20-minute default**; support for modes means active mode was observed; **8,500 mAh** reference cell capacity means remaining battery percentage; an example voltage proves battery health or a successful command. |
| **AirGradient Open Air Max** — [sheet A, §2](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/AirGradient_Open_Air_Max.md#2-power-operation-and-configuration), [pinned firmware README](https://github.com/Open-Air-Foundation/firmware-openair-max/blob/84e479a415d81151e7a81aea9a2f7449a15e98cc/README.md) | Source README default **cellular** vs selectable Wi-Fi, APN/credentials/HTTP destination. **Separate firmware 0.7.0+ guide** describes Standard (USB or above 10 V), Power Saving (**9.5–9.9 V**) and Deep Power Saving (**below 9.5 V**) with different intervals. One-hour README configuration-check claims versus later **90-minute/daily** guide are **not a merged runtime contract**. | Firmware docs define LED patterns for **sensor initialization and network/post failures**, but no LEDs were observed. Wi-Fi/cellular voltage examples are **separate manufacturer illustrations**, not a real identified unit's synchronized state. | A source voltage example proves the **0.7.0+ power mode** of an observed unit, a documented LED pattern occurred, cellular cache and Wi-Fi transmit identically, or voltage yields state-of-charge/health without a source rule. |
| **Campbell CR1000X + ClimaVue 50** — [sheet D §1 and §5](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Campbell_CR1000X_ClimaVue50.md#5-complete-cr1000x-status-example-inventory), [publisher MQTT Status example](https://help.campbellsci.com/datalogger-api/mqtt/status.html) | ClimaVue factory-default **SDI-12 address 0**, documented scan recommendations and possible verification commands.
