The **2019 program**, **2024 sensor manual** and **2026 logger-only Status example** are separate source contexts, not a verified installed pairing. | Published **logger-only** illustration: **Battery = 13.2368 Vdc** supply, **LithiumBattery = 3.70477 V** internal backup, **PanelTemp = 25.91098 °C** logger panel, **SW12Volts(1–2) = true** Boolean terminal states, **WatchdogErrors = 0** with reset semantics, and an example **CompileResults** string. Additional fields lack verified detailed meanings. | One logger Battery voltage = battery % or ClimaVue sensor supply; **SW12Volts true** is numeric 12 V; **PanelTemp** is ambient air; zero watchdog count is lifetime fault-free; **“Compiled in PipelineMode”** means that the unobserved fielded Campbell pairing ran the cited program. |
| **Bennett's Point** — [operator 2024 meteorological metadata](https://cdmo.baruch.sc.edu/waf/YearlyFiles/ACE%20Basin/meteorological/metadata/acemet01-12.24m.pdf), [pinned original readings and history](../Candidate_Specification/examples/bennetts-point-ee181.json) | Operator describes an **actual 17 April 2024 logger operating-system update**, historical equipment use, and expected 5-second/15-minute averaging practice. It does **not** give an authenticated full active parameter/configuration export for the two selected historical temperature reports. | Source **F_ATemp = "<0> "** on 15 and 18 April selected air-temperature records means **passed initial parameter-level QA**, not equipment condition. Rejected **17 April F_ATemp = "<-3> [GPR] (CSM)"** reflects a failed temperature-parameter quality check during an OS-update period. | A maintenance date proves exact applied configuration at every time; station result QA = physical EE181 or logger health; historical as-of-2024 installed status = November 2026 current operation; logger update = proof the selected program and all options were active. |

**SOURCE_FACT (manufacturer reference scope)** supports documented choices/defaults and meaning of possible replies/diagnostics. **SOURCE_EXAMPLE** supports literal example values, not a verified individual or performed task. **SOURCE_FACT (operator historical scope)** supports Bennett's maintenance/use history and actual station-temperature reports, but **NOT** a full as-applied configuration history. **UNKNOWN** remains the effective setting, actual mode and device condition of unverified installed units. **REJECTED_EQUIVALENCE** excludes every rightmost-column shortcut; **no new tests or live equipment observations were performed**.

### 24.3 Donor-native semantic tests

| Original donor / locator | Distinction supported in its own terms | Boundary for candidate DIM |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **SensorML 3.0** — [§§8.2.9.3.2 and 8.8, Requirements 15 and 37–43](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-000/23-000.html) | **Configurable** processes declare options, allowed parameter values and Modes; **configured process descriptions** select/restrict settings. Modes may represent sets of settings. | Source's configured *description* does not prove observed physical current settings. Normative requirements apply to identified **SensorML requirements classes**, not unilaterally to our DIM/Profile. |
| **OPC UA Devices 1.05.0** — [§6.3 Online/Offline](https://reference.opcfoundation.org/specs/OPC-10000-100/6.3) | Separately modeled **offline engineering/desired values** and related **online device parameters**; online read may communicate with a physical device or return **Bad_NotConnected**. | “Offline” is not “expired” or “formerly installed”; “online” is not proof our vendor cases supplied that information. A transferred setting still needs actual source-specific evidence. |
| **OPC UA PA-DIM 1.02.0** — [§7.24 GeneralDeviceConditionSetType](https://reference.opcfoundation.org/specs/OPC-30081/7.24) | Source-local optional **InternalTemperature, ResidualLife, PowerOnDuration, OperationDuration and cycle count** diagnostics/condition quantities. ResidualLife denotes degradation normalized **1 good → 0 bad**, not battery SOC. | PA-DIM's example default numbers are **not historical instrument readbacks**. InternalTemperature is a manufacturer-defined *internal* location, not environmental air; resultQuality does not equal DeviceHealth. |
| **BSI SAPIENT v2 / immutable Dstl companion** — [registration.proto](https://github.com/dstl/SAPIENT-Proto-Files/blob/244a741c13bcee628f84944aef91e4a66854bd7d/bsi_flex_335_v2_0/registration.proto), [status_report.proto](https://github.com/dstl/SAPIENT-Proto-Files/blob/244a741c13bcee628f84944aef91e4a66854bd7d/bsi_flex_335_v2_0/status_report.proto) | **ModeDefinition**, permitted mode/task/report schemas and **StatusDefinition** in Registration differ from later **StatusReport.mode**, power level **0–100**, power status **OK/FAULT** and **Info NEW/UNCHANGED**. | Registration ≠ current state; status information category ≠ arbitrary sensor health; SAPIENT power level % ≠ Dragino battery voltage/Campbell supply. BSI registration **tag 9 vs 10** and timestamp disagreements remain unresolved. |
| **ISA 6.0 SDD / The Open Group SOSA** — [earlier direct primary review §16](#16-step-12--isa-60-and-the-open-group-sosa-physical-functional-visible-and-managed-relationships) | ISA **Component Capability Description** vs Status; Open Group SOSA **configurationState**, **operationalState**, **healthStatus**, and separate built-in-test **result**; direct versus indirect System management. | Not one newly required all-vendor state triad or proof a virtual/opaque/managed component's status directly measures the physical component's true health. |

### 24.4 Configuration versus Condition — deliberately not forced into two disconnected boxes

There is a defensible semantic **difference** between a **configuration proposition** (a setting/intended value/option or known effective setting) and an **equipment condition proposition** (a health/performance/diagnostic assessment), but source records can carry **related information with different meanings**. Examples:

- A **mode** might be an operator-selectable collection of settings, or a power-triggered operating **regime reported at T**, or both. **Do not force every mode into a static enum or assume it is already effective.**
- A **battery voltage** is a numeric equipment diagnostic, not itself a mode, a selected setpoint, a remaining-capacity percentage, or a health score. A source-specific voltage-threshold rule **could** support an interpretation of operational regime or condition if applied to a verified same-subject, same-version, appropriately timed reading; we have no such joined evidence in this corpus.
- A **reported terminal state** (Campbell SW12Volts Boolean) can describe an actual digital on/off state in the *example source*, but it is not a direct measurement of terminal voltage or confirmation of the resulting externally attached equipment condition.
- A **measurement quality flag** can indicate a suspect/accepted environmental result without being a diagnostic about physical hardware. Bennett's parameter-specific QA must **not** silently update EE181/CR1000X equipment health.

**Working candidate modeling rule:** express the **original assertion and its native scope first**, and preserve **distinct configuration, operational-state, diagnostic and assessed-condition roles when actually supported**; allow a claim to contribute to more than one interpretation only with **explicit source- or rule-supported relation and qualifications**, never by automatic inheritance or field-name matching. **The exact DIM class boundaries, shared attributes and multiplicities remain OPEN** for the integrated Step 24 check. This is not approval of a universal power/condition-first class family or a health “PASS” for any of the four cases.

### 24.5 Stop boundary and next single iteration

**What Step 20 actually delivered:** the [new candidate DIM semantics](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-20--configuration-operating-state-and-condition) and this source-qualified comparison of four cases plus targeted original donors, with explicit **REJECTED_EQUIVALENCE** tests. Earlier Steps 16–19 remain intact; the earlier candidate “Which setting applies?” section remains **historical/unaltered**, rather than silently adopted as a complete model. We have **not** observed a full live operating-System configuration/health state, run cross-vendor interpretation software, analyzed every donor artifact, adopted a new DIM class hierarchy or added CSAPI Profile obligations. **No code, original case data, source fixtures, Cloudflare deployment or 52°North/Oracle-backed server was changed. Gate D remains OPEN.**

**Historical Step 20 handoff (Step 21 is now completed in [§25](#25-step-21--source-qualified-control-request-acknowledgement-and-effect)):** develop the source-backed distinction between **available command, issued request/Task, acknowledgement, accepted/completed/failed execution and evidence of physical or reported effect**. Use SAPIENT's source TaskAck/status versus documented device commands; **do not** let an ACK alone prove a configuration change or Observation, and do not proceed into Step 22 replacement/Deployment or any Profile/client/server work.


## 25. Step 21 — Source-qualified control request, acknowledgement and effect

**Status: Step 21 COMPLETE, 2 November 2026. Step 22 NEXT; Gate D OPEN.** The [Step 21 section in the actual Candidate DIM](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-21--command-availability-issued-request-acknowledgement-outcome-and-evidence-of-effect) is the **model deliverable**. It separates a **source-declared control capability, particular issued request/Task, delivery/receipt, correlated acknowledgement, native acceptance/rejection, reported completion/failure, post-request equipment status, independently supported effect and observation**. This workbook section documents source checks and remaining gaps, **not a ten-class mandatory ontology, a newly accepted CSAPI Profile or a running control/actuation test**.

### 25.1 Plain-language result: "the camera replied" does not mean "the camera did it"

For equipment management, different kinds of evidence prove different things. A source manual can publish a **command's possible syntax**; a request can be sent to one intended target; a response can be matched to it; a response can report **acceptance**; a later response can report **completion**; and a separate readback or physical measurement may support that a requested condition actually occurred. **Those statements cannot be substituted for one another.** A response without a status does not have an invented success/failure state, and two nearby source examples need not be from the same transaction.

**Original SAPIENT v2 is especially valuable because it has distinct Task and TaskAck meanings.** [BSI Flex 335 v2.0:2024-03, §§6.5.7–.9 and Tables 86–87](https://www.bsigroup.com/siteassets/pdf/en/insights-and-media/insights/brochures/bsi-flex-335.pdf) and immutable [Dstl Task](https://github.com/dstl/SAPIENT-Proto-Files/blob/244a741c13bcee628f84944aef91e4a66854bd7d/bsi_flex_335_v2_0/task.proto) and [TaskAck](https://github.com/dstl/SAPIENT-Proto-Files/blob/244a741c13bcee628f84944aef91e4a66854bd7d/bsi_flex_335_v2_0/task_ack.proto) specifications distinguish **ACCEPTED, REJECTED, COMPLETED and FAILED** from mere command availability. The pinned TaskAck has a **mandatory-field annotation** for task_status, a task ID and possible reason strings. The source statuses express the **task participant's reported lifecycle**, not an independent physical or environmental effect.

### 25.2 Two original SAPIENT counterexamples that prevent false success

| Original published artifact and precise test | What is actually supported | Status that cannot truthfully be claimed |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **BSI original informative camera example, Annex B Figures B.5–B.6** ([earlier exact source/figure trace](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#a-published-camera-exchange)) | A fusion Node's **START/lookAt** Task at **10:29:39 UTC on 3 November 2022** and the camera's **TaskAck** at **10:33:04** have the **same task_id**: **01GGYGM3F6FHSDDMVCQJ11ZNBF**. The camera **b902eb31-97f1-4acf-b994-da3ec901074e** and fusion participant **139bf8c7-84da-4c00-a068-4d58dca747b8** exchange sender and intended-recipient roles. **The reply is correlated.** The printed TaskAck does **not** include the required task-status field. | **Acceptance, rejection, completion, failed execution and actual camera pointing are UNKNOWN.** The earlier **RegistrationAck acceptance** is a different interaction; an earlier DetectionReport has a **different task ID**. No valid CSAPI CommandStatus.statusCode or changed mode may be manufactured from this incomplete task reply. |
| **Dstl SAPIENT v2 Test Harness static mode-change templates** at commit **6a2999d70d761d224914ddd8fa3f663af5005be0**: [request](https://github.com/dstl/BSI-Flex-335-v2-Test-Harness/blob/6a2999d70d761d224914ddd8fa3f663af5005be0/SapientDmmSimulator/SapientDmmSimulator/Default.Task.Mode.json) and [TaskAck](https://github.com/dstl/BSI-Flex-335-v2-Test-Harness/blob/6a2999d70d761d224914ddd8fa3f663af5005be0/SapientAsmSimulator/SapientAsmSimulator/Default.TaskAck.ModeChange.json) | The request template commands **modeChange "REQUEST"** with **task ID ending BY6**; the separate reply template explicitly reports **TASK_STATUS_ACCEPTED** for **task ID ending BY8**. The stored reply has the **same source/destination direction as the request**, not its reverse. The templates were read directly at the pinned commit. | **No matched acceptance for the BY6 request**, no enacted mode change, no fielded device state, and no assertion that the static files are a captured operation. The Dstl simulator's generator can populate fields at runtime, but **we did not execute it**. |

**The prior offline source-matching checker is not a new Step 21 runtime result.** It already verified the published cases and synthetic negatives on **31 October 2026**, as recorded under [candidate Profile's repeatable check](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#repeatable-check-which-task-did-the-reply-answer). This iteration **read the original source definitions/templates again** and **did not rerun or deploy** the checker or send any Task. The BSI camera printed status omission is a **publisher informative-example inconsistency with normative field requiredness**, not a transmitted status of UNSPECIFIED or PENDING. The Dstl mode-change TaskAck's explicit acceptance applies to a **different, unjoined task**.

### 25.3 Four equipment-case checks: available control versus historical operation

| Case and original source scope | Source-grounded available action / activity | What is **NOT** proved by this packet |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Dragino S31-LB** — [controlled sheet C-C01–C-C18](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Dragino_S31_LB.md#5-configuration-and-command-inventory), [manual](https://wiki.dragino.com/docs/LoRaWAN-End-Node/temperature-humidity-sensors/s31-lb/) | Manufacturer documents **AT+TDC**, temperature/humidity alarm thresholds, 5-V duration, history queries, request FPort-5 status and two-point calibration command syntax. It also prints command examples, including expected OK forms. | **No actual issued command, matched acknowledgement from an identified unit, verified applied setting, calibration run, change in environmental results or state/health outcome**. The printed **AT+TDC 60000 ms** example is not even the documented 20-minute default. |
| **AirGradient Open Air Max** — [controlled sheet §2](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/AirGradient_Open_Air_Max.md#2-power-operation-and-configuration), [pinned firmware Configuration.cpp](https://github.com/Open-Air-Foundation/firmware-openair-max/blob/84e479a415d81151e7a81aea9a2f7449a15e98cc/main/Configuration.cpp) | Source documents a settings portal, cellular/Wi-Fi/network destinations and parsed **co2CalibrationRequested** and **ledBarTestRequested** flags. Design documents explain mode changes/LED patterns. | **No commanded physical calibration, LED test, authenticated unit target, request/ACK or later verified as-applied state**. Field name **Requested** is not a completion report; config may apply at a later wake cycle, which was not witnessed. |
| **Campbell CR1000X + first-generation ClimaVue 50** — [controlled sheet D-V01–V08](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Campbell_CR1000X_ClimaVue50.md#3-sensor-identity-verification-and-controls) | The sensor manual documents **aI!, aV!, aAb!, aM!, aC! and address query** commands; separate manufacturer example programs and logger-only Status structures exist. | **No captured sensor reply, changed installed address, actual deployed program task, or fielded sensor/logger command-outcome chain**. The printed **SN-yyyyyyyy** is a placeholder; 2026 logger-only **CompileResults** does not establish an execution of the 2019 ClimaVue example. |
| **Bennett's Point** — [operator's 2024 station metadata, §13](https://cdmo.baruch.sc.edu/waf/YearlyFiles/ACE%20Basin/meteorological/metadata/acemet01-12.24m.pdf), [pinned historical source](../Candidate_Specification/examples/bennetts-point-ee181.json) | Actual operator **multi-instrument maintenance and probe replacement on 16 April 2024**, and a **CR1000X operating-system update on 17 April** are historically documented. Two genuine accepted-initial-QA temperature summaries and a separately rejected one are available. | Historical service documentation **is not a digitally captured Task, its ACK, actual low-level control exchange, exact changeover minute or independently observed successful configuration transition**. The rejected environmental result on 17 April does not supply a device fault code or completion status. |

**Counterexample logic:** Source capability ≠ observed request. An example request ≠ historical device dispatch. **Matched reply without native status ≠ accepted.** Explicit status from a **wrongly correlated reply ≠ outcome of the task being investigated**. Explicit **COMPLETED** would establish **source-reported** completion, but no independent physical effect without stronger evidence. An actual subsequent device-state report can establish **reported state at its own time** but not necessarily which command caused it. Environmental Observation/QA is separate from Task status. This is a **qualitative source-to-DIM model falsification**, not a conformance PASS.

### 25.4 Original standard meanings and outstanding interoperability work

- **SAPIENT v2, BSI Flex 335:2024-03**, §§6.2.5, 6.3.2, 6.5.7–.9 and immutable Dstl [task.proto](https://github.com/dstl/SAPIENT-Proto-Files/blob/244a741c13bcee628f84944aef91e4a66854bd7d/bsi_flex_335_v2_0/task.proto), [task_ack.proto](https://github.com/dstl/SAPIENT-Proto-Files/blob/244a741c13bcee628f84944aef91e4a66854bd7d/bsi_flex_335_v2_0/task_ack.proto) and [status_report.proto](https://github.com/dstl/SAPIENT-Proto-Files/blob/244a741c13bcee628f84944aef91e4a66854bd7d/bsi_flex_335_v2_0/status_report.proto) provide **declaration, Task, TaskAck and reported mode** meanings, not universal evidence that physical effects were achieved.
- **OGC API – Connected Systems Part 2 v1.0**, [OGC 23-002 §10](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html), has its own control command, command-status, required status-code and object-scope rules. **The prior proposed SAPIENT→CSAPI mapping in the Candidate Profile is historical candidate work**, not revised or adopted here; no absent native status may be replaced with a guessed valid-looking statusCode.
- **W3C WoT Architecture/Thing Description 1.1** provides digital actions and intermediaries where an ActionAffordance or queued proxy action is not an effect; **SensorML 3.0** describes process/method but not executed command; **ISA SDD 6.0** and **The Open Group SOSA** distinguish control/manager/tasking and actual status with edition-specific boundaries. See earlier original-source reviews in [workbook §§16–18](#16-step-12--isa-60-and-the-open-group-sosa-physical-functional-visible-and-managed-relationships).
- **Source integrity guard:** BSI **Registration Table 15 config_data tag 9** versus pinned Protobuf **tag 10**, plus BSI message **content timestamp** versus Protobuf **sent-time comment**, remain unresolved conflicts. Do not use either mismatch to manufacture time semantics or a completed control chain. In any actual future converter, source edition and wire version must be checked before applying a translation.

**What was actually delivered:** a new [candidate DIM Step 21 action-lifecycle section](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-21--command-availability-issued-request-acknowledgement-outcome-and-evidence-of-effect), this workbook evidence/rejected-equivalence record, and execution-plan/README status updates. Earlier candidate control examples remain preserved with their qualification. **No new source artifact, physical deployment, command request, actual acknowledgement, device-state readback, successful task or completed environmental Observation was acquired.** No automatic cross-vendor action semantics, CSAPI Profile SHALL, Glaux code, cloud deployment or live CSAPI server mutation.

**Gate D remains OPEN** until source-qualified Step 22 physical replacement/Host/Deployment history, Step 23 whole-System cross-cutting gap checks, and Step 24 integrated DIM relationship diagram/falsification. **Historical Step 21 handoff (Step 22 is now complete in [§26](#26-step-22--dated-equipment-use-and-persistent-station-identity)):** — time-scoped installed equipment roles and identity continuity at Bennett's Point, with dates rather than invented swap clock times or new CSAPI Deployment assignments. Do not start Profile/Glaux implementation or additional donor research in that same iteration.


## 26. Step 22 — Dated equipment-use and persistent station identity

**Status: Step 22 COMPLETE, 2 November 2026. Step 23 NEXT. Phase D Gate D OPEN.** The model deliverable is the [new Step 22 candidate DIM section on equipment replacement, dated service, Host/Deployment and maintenance](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-22--equipment-replacement-dated-service-hosting-deployment-and-maintenance). This workbook entry records **the existing original-source evidence, three period/identity tests, four-case falsification and original donor semantic boundaries**. It **does not create an actual SensorML/OMS/CSAPI Deployment resource**, issue an equipment command, inspect an installed probe or claim any original-source timestamps beyond the documented resolution.

### 26.1 What we learned, in ordinary language

**Bennett's Point was the same station before and after the probe changed.** The operator reports actual station **acebpmet**, two different individual EE181 physical probe serials, and **three date-qualified stretches of equipment use**. One physical probe **came back** after having served previously. That means **two physical units in three use periods**, not three units or separate new stations; a reusable product model EE181 is not an individual. A unit's role *at a station during a period* is a relationship between separately identified referents, **not a permanent physical-containment property** of either one.

**The original dates are not exact transition instants.** The operator's equipment table shares the **8 June 2023** and **16 April 2024** dates between outgoing and incoming use descriptions. It does **not** specify a probe's installation/removal hour, whether its end-day and the next probe's start-day overlap, or a particular time-zone-specific instant for the transition. The **16 April 09:45–13:30 fixed-EST station downtime/maintenance interval is a broader, multi-instrument work window**, not a sampled probe connection/disconnection trace. Turning those date-only entries into fabricated midnight endpoints or the precise start/end of that maintenance window would change the source meaning.

### 26.2 Source-native history: three use records, same station, two physical units

The original [2024 ACE Basin/Bennett's Point meteorological metadata](https://cdmo.baruch.sc.edu/waf/YearlyFiles/ACE%20Basin/meteorological/metadata/acemet01-12.24m.pdf), particularly **§9 Temp/RH equipment-use history (printed p. 6), §9 logger/transmitter (printed pp. 10–11), §13 maintenance (p. 13)**, was **previously verified and source-attributed in workbook §12**. The original metadata PDF and annual CSV were **not re-downloaded or independently re-extracted in this Step 22**; use the preserved [source-bound selected snapshot](../Candidate_Specification/examples/bennetts-point-ee181.json), including its original row/source distinctions.

| Host and source-native role | Model / individual equipment | Original date-scoped claim | Confidence and critical boundary |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **acebpmet / Temp-RH sensor 3** | **EE181 / 21281600077814** | **4 May 2022 – 8 June 2023** | **SOURCE_FACT** about first described service period. Sensor number 3 is a station-local label, not global identity. |
| **acebpmet / Temp-RH sensor 4** | **EE181 / 222016000109CE** | **8 June 2023 – 16 April 2024** | **SOURCE_FACT** about another physical individual in the same station role. No exact end instant is provided. |
| **acebpmet / Temp-RH sensor 3 (returned)** | **EE181 / 21281600077814** | **16 April 2024 – still in use as of 31 December 2024** | **SOURCE_FACT** of returning use and 2024 as-of status. **No removal date and no 2026 current-status assertion.** |
| **acebpmet / active datalogger role** | **CR1000X / serial 13916** | **4 May 2022 – still in use as of 31 December 2024** | Different physical equipment, station logger role; inactive serial **13915** separately listed, not presumed currently running. |
| **acebpmet / GOES transmitter role** | **TX321 / serial 2335** | **Installed 4 May 2022** | Physical reporting participant distinct from logger/probes; removal date **UNKNOWN**. |

**Two dates shared by consecutive periods do not establish an overlap or precise handoff clock time.** The returned EE181 is **the same physical serial** as in 2022–2023; its resumption does not mean a newly manufactured instrument. The continuing station code is unchanged. The source metadata **last revised 18 August 2025** is a revision/publication attribute **of the operator document**, not an additional field-install date. The 2024 as-of status is not an end-of-deployment instant.

### 26.3 Maintenance, calibrated-equipment context and actual observation roles

- **16 April 2024:** the CR1000X was documented powered down around **09:45 EST** during maintenance; work affecting several instruments, including a temperature/humidity sensor replacement, finished and the station logger resumed around **13:30 EST**. The recorded **13:45** summary was rejected for averaging uncertainty after the work. **Neither 09:45 nor 13:30 is a verified EE181 swap instant.**
- **17 April 2024:** distinct logger operating-system **CR1000X.Std.07.02** update; a **12:00 EST** station ATemp record was rejected **F_ATemp = "<-3> [GPR] (CSM)"**. It is **not** evidence of another probe swap, a digital Task/TaskAck, or a source-stated EE181 hardware failure.
- **Source calibration metadata:** the older EE181 **222016000109CE** lists **20 May 2022** as last calibration; the returning **21281600077814** lists **4 March 2024**. These are documentary last-calibration date claims, **not verified per-record calibration results, current health or a universal calibration lifecycle**.
- **Two genuine historical air-temperature station summaries:** **15 April 2024 12:00 fixed EST = 29.1 °C**, **18 April 2024 12:00 fixed EST = 27.3 °C**, both original **F_ATemp = "<0> "** (passed initial parameter quality checks only). Their nominal **16:45–17:00 UTC** sampling intervals and **probable contributing EE181 serials** are **DERIVED** from source convention and the equipment-use schedule, **not originally present as source row fields**. Both selected full periods fall away from the 16 April transition's ambiguous date. No same-day probe attribution is asserted here.

**Do not treat maintenance as an executed API command, the observation QA as instrument health, or the physical probe as necessarily the same OMS Observer as the software/logger producing the averaged result.** Station identity, individual equipment and functional/processing/telemetry roles survive independently. Bennett still has **two original selected NOAA source temperature measurements**, despite the project's **four prior test-server Observation representations**, and this Step 22 does not create a new measurement.

### 26.4 Model relationship meanings with explicit provenance and time precision

| Candidate relationship / proposition (NOT adopted key) | What its evidence must preserve | Falsification / negative test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Station/Host has source identity that persists** | Name/code, native issuer, site-level evidence and time scope of the document. | Creating another station because a temperature probe changes. |
| **Individual equipment served a role at a Host** | Actual physical serial, source model/kind, station code, **source role label**, original first/last/as-of **dates**, issuer/locator and evidence grade. | Making date-only service into precise UTC start/end instants; station ID = probe ID. |
| **One individual can have several separate service periods** | Same serial and product kind in two source records, keeping both time-qualified associations. | Duplicating physical serial **21281600077814** into two separate pieces of equipment. |
| **Maintenance event concerns specific documented participants** | Maintenance description, source date, actual work-window times where supplied, affected role/equipment *only when identified*. | 09:45 or 13:30 station work events automatically timestamp exact probe removal/install. |
| **Calibration/versioning metadata qualifies a specific item** | Which probe, source **last-calibration date**, associated maintenance/program record and assertion scope. | Last calibration means verified accuracy/healthy at the selected temperature observation or today. |
| **Station spatial context differs from unit geometry** | Original **station point 32.55934° N, 80.45456° W** and Temp/RH role mounting **about 3.3–3.35 m**, without fabricating serialized-probe surveyed coordinates. | Automatically assigning station coordinates as separately verified geometry of either EE181. |
| **Observation may be associated with historical service** | Source **station+role+whole nominal averaging interval** join from actual original row to separate operator use history; preserve **DERIVED** rather than SOURCE_FACT identity. | NOAA ATemp itself contains probe serial, per-sample sensor provenance or native actual resultTime. |
| **Deployment carrier may later express qualified association** | Match actual conceptual model's **Observer/System/Host/Deployment** meanings, source date resolution, spatial/temporal evidence, and unknowns when choosing future binding. | A service-use history is already a populated valid CSAPI/OMS/SensorML Deployment record; a hypothetical reference product is a deployed unit. |

The source's **day-only first/last-use assertions must remain at that granularity**. If a later profile requires explicit instants or a date-time interval, *that is an encoding gap to address honestly*, not permission to infer midnight, inclusive/exclusive endpoints, a probe connection timestamp or a probe's unverified location. Treat “still in use as of” as a **statement of knowledge at one dated checkpoint** rather than a complete future lifetime history. An exact date-only record remains valuable evidence even though it cannot support every derived API property.

### 26.5 Original donor native meanings, and what they do NOT resolve

**OGC Observations, Measurements and Samples (OMS) 3.0**, original [OGC 20-082r4 §§8.6–8.8 and 9.8.3](https://docs.ogc.org/as/20-082r4/20-082r4.html) distinguishes **Observer (identifiable performer), Host (grouping of observers, such as an environmental monitoring station), and Deployment (assignment of an Observer to a Host)**. In the Abstract Observation Core, **deploymentTime**, when supplied, is a **TM_Period**. **This does not imply** the selected source equipment-use table explicitly names an OMS Observer for each 15-minute aggregate, carries a native formal Deployment object, defines exact interval instants, or establishes permanent physical composition.

**OGC Sensor Model Language (SensorML) 3.0**, original [OGC 23-000 §8.9 and Requirement 44](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-000/23-000.html) introduces a **Deployment** description of when/where/why/how; its **DeployedSystem** references an actual described system/process and a **configuration** with separately qualified **platform** and **location**. It does **not** automatically fill the unknown physical EE181 unit geometry, precise swap time, or active CR1000X program version. The eventual CSAPI Part 1/2 Profile may be able to bind some of these facts, but Step 22 does **not** choose source-to-API mappings, normative class cardinalities or a station-led versus probe-led DataStream owner.

**AAS v3.2, OPC UA Devices v1.05.0, W3C/OGC 2017 SSN and separately marked 2026 SSN Working Draft** have asset, physical component, relationship, Host or deployment vocabulary **with different original semantics**, not one universal standard “part-of,” replacement or temporal-Deployment class. The draft remains a Working Draft, not a replacement recommendation, and no 182-donor comprehensive conformance review is implied by this targeted analysis.

**Important separate time concepts:** date-only physical role-use history, bounded multi-instrument maintenance event time, probe's documentary last-calibration date, document revision/as-of date, SensorML description validTime, OMS Observation phenomenonTime and resultTime, and device status report time **are not interchangeable**. **Bennett's original result-availability time still remains UNKNOWN**; source station timestamp/derived nominal sampling period cannot be repurposed to fill it. No independent physical-probe location is derived from a station point.

### 26.6 Stop point and next authorized step

**Completed:** the [candidate DIM Step 22 relationship section](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-22--equipment-replacement-dated-service-hosting-deployment-and-maintenance), this original-source/temporal-granularity evidence review and four-case falsification, with equipment identities, dates, station continuity and unresolved semantic gaps explicitly retained. **No exact exchange instants, inclusive/endpoints, full machine-readable Deployment instances, invented current service, new Profile SHALL, shared client view, new commands, new source records, server changes or Cloudflare deployment**. The earlier source sheets, Bennett snapshot, donor research and Steps 16–21 model material remain intact.

**Gate D remains OPEN** until **Step 23 whole-System cross-cutting power, communications, software, security, provenance, quality and maintenance coverage**, followed by **Step 24 integrated relationship diagram/source falsification**.

**Step 23 NEXT, only on the owner's next “proceed”:** evaluate **cross-cutting whole-System coverage and evidence gaps** from the original sources, preserving only warranted qualifiers and documenting explicit deferrals. Do not create the integrated Step 24 diagram, select Profile bindings, change Glaux or deploy anything during this Step 23 iteration.


## 27. Step 23 — Cross-cutting whole-System coverage and evidence gaps

**Status: STEP 23 COMPLETE · 2 November 2026; STEP 24 NEXT; Phase D Gate D OPEN.** Candidate model output: [Step 23 — cross-cutting whole-System source-qualified relationships](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-23--cross-cutting-whole-system-services-assertions-and-evidence-limitations). This section is the **bounded source-first coverage review** of power/energy, communications, software/firmware, security, provenance, data quality and maintenance. It relies on the same four cases and original donor sources as earlier steps. It does **not** complete the integrated Step 24 model/diagram or any Profile, software or runtime implementation.

### 27.1 Plain-language finding

**A whole sensing System needs connections among its equipment, operations, reports and management; it does not need seven new universal classes just because we reviewed seven cross-cutting subjects.** The substantive problem is attribution: battery design belongs to a model or specific part; a measured voltage would belong to a particular source unit and instant; firmware may apply to a logger or processor rather than an external probe; result quality belongs to a specific reported value; and a station-wide maintenance event may affect several devices without identifying an exact replacement instant. A false assignment to the entire focal System would undo much of our identity, function, activity and time research.

The Step 23 [candidate DIM section](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-23--cross-cutting-whole-system-services-assertions-and-evidence-limitations) therefore proposes **qualifiers on existing equipment, interface, process, state, action, report and historical-service assertions**, not new compulsory domain modules. This is consistent with the source-attested, bounded working structure chosen at [Gate C (§19)](#19-step-15--phase-c-comparison-bounded-working-subject-and-gate-c), but it remains subject to the **integrated Step 24 falsification**.

**Evidence categories used:** SOURCE_FACT at exact *manufacturer design* or *operator historical* scope; SOURCE_EXAMPLE for publisher example values; DERIVED for our calculation or join; CANDIDATE_MAPPING for our interpretive proposal; VERIFIED_RUNTIME only for previously documented live tests; UNKNOWN for unavailable source facts; and REJECTED_EQUIVALENCE for explicitly invalid shortcuts. Manufacturer manual capability is never automatically actual installed state.

### 27.2 Source locator and four-case coverage matrix

**C** = [Dragino S31-LB inventory](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Dragino_S31_LB.md), original manual/decoder source keys C01–C04 (source §9). **A** = [AirGradient Open Air Max inventory](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/AirGradient_Open_Air_Max.md), hardware README pinned **89aacf5**, firmware README pinned **84e479a**, A06/A07 product guidance and A09 separate manufacturer cloud examples (source §8). **D** = [Campbell CR1000X + first-generation ClimaVue 50 inventory](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Campbell_CR1000X_ClimaVue50.md), original 2024 ClimaVue manual, separate 2019/2021 program references and 2026 logger Status example (source §9). **B** = original Bennett's Point 2024 ACE Basin operator history and NOAA/CDMO measurements already established in [§12](#12-step-08--bennetts-point-actual-station-time-scoped-probes-and-historical-temperature-reports) and [§26](#26-step-22--dated-equipment-use-and-persistent-station-identity). These are *not four equally complete deployed Systems*. Previously pinned Bennett source rows and metadata were **not re-downloaded** in this step.

| Topic | C — Dragino documented reference | A — AirGradient documented reference | D — Campbell documented pairing | B — Bennett actual historical station |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **X23-P: Power** | **Design C-I04–06, C-P01–05:** 8,500 mAh nonrechargeable Li/SOCl2, 2.5–3.6 V supply and rated currents. **Examples/formats C-F01, C-S05:** possible battery voltage, **not** actual remaining charge. | **Design A-P01–07:** 10 W/18 V panel, three 3,500 mAh 18650 cells **in series**, USB-C and battery-switch options. **A-M01–04** describes firmware-0.7.0+ modes; A-F11/F12 and A-API37/38 are unjoined **examples**, not measured installed power. | **Design D-I04–07:** distinct logger and sensor 12 V support. **Separate logger Status example:** D-S03 Battery=13.2368 Vdc versus D-S04 LithiumBattery=3.70477 V; D-S30/31 SW12Volts are Boolean terminal states. **Not sensor supply measurements.** | **Operator:** station solar/power-support context; CR1000X shutdown/restart during 16 April maintenance. **No real continuous battery/solar output, remaining capacity or current 2026 power state.** |
| **X23-C: Communications** | **Design C-I12–15, C-O03, C-O08–11:** LoRaWAN 1.0.3 Class A and default OTAA/20-min uplink; BLE/UART/downlink; time-sync and historical retransmission possibilities. **No authenticated gateway/session or delivery success.** | **Design A-I11–12, A-C02/10–12:** cellular LTE and Wi-Fi, different HTTP/MQTT and batching/cache behavior. README says cellular retries with cache lost at power-down; Wi-Fi path has no equivalent retry cache. Cloud example ≠ physical transport log. | **Design D-I06/08:** SDI-12 sensor bus/address, separate logger. **D-I16/D-C06–07:** unrelated later MQTT Status publishing, not proved as the selected ClimaVue installation. No actual broker/session. | **Operator:** CR1000X 5-second inputs and 15-minute summaries; TX321 **2335** sends four summaries hourly via GOES toward NOAA, distinct from archive/LoggerNet/USB paths. No per-packet delivery/latency or live network condition. |
| **X23-S: Software/firmware** | **Design C-I16, C-S02, C-O12:** region-dependent firmware, FPort-5 version reply format and possible OTA/UART servicing; scalar version example not an acquired node reply. | **Design A-I03/I04:** separate pinned hardware and firmware source revisions; portal/OTA behavior A-C01–09; A-M version-specific timing. README hourly configuration checks differ from guide 90-min/daily; no verified running source/build. | **Design D-I13, D-V02/03, D-C01–05:** CRBasic programming and sensor version-query definitions; historical 2019 program and 2021 package remain unjoined. D-S06 CompileResults is a **different logger example**, not actual running program. | **Operator history:** CR1000X.Std.07.02 logger OS update on **17 April 2024**; station logging/program references exist, but complete installed program and software lineage are unverified. Not another probe swap or executed API Task. |
| **X23-A: Security/identity/access** | Design describes DevEUI/OTAA registration and keys (**C-I15**); no acquired installed credentials, authorized actor, or live command-permission record. | **A-C03–05:** configurable APN, Wi-Fi credentials and HTTP destination; no authenticated unit, verified protected transport/session or role policy. **A-G07** explicitly withholds full security/lifecycle assessment. | **D-G07:** no evidence of executed command authorization, actor roles or security enforcement for the sensor/logger reference pairing. Published protocol commands do not constitute permissions. | Operator site/equipment identifiers are actual historical evidence, **not** network credentials, API privilege proof or 2026 authorization policy.
