These are **analytical crosschecks**, not passed OGC compliance tests or software assertions.

| Check | False source→CSAPI binding to attempt | Outcome and forward rule |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **T26-01 Reference vs instance** | Create one `System` for each Dragino, AirGradient and Campbell product reference sheet. | **REJECTED:** Part 1 §9.1 note 1 and the actual corpus support **Procedure/type** descriptions, not authenticated installed Systems. |
| **T26-02 Identity reuse vs duplicate individual** | Create a third actual EE181 System when serial **21281600077814** returns in 2024. | **REJECTED:** stable source individual; two units, three period statements and one continuing station. |
| **T26-03 Equal model vs equal unit** | Merge the two serial-numbered probes because both have model `EE181` / a common kind Procedure. | **REJECTED:** one kind can link to several individual Systems; the original serials and station are different identity schemes. |
| **T26-04 Physical part vs external** | Publish Dragino external SHT31, Campbell separately manufactured sensor/logger and Bennett interchangeable probes as permanently nested internal Subsystems without distinct meanings. | **REJECTED:** Part 1 §10.2 composition versus aggregation; source-specific connection/hosting does not imply one parent/part tree. |
| **T26-05 Source output vs physical inventory** | Make two PM1/PM2 sockets into PM1.0/PM2.5 physical types; make 14 ClimaVue response positions 14 Systems. | **REJECTED:** original components, connectors, interface addresses and output values are separate. Full output/channel bindings reserved for Step 27. |
| **T26-06 Identity local ID vs source UID** | Treat station code `acebpmet`, application `id`, manufacturer EE181 serial, `sosa:Sensor` URI or a source record URL as all the same kind of unique ID. | **REJECTED:** distinct referent/scheme/issuer; `uid` must be a URI and be unique in claimed service scope. A profile project URI requires future traceable issuance. |
| **T26-07 Historic date vs exact instant** | Write the source 16 Apr 2024 change as UTC midnight or as 09:45/13:30 maintenance boundaries. | **REJECTED / TEMPORAL HOLD:** original history is calendar day only and broader work window is separate. The proposed exact `Deployment.validTime` is **NOT populated**. |
| **T26-08 2024 as-of vs ongoing validity** | Use `now` in `Deployment.validTime` for returning probe because the source says in use **as of 31 Dec 2024**. | **REJECTED:** “now” would imply a statement current beyond the operator's evidence, not a harmless missing value. |
| **T26-09 Geometry scope** | Assign station CRS84 point or product example `0,0` to individual serial-numbered probes or to design-only Procedures. | **REJECTED:** station geometry is source station context, probe surveyed position absent; Procedure is nonspatial. |
| **T26-10 Manufacturer Procedure provenance** | Declare the current project-authored EE181 SensorML `typeOf` static fixture a manufacturer-validated, published CSAPI Procedure. | **REJECTED:** its authorship and resolution status remain explicit; publication and source authority require later evidence and tests. |
| **T26-11 SensorML class from generic word** | Convert every process/product into a `PhysicalSystem` or every assembly board into its own fielded `System` because it appears as a physical component in a datasheet. | **REJECTED:** class is determined by original physical/computational/process scope and actual described granularity, not names. |
| **T26-12 Premature DataStream owner** | Choose the station or individual probe as producing System because a deployment candidate contains a probe. | **REJECTED/OPEN:** the aggregated 15-minute summary involves sensing/processing/publishing roles; Part 2 one-System restriction and actual source data must be handled in Step 27. |

**Result:** Each standard-native mapping that passed its source-attested referent and representation prerequisites is a **credible candidate** for later Profile drafting. Crucially, the original source **does not** warrant an accepted, conformant encoded Deployment for the three Bennett period statements, a fabricated instance for any manufacturer reference product, or a particular Observation/DataStream owning System. Those limits remain **deliberate, recorded failures of the stronger claim**, not successes disguised by a generic disclaimer.

Directly challenge **one producing System per DataStream** against the real Bennett station-temperature aggregation/relay and preserve source-labeled contributor vs observer vs publisher; do not solve it by selecting whatever legacy live probe stream already exists. ## 31. Step 27 — Interface, output, DataStream and producing-System binding review

**Status: STEP 27 COMPLETE, 2 November 2026; STEP 28 NEXT; Phase E Gate E OPEN.** This is the **source-first audit and falsification** of [Candidate CSAPI Profile — Step 27, P27-01–14](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#step-27--interface-output-and-datastream-producing-system-binding-proposals). It interprets original OGC API – Connected Systems (CSAPI) Part 2, Sensor Model Language (SensorML) 3.0, SWE Common 3.0, conceptual Observations, Measurements and Samples (OMS) 3.0, public IEEE 1451 technical material and the **four original case evidence scopes**. It does **not** adopt an unresolved producing-System choice, issue a new Profile SHALL, fabricate a fielded unit or runtime Observation, reconfigure the 52°North server, or implement the client. [Step 25 §29](#29-step-25--original-csapi-and-ogc-standard-requirements-evidence-qualified-profile-binding-inventory), [Step 26 §30](#30-step-26--identity-kind-composition-host-and-deployment-binding-proposals), and [integrated DIM Step 24](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-24--integrated-source-bounded-whole-system-dim-and-relationship-falsification) remain the controlling prior source and relationship context.

### 31.1 What we learned, without the API terminology

**A sensing part, its native connection, a data field, and a published series of observations cannot be assumed to be the same “channel.”** Dragino's external probe produces environmental properties, but the S31-LB node packages LoRaWAN messages. AirGradient's two PMS5003 devices are connected at sockets called PM1/PM2, yet its output keys pm01/pm25/pm10 are *particulate quantities*, not sockets. Campbell's weather instrument returns fourteen ordered values through one SDI-12 device address, while a separate datalogger can process them and issue unrelated logger Status reports. Bennett's individually serialized probes supplied inputs at different dates, but the **published values are 15-minute station summaries** produced through a logger/archival chain.

The [approved Part 2 §9.1–9.2](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) gives **DataStream a specific semantic job**: it represents data from **one output of an observation System**, with **all contained Observations produced by the same System and sharing the same observed properties/result schema**. Table 5 requires the stream's **one `system` association** to a System resource. The standard allows one Observation to hold **several measured properties**, so we cannot arbitrarily require one physical sensor or one DataStream for every response field. A sensor capability description without actual observations is useful, but it is not the same proof as a real DataStream with correctly attributed original results.

**Bennett's Point makes the distinction concrete:** the source establishes *one continued station*, one actual CR1000X logger serial **13916** generating 15-minute summaries from 5-second input, changing EE181 probes and separate GOES/CDMO distribution. **The correct source-supported physical contributor is not necessarily the CSAPI System that produces the completed 15-minute mean.** Station-led and logger-led output ownership are both source-plausible; the old live probe-owned scalar and archival-report streams do not settle that choice. We now record alternatives, evidence conditions and contradictions rather than selecting the previous code's data layout by convenience.

### 31.2 Exact original standard and schema trail — requirements have different authority

| Original source; actual inspected clause | Established meaning or property | Limit that prevents unsupported mapping |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **CSAPI Part 2, OGC 23-002 v1.0, §§9.1–9.2, Tables 2–5** ([approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html)) | `DataStream` = data produced by a **single output of one System**; all Observations share **the same producing System, observed property set and result schema**. Table 3 `type=status` relates to parent/subsystem state versus `type=observation` for external features. Table 5 `system` association is **one required System**. | Identifying a particular original Sensing/Processing/Station System is a producer decision requiring source evidence. No conclusion that field, probe or publisher must be that System; the stream is not a physical part or transducer channel. |
| **Part 2 §9.2.2 Table 2 and §16.1.3 Req95** ([original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html), [companion JSON `dataStream.json`](https://github.com/opengeospatial/ogcapi-connected-systems/blob/master/api/part2/openapi/schemas/json/dataStream.json)) | JSON `system@link` references producer; optional `outputName` relates to the named System output. `observedProperties`, `resultType`, `phenomenonTime`, `resultTime` must be **server-generated from nested Observations**; **null** if none. Optional `procedure@link` and `deployment@link` are only summary links if **every Observation shares the association** (source companion schema). | A manufacturer-only advertised output cannot be promoted into populated real Observation-derived summary values; `outputName` does not prove a physical sensor, and the 2026 mutable GitHub companion schema is **not independently hashed against the exact published 2025 release**. |
| **Part 2 §9.6 Requirement 11 and §9.7/Table 6–7** ([original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html)) | One `Observation` always belongs to a `DataStream`; it may contain **several observed properties**. For every DataStream, `GET /datastreams/{id}/schema?obsFormat=...` must provide a schema for the requested **supported** media format. `procedure` is a *performed-method* association; `samplingFeature` is the observed sampling subject where supplied. | Calling a service's media type accepted is **not** proof that the schema or payload follows its declared encoding. OMS observer/producer and original result-availability time are not solved by API nested resource URLs. |
| **Part 2 §16.1.4 Req96–98** ([original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html), [companion `observationSchemaJson.json`](https://github.com/opengeospatial/ogcapi-connected-systems/blob/master/api/part2/openapi/schemas/json/observationSchemaJson.json)) | JSON observation `result` conforms to SWE `resultSchema`; optional `parameters` conforms to a declared `parametersSchema`; media `application/json` uses its own Observation representation. | A grouped `DataRecord` result is *not* by itself a source-proven actual Observation nor a native solution to original Bennett phenomenonTime interval, resultTime or QA. **Step 29** owns that mapping. |
| **SensorML 3.0, OGC 23-000 §§8.2.9.1, 8.3–8.6, 9.3.3–9.3.4; Req11–12** ([approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-000/23-000.html)) | Process `inputs`/`outputs`/`parameters` may use `ObservableProperty` and SWE Common types; directly encoded output may be a `DataInterface`. An `AggregateProcess` description can have named `components[]` and directional `connections[].source` / `destination`. | A process output form is not necessarily a CSAPI `DataStream` resource, a physical native interface address, a deployed actual System, or a completed Observation. Unverified Campbell runtime program and probe-installed arrangements remain unknown. |
| **SWE Common 3.0, OGC 24-014 §§8.2.8–8.2.9 (Quantity/Time), 8.3.1 (DataRecord) and §10.2 JSON** ([approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/24-014/24-014.html)) | `Quantity`, `Text` and `DataRecord` support typed values and field name, definition, unit, encoding/context. A grouped record **may legitimately contain multiple properties** at one reporting context. | A scalar field is not one physical sensor; a battery voltage and air temperature may be in the same vendor report without being the same observed feature; a `DataRecord` does not determine whether an Observation actually happened. |
| **OMS 3.0, OGC 20-082r4 §§8.2, 8.5.2, 8.6.2, 8.7–8.8** ([approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/as/20-082r4/20-082r4.html), [original PDF recovery index](../Primary_Sources/OGC_20-082r4_OMS_3.0.0.md)) | **ObservingProcedure = reusable method**; **Observer = individually identifiable entity/algorithm implementation capable of generating Observations**; Host = grouped observing context; Deployment = observer-to-Host association. | A manufacturer product kind Procedure is not automatically the exact executed method. Physical EE181, actual processing/logger, GOES transfer, station Host and archive publisher are not proven the *same* Observer for one aggregated result. Full method/program lineage and original Bennett `resultTime` remain missing. |
| **IEEE 1451.0-2024-linked public technical evidence** ([Meta TEDS](https://iml.ubi.pt/playground/docs/ieee1451/teds/meta-teds/), [TransducerChannel TEDS](https://iml.ubi.pt/playground/docs/ieee1451/teds/transducer-channel-teds/); [qualified original study §17](#17-step-13--ieee-1451-transducerchannel-evidence-and-sensorml-30-processsystem-semantics)) | Source-native TIM shared metadata, TransducerChannel information, MaxChan/group/proxy, PhyUnits, sample period and time-source fields show **actual addressed-channel versus module versus output result distinctions**. | Full edition-specific normative IEEE 2024 field requiredness **not verified**; no selected manufacturer was demonstrated to implement IEEE 1451 TEDS. Do **not** reinterpret LoRaWAN FPort, AirGradient socket or Campbell `aR7!` result position as an IEEE channel. |

**Authority:** published approved OGC normative text applies only in the corresponding standard requirements classes. Manufacturer manuals and original ACE Basin/NOAA records establish what the *case* says, not which CSAPI extension to invent. The current GitHub `master` companion schemas are **source artifacts read directly in Step 27**, but not an independently verified byte-identical release snapshot. An earlier success loading and displaying resources on 52°North/Glaux does not turn an experimental producer assignment into OGC normative authority.

### 31.3 The four original systems — outputs, physical channels and producer evidence must remain disjoint

| Source case and exact internal source record | What is actually supported at native scope | Candidate output/DataStream interpretation **without fabricated activity** |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Dragino S31-LB** — [C sheet: C-I02, C-I12–17; C-F01–07; C-H01–08; C-S01–05](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Dragino_S31_LB.md) | **DESIGN** one external SHT31 temperature/humidity probe on 3 m cable; node packages outputs into **LoRaWAN FPort-2 measurement**, **FPort-3 history** and **FPort-5 status** forms; `TempC_SHT31` (°C) and `Hum_SHT31` (%RH) are two environmental properties, `BatV` is node voltage. C-E01–08 are original *manufacturer example history bytes*, NOT an authenticated identified fielded unit. | A manufacturer datasheet `Procedure` may describe two source-proven properties and the node/probe functional flow. A future *identified* S31-LB node/report-output System could be evaluated as producer for actual node-emitted measurements, with proper schema/property set; **NO DataStream/Observation populated from examples now**. FPorts are message discriminators, not individual sensor Systems or TEDS channels. |
| **AirGradient Open Air Max** — [A sheet: A-I05–16; A-F01–18; A-API01–43](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/AirGradient_Open_Air_Max.md) | **DESIGN** main board plus two physical PMS5003 particulate sensors at hardware PM1/PM2 sockets; other CO2, SHT4x, SGP41, electrochemical and communications modules. Native **`pm01`/`pm25`/`pm10`** denote reported particulate fractions; Wi-Fi example is 17 named fields, cellular batches **1 interval integer + 3 × 17 values**; separate cloud W/L replies have different keys, timestamps and source-validity caveats. | Candidate type-level SensorML outputs may describe original device-versus-cloud representations. One fixed result schema/source method/producing System must be justified for a **real identified device** before any `DataStream`. No joining Wi-Fi/cellular/cloud examples into one actual run or treating raw O3/NO2 electrode voltages as certified gas concentrations. |
| **Campbell CR1000X + first-generation ClimaVue 50** — [D sheet: D-I01–17, D-R01–14, D-V01–08, D-S01–31](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Campbell_CR1000X_ClimaVue50.md) | **DESIGN** separate ClimaVue sensing assembly and CR1000X logger, SDI-12 bus whose device address is **default 0**, one `aR7!` response of **14 value positions** (air temperature °C, humidity fractional, other quantities), possible CRBasic transformations. A later **31-field logger-only Status sample** belongs to a different source/scope. **No actual matched installed pair or original numerical ClimaVue response**. | Candidate type-level `DataRecord` for one source-defined `aR7!` output, a separate logger status output descriptor, and possible producer roles sensor for native response versus logger for actual *program-derived results* **only when identified runtime evidence exists**. Do not combine publisher logger `PanelTemp` with environmental `ATemp` in one uncontrolled stream or invent a real DataStream producer. |
| **Bennett's Point / ACE Basin actual 2024** — [source-preserved operator/NOAA snapshot](../Candidate_Specification/examples/bennetts-point-ee181.json), [source reconstruction §12](#12-step-08--bennetts-point-actual-station-time-scoped-probes-and-historical-temperature-reports), [dated unit service §26](#26-step-22--dated-equipment-use-and-persistent-station-identity) | **SOURCE_FACT** one continuing station `acebpmet`; two named actual EE181 serials used on different **calendar-day** service periods; CR1000X logger **13916** collects 5-second instrument readings and stores **15-minute station mean**, TX321 **2335** transmits GOES reports, NOAA/CDMO reviews/publishes. Two original station rows: **15 Apr 12:00 fixed EST, 29.1 °C** and **18 Apr 12:00 fixed EST, 27.3 °C**, original `F_ATemp` quality flags. Probe attribution and nominal 16:45–17:00 UTC windows are **DERIVED from separate original sources**; original result availability unknown. | **Two competing conditional single-output producers:** station-level `System` for native station summary across swap, or logger `System` for actual computed/stored summary; both require a published source-qualified Part 1 System/output and a clear Profile convention. Individual EE181 is attested as the physical sensing **contributor**, not directly producer of the **completed averaged report**. Source original two results must remain **two** despite four prior test-server representations. No time/QA encoding decision made here. |

**Source integrity:** The original NOAA/CDMO annual CSV was not re-fetched in Step 27; its previously retained 31 October SHA-256, two selected row tokens and equipment metadata stand as original snapshot evidence. Dragino, AirGradient and Campbell remain **manufacturer design/reference studies**, not authenticated real current/historical deployments. A built client can show design capabilities but must **not portray publisher sample payloads as physical observations**. The rejected 17 April Bennett temperature reading remains outside the two accepted historical source means.

### 31.4 Bennett's Point producer hypotheses — explicit comparison and evidence required

| Hypothesis | Positive source argument | Contradiction/risk and acceptance evidence | Step 27 standing |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **H27-A: station `acebpmet` as `DataStream.system`** | Archive rows are **station-coded 15-minute ambient air-temperature summaries**. A continuing equipment-realized station provides a stable focus across probe change, preserving one producer for one output/property/schema over both source periods. | The station must be published as an appropriately classified and individually identified Part 1 `System`; a defensible *station-level output* must be declared, with actual logger aggregation, individual EE181 inputs and publisher roles explicitly represented/qualified. **Source station code alone is not a native CSAPI producer selection.** | **VIABLE PROPOSAL; HOLD for adoption**, strong native archive identity/continuity, requires producer-output modeling. |
| **H27-B: CR1000X serial `13916` as `DataStream.system`** | Operator directly says the CR1000X program collects 5-second data and **stores/produces the 15-minute summaries**; logger is an actual identifiable physical computational participant. Can remain one result-output producer while EE181 input changes. | Need source-supported output semantics at the chosen logger `System`, correct distinction from station report/source subject, known role/period and whether the full 2024 as-run software context is adequate. A logger is not thereby the **physical sensing transducer** or NOAA archive publisher. | **VIABLE ALTERNATIVE; HOLD for adoption**, strongest evidenced immediate mean-computation role. |
| **H27-C: EE181 probe per selected mean as `DataStream.system`** | Physical temperature input and date-qualified sensor service are real; selected station results can be associated with likely contributing serial by **DERIVED** date/role/averaging-period join. | **Original source does NOT assert an EE181 produces the completed 15-minute station mean**. Splitting one source station series into probe-owned streams may be possible as an alternate engineered representation, but must preserve logger aggregation/provenance and prevent source event duplication; raw EE181 individual sample series not acquired. | **REJECT as a directly source-proven owner of the finished aggregate**, without claiming the EE181 is not a true sensing contributor. |
| **H27-D: TX321/GOES, NOAA/CDMO or our 52°North API as `DataStream.system`** | These are real/operator-described transmit, archive and project copy/presentation participants. | Native source does not say they physically sense or create the original station mean; archival QA may create a *distinct assessment or transformed output* only when separately identified. A server copying a source value is not necessarily that source's observer/producer. | **REJECT as substitute original producer**; retain distinct source authority, publishing and transport roles. |

**The single producer / multiple participants rule is the core of this step.** Part 2 **Table 5** expects one System for the declared DataStream. Our original source shows multiple participants in the *making, averaging, carrying and publishing* of an archived report. Those facts are not contradictory when represented as separate actual roles; they become contradictory only when a Profile says all must be the same physical producer or when it uses two `DataStream.system` values for one stream. The resulting Profile must name **one source-defensible output-bearing System** and separately preserve other participants, which could require a justified extension/carrier for per-result physical contribution.

**We do not declare a winner merely because the old server already stores one probe-owned stream per physical serial.** That live experiment was source preservation/readback under manually curated identifiers: two original NOAA means represented as two scalar Observations **plus two parallel archival-report Observations**. Its value lies in revealing exact gaps. It is **not** normative CSAPI stream ownership, correct original OMS Observer for a logger-averaged result, or complete source-to-UI interoperability.

### 31.5 Actual original standards boundaries that matter to a future generic client

1. **One producer, stable result properties/schema:** Part 2 §9.2.1 means a stream cannot blindly collect records with different property sets or schema versions under an unqualified identity. A grouped source `DataRecord` may contain multiple environmental properties, but *it does not create several sensor identities*. A source originally reporting one station mean must not become independently counted means just because a client chooses two record encodings.
2. **`observedProperties` is not a brochure inventory:** Part 2 §9.2.2 directs the server to derive observed properties, times and result type from linked Observations, with **null** where no observations exist. Reference-only sensor capabilities belong in source-backed descriptions and reported output definitions, not a synthetic actual historical stream populated with invented values.
3. **Separate source representation/report and runtime:** SensorML output can describe a **possible process output**. An actual CSAPI DataStream is a resource with a producer System, chosen source output, supported format/schema and linked Observations. The client can display a documented design capability without asserting `live=true`, completed observing activity or an authenticated physical unit.
4. **A stable stream can span an equipment change without losing physical provenance:** Station-led or logger-led `ATemp` summary candidate may persist through the EE181 serial swap, but the original per-result physical contributor is documented through a **separate date-based source join**; no built-in `DataStream.system` list encodes alternating sensors. **A single `deployment@link` across a probe change would be false**, particularly because Step 26 has not justified proper timestamps.
5. **ObservingProcedure vs product kind:** OMS §8.5 separates the actual used **method** from an individual sensor; Part 2 Observation has optional `procedure` link. Campbell's product manual and a sample CRBasic script do **not** prove that exact method was running at a given Bennett date. An identified unit model `Procedure` from Step 26 is not automatically the performed observing method.
6. **Original field definitions and subject meaning matter:** Campbell's source-relative humidity `fraction` differs from a reported percent, and two temperatures with °C might be ambient air vs humidity sensor interior vs logger panel. AirGradient raw electrode and index values are not validated gas concentration. In a later `resultSchema`, a correct `Quantity` unit without correct property/feature/source version is not truthful interpretation.
7. **Formats and quality/time still separate:** Part 2 §9.6 Req11 and the Part 2 JSON/SWE Common formats require truly matching schemas/encodings, not a MIME label only. **Step 29** must revisit Bennett's native `phenomenonTime`, unavailable `resultTime`, original `F_ATemp` and duplicate Observation representations; no choice made in Step 27 silently repairs them.

### 31.6 Negative conceptual/contract checks — source meaning must survive

| Test | Incorrect mapping to falsify | Grounded result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **T27-01** | Two Dragino environmental values require two physical SHT31s or two separately invented streams. | **REJECTED:** source has one external physical probe and two observed properties; Part 2 permits multi-property Observations. |
| **T27-02** | A LoRaWAN FPort, AirGradient physical PM socket, SDI-12 address, SDI-12 response position and IEEE 1451 TEDS TransducerChannel are universally equivalent. | **REJECTED:** different original layers, namespace and identity; IEEE is a technical donor, not manufacturer-implemented proof. |
| **T27-03** | A manufacturer manual/schema/packet example is evidence a real, identified unit performed an Observation. | **REJECTED:** A/C/D case standing remains reference/design/exemplary. |
| **T27-04** | AirGradient Wi-Fi, cellular and independently sourced cloud payloads have one validated producer, time, normalized units and resultSchema. | **REJECTED:** different source forms/revisions, no original joined installed runtime. |
| **T27-05** | ClimaVue `aR7!` 14 response fields mean 14 physical sensors or bus channels; logger `PanelTemp` can be displayed as ambient air. | **REJECTED:** response positions ≠ hardware; system/property referent differs. |
| **T27-06** | An EE181 must be the completed summary DataStream producer simply because it senses temperature. | **REJECTED as direct source proof:** CR1000X aggregates, original NOAA output is station summary, and per-result probe assignment is DERIVED. |
| **T27-07** | GOES, NOAA/CDMO or our 52°North API is automatically the original observing System because it reports data. | **REJECTED:** source source/processor/transmitter/archive roles distinct; copying ≠ original new observation. |
| **T27-08** | Set both station and logger in a single stream's `system@link` or omit `system` because both participate. | **REJECTED:** one required System producer; others need independent source-qualified association. |
| **T27-09** | A single physical-probe Deployment link qualifies the entire station-mean stream before and after probe swap. | **REJECTED:** multiple source service assertions, source day precision and still-open Part 1 Deployment encoding; JSON `deployment@link` only when shared across all members. |
| **T27-10** | Count scalar and archival-report CSAPI resources as four independent NOAA measurements. | **REJECTED:** two original source rows/measurement acts; the other two are parallel project-authored representations. |
| **T27-11** | Use one product `Procedure` to populate `DataStream.observedProperties` and `live=true` without actual linked observations. | **REJECTED:** observed properties are server-derived, null if none, and design support does not establish streaming state. |
| **T27-12** | `application/swe+json` response header alone proves SWE serialization and complete Bennett averaged-time/quality representation. | **REJECTED / OPEN:** prior tested 52°North body/schema mismatch and original unavailable `resultTime`; Step 29 not preempted. |

**Evidence classification:** these are **documentary/semantic negative tests performed on original-source relationships**, **NOT** tests that sent a device command, ran an IEEE 1451 tool, validated a live candidate GeoJSON/SensorML schema, or executed a shared Profile-driven client. **No source data changed.** The fact that both station-led and logger-led choices survive means **the producing-System question is a required later Profile decision**, not a passed mapping check.

### 31.7 Step 27 closure and exact handoff

**What changed:** [candidate Profile Step 27](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#step-27--interface-output-and-datastream-producing-system-binding-proposals) contains **P27-01–14** original-standard and source-qualified mapping proposals; this workbook contributes the direct standard provenance, four native cases, **H27-A/B competing producer options, H27-C/D rejected direct producer shortcuts, T27-01–12 negative tests, and release-critical decision guardrails**. The existing Step 26 System/Procedure/Deployment proposals remain in place and are **not silently promoted**. The source and original OGC/IEEE documents remain independently authoritative, not replaced by our synthesis.

**What did NOT change:** original NOAA/source sheets, number of actual Bennett readings, actual chosen deployed CSAPI stream producer, station/logging hardware, source-derived QA/time claims, observational TimeRange/SWE encoding, source-to-client runtime, app, server, Cloudflare, or conformance result. Neither a new OGC normative SHALL nor a fabricated actual Observation was created. **Gate E remains OPEN.**

**STEP 28 NEXT — on the owner's next “proceed” only:** propose source-qualified CSAPI **ControlStream, Command, CommandStatus, request/result/feasibility and SensorML settings/modes** mappings for **supported choices versus actual configuration**, available controls versus issued requests, correlated ACK/outcome versus verified physical effect, **security/authorization**, and separately recorded changes/lifecycle. Reuse the [Step 20 configuration](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-20--configuration-operating-state-and-condition), [Step 21 control](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-21--command-availability-issued-request-acknowledgement-outcome-and-evidence-of-effect) and [Step 23 cross-cutting](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-23--cross-cutting-whole-system-services-assertions-and-evidence-limitations) source records, with negative evidence checks. **Do not do Step 29 time/QA, Step 30 normative writing, Step 31 release contracts, app/server code or a live command in Step 28.**


## 32. Step 28 — Configuration, control, status, feasibility and lifecycle binding review

**Status: STEP 28 COMPLETE · 2 November 2026; STEP 29 NEXT; Phase E / Gate E OPEN.** The model-level source meanings are in [candidate DIM Step 20](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-20--configuration-operating-state-and-condition), [Step 21](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-21--command-availability-issued-request-acknowledgement-outcome-and-evidence-of-effect) and [Step 23](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-23--cross-cutting-whole-system-services-assertions-and-evidence-limitations). The new [candidate Profile Step 28 / P28-01–15](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#step-28--source-qualified-configuration-command-and-lifecycle-binding-proposals) proposes **actual original CSAPI Part 2/SensorML carriers**, without adopting them as requirements or creating/controlling a live resource. The current task covered supported setting vs effective unit state, declared available control vs actual request, correlated status/outcome vs independently verified effect, optional feasibility/authorization and documented service history. **Step 29 observation time/QA and Step 30 normative drafting were not performed.**

### 32.1 What the work proved at source scope

The simplest temptation is to put “Configuration” and “Commands” on a single Resource Details page and treat them as interchangeable. They are not. A manufacturer says what an instrument **could support**, a configured-instance description might assert a setting **selected or intended**, an actual source readback may show a particular unit **reporting what it was using**, and a command can **ask** the unit to change it. The receiving System can separately report **receipt/acceptance, execution, completion or failure**, while a later status/data reading may or may not independently support the intended physical outcome. These facts can be related, but no one stage automatically proves the next.

A functioning source-independent CSAPI client must also know whether an API lets it **read a channel's possible commands**, whether the API actually **allows creation of new Commands**, whether the particular user has permission, and whether a particular target/unit is **currently willing/able** to accept them. The original [CSAPI Part 2 §10 and §14.5](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) make the REST distinction explicit: **`/req/controlstream` read/discovery requirements** and the **separate `/req/create-replace-delete` command submission requirements** are not one guarantee. `ControlStream.live` is a reported channel-acceptance indicator, **not** user authentication or proof a requested physical act will succeed.

This is **actual standards review and documentary source alignment**, **not** execution of any manufacturer or API command, test of authorization or conformance to `/req/controlstream`. A source packet may have real historical maintenance without a recorded digital Command; a TypeScript test or published PDF example may have a Command-shaped message without proving the target performed it.

### 32.2 Original standards source register — what the actual approved clauses govern

| Original text / exact subsection | Source-native fact relevant to Step 28 | Boundary or ambiguity we must carry forward |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **OGC SensorML 3.0, OGC 23-000 v3.0, §8.8**, [approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-000/23-000.html) | A **configurable process** specifies supported parameter values, constraints, mode options and possible enabled inputs/outputs/components. A **configured process** inherits using `typeOf` and includes `configuration` with `Settings` (Req40); **Req39** applies two-or-more-mode requirement **if a modes list exists**; Req41–43 constrain the kind of parameter/array/constraint paths. | These are **SensorML configurable-process requirements**, not proof a source example was installed/as-applied or that every device must declare multiple modes. Original Req42/Req43 explanatory wording in the published HTML is not a license to invent a setting to fill a field. |
| **OGC API – Connected Systems Part 2, OGC 23-002 v1.0, §10.1–10.2/Tables 8–10**, [approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) | `/req/controlstream` class: `ControlStream` serves **one receiving System** and one controlled-property/parameter schema for all Commands. Attributes include `controlledProperties` (required), `live`, `async`, `formats`, channel `type=self/external`, `issueTime`, `executionTime`; association `system` exactly one. | A control's `live` does not grant authorization. `issueTime/executionTime` stream summaries are associated to **existing Commands**, not proof an action has been executed by our source product design. A desired setting is not automatically a control property. |
| **Part 2 §10.6 Req25/§16.1.7 Req100**, [approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) | GET format-qualified `.../controlstreams/{id}/schema?cmdFormat=...`, with one actual schema for supported command format. `application/json` command schema has `commandFormat` and `parametersSchema`, optional result/feasibility result schemas. | Manufacturer `AT+TDC`, `aAb!`, REST and SAPIENT Task are **different native commands**; there is no default cross-donor request shape, and no actual server/control schema validation in this step. |
| **Part 2 §10.7/Table 11, §10.9**, [approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) | A `Command` belongs to a single ControlStream, has `parameters`, server-reported `issueTime`, possibly `sender`, `currentStatus` and `executionTime`. `issueTime` is defined in Table 11 as **time received by the System**, while different code/schema comments sometimes call it issued. | Server receipt time ≠ user click time ≠ actual start or effect. The source's SAPIENT `task_id` is not inherently CSAPI local Command ID. The exact published route spelling varies across subsections; do not erase the discrepancy. |
| **Part 2 §10.11/Table 13–14**, [approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) | `CommandStatus` has required `reportTime` and `statusCode`, optional progress/time/message/result. Native codes **PENDING, ACCEPTED, REJECTED, SCHEDULED, UPDATED, CANCELED, EXECUTING, COMPLETED, FAILED** with source-defined lifecycle. `SCHEDULED` requires scheduled time; `COMPLETED` reports success with actual execution period. | Native status describes a **System-reported command stage**. Even `COMPLETED` is not automatically independent physical effect/calibration proof. A missing status is **UNKNOWN**, not evidence of pending; no default CSAPI status can be generated from a partial SAPIENT example. |
| **Part 2 §10.13/Tables 16–17, §16.1.10**, [approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) | `CommandResult` can return inline data or links to Observations, Observation sets, DataStreams or external data; status can report results in context. | Output/result of a command is not necessarily physical effect evidence, and the same observation cannot be counted anew merely because a Command references it. Published Table 13's `result` vs JSON `results` need explicit encoding qualification. |
| **Part 2 §11 `/req/feasibility`**, [approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) | A separate optional feasibility channel lets a target evaluate possibility, constraints, alternatives or estimated success **without sending the actual control command**. Requires `/req/controlstream`; feasibility CREATE has additional transactional requirements in §14.8. | A reported feasible outcome does not mean a real command was issued or target changed. No live feasibility call/return evidence is present. |
| **Part 2 §12/Tables 19–21**, [approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) | `SystemEvent` documents actual maintenance, part replacement, recalibration, software changes etc. Required **`type` URI** and **`eventTime`** with separate single System target. | Source standard's Table 20 example URIs contain **`TBD`**, not proven registered final codes; original Bennett events lack exact clock times for serialized-probe swaps; no native CSAPI Event resource was issued. |
| **Part 2 §14.4–14.8 / Requirements 68–77**, [approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) | **Create/Replace/Delete** is a distinct requirements class with **Req71 CREATE Command** at `{api_root}/controlstreams/{csId}/commands` and Req72 parent schema validation. Command cancellation is **not HTTP DELETE**: semantic `CANCELED` requires appropriate status update. | Read-only control discovery ≠ write support ≠ operator permission. No assertion that the 52°North target implements transaction, command authorization, feasibility or verified physical control from previous Bennett data GET tests. |
| **Part 2 §16.1 / Req99–106 JSON requirements**, [approved original](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html), [pinned OGC companion revision](https://github.com/opengeospatial/ogcapi-connected-systems/tree/fb858be23aa03e2b824ec50b5a97ac2c2a1d3fec/api/part2/openapi/schemas/json) | Inspected companion `controlStream.json`, `commandSchemaJson.json`, `command.json`, `commandStatus.json`, `commandStatusCode.json`, `commandResult.json`, `systemEvent.json` for exact keys. Schema requires e.g. `command@id`, `reportTime`, `statusCode` on status, with optional `results` array. | The GitHub revision is **2026-09-21 master**, not checked byte-for-byte against the OGC 2025 published immutable schema. Some approval text/JSON property names and endpoint singular/plural conventions diverge. Do not declare silent equivalences, an erratum or tested implementation support. |
| **BSI Flex 335 v2.0:2024-03 §§6.2.5, 6.3.2, 6.5.7–.9 and Tables 86–87**, [original 116-page PDF](https://www.bsigroup.com/siteassets/pdf/en/insights-and-media/insights/brochures/bsi-flex-335.pdf), [Dstl pinned TaskAck](https://github.com/dstl/SAPIENT-Proto-Files/blob/244a741c13bcee628f84944aef91e4a66854bd7d/bsi_flex_335_v2_0/task_ack.proto) | SAPIENT `Registration` declares possible modes/Tasks and `StatusReport` declares actual reported mode/state. Task and TaskAck statuses distinguish **ACCEPTED/REJECTED/COMPLETED/FAILED**. **Original BSI Table 86/87 pages were visually checked for this step**; `task_id` and `task_status` are mandatory fields in BSI Table 86. | **Native donor**, not a CSAPI wire mapping or proof that A/C/D/B manufacturer devices use SAPIENT. BSI example TaskAck omits status; Dstl static examples differ in ID/direction. Do not use normative SAPIENT obligations to fill missing printed source example data. |
| **Other original contributors** — [ISA/The Open Group SOSA study §16](#16-step-12--isa-60-and-the-open-group-sosa-physical-functional-visible-and-managed-relationships), [WoT/OMS/SSN/SAPIENT study §18](#18-step-14--sapient-v2-oms-30-w3cogc-sosa-ssn-and-web-of-things-observation-action-time-and-representation), [cross-cutting source review §27](#27-step-23--cross-cutting-whole-system-coverage-and-evidence-gaps) | ISA distinguishes property/observable/command declarations vs actual messages; Open Group SOSA separates **System Manager** from **Task Manager**; WoT ActionAffordance and intermediaries have their own access and proxy semantics, and SensorML `securityConstraints` is a document marking. | None creates mandatory universal control hierarchy, validates an installed SAPIENT/ISA/WoT client, supplies credentials or authorizes physical commands to the four cases. No donor “vote” or F-01–F-05 force-fitting. |

| --- | --- | --- |

No authentic installed node, actual chosen interval, command dispatch, status reply, effective 5V output, calibration result, authorization or current battery/health history. The 60000-ms command example is not the default 1200-sec setting; neither is a unit readback. |
| **AirGradient Open Air Max** — [A inventory A-C01–13, A-M01–04, A-L01–13](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/AirGradient_Open_Air_Max.md), [firmware Configuration.cpp at 84e479a](https://github.com/Open-Air-Foundation/firmware-openair-max/blob/84e479a415d81151e7a81aea9a2f7449a15e98cc/main/Configuration.cpp) | Product reference supports default cellular or optional Wi-Fi, settings portal with APN/credentials/destination, version-sensitive 0.7.0+ power-saving mode guide, parsed `co2CalibrationRequested` and `ledBarTestRequested` fields. Source guidance differs on interval between README and later guide. | **No installed monitor identity, confirmed active firmware edition or selected mode, actual completed calibration, LED test, device state or permission record**. A parsed `Requested` Boolean is not proof a physical act occurred. No joining Wi-Fi/cellular/cloud examples as one operating history. |

Separate **2026 publisher logger-only Status example** names `Battery`, `LithiumBattery`, `PanelTemp`, `WatchdogErrors` etc. | No actual joined station, physical unit serial from `SN-yyyyyyyy` pattern, active CRBasic program, address-change command or device reply, live condition, confirmed logger/sensor access. Different source publication editions and code samples are **not one device's active state**. |

Operator as-of 2024 is not current 2026 state, and original `F_ATemp` is result quality not equipment-health or a command outcome. No conforming timestamped `SystemEvent` was issued. |

**Data-standing comparison:** Each reference manufacturer case genuinely contributes syntax, available features, source defaults and native statuses at **SOURCE_FACT design** or **SOURCE_EXAMPLE** level. They do not supply **any matched actual request+ack+readback chain for an identified physical unit**. Bennett offers **actual operator history**, but not the API control chain or a complete effective-configuration snapshot. Inconsistent manual revisions, source QA values, project-derived physical probe attribution, and publisher sample status packets remain separately labeled. **This is not a failed model test; it is the correct experimental evidence ceiling.**

### 32.4 SAPIENT example falsification — visually checked original required fields versus printed data

**Original mandatory semantics:** BSI Flex 335 v2.0 **§6.5.7** says node responds with TaskAck containing accept/reject information; **§6.5.9 Table 86** requires `task_id` and **`task_status`**, with source-defined values in **Table 87**, including ACCEPTED, REJECTED, COMPLETED, FAILED. The original PDF **page 84 (zero-index 83)** and preceding task-protocol page were examined at original visual layout, not solely secondary summary. This standard's task statuses remain distinct from CSAPI statuses, and one source-native status cannot be transplanted without mapping.

**Published camera example:** the BSI **Annex B** informative task and acknowledgement at **10:29:39 and 10:33:04 UTC on 3 November 2022** are correlated by source `task_id=01GGYGM3F6FHSDDMVCQJ11ZNBF` and reversed camera/fusion roles, yet the **printed TaskAck omits `task_status`**. Therefore that source's **outcome is UNKNOWN**; it does *not* say ACCEPTED, REJECTED, PENDING or COMPLETED. Earlier RegistrationAck concerns registration, not this Task. [Prior original example trace](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#a-published-camera-exchange). The omission is an **informative example versus normative Table 86 discrepancy**, not source-reported success or permission to create a valid-looking `CommandStatus`.

**Dstl pinned v2 Test Harness mode-change templates** at commit `6a2999d70d761d224914ddd8fa3f663af5005be0`: the [request template](https://github.com/dstl/BSI-Flex-335-v2-Test-Harness/blob/6a2999d70d761d224914ddd8fa3f663af5005be0/SapientDmmSimulator/SapientDmmSimulator/Default.Task.Mode.json) requests mode `REQUEST` with task id ending `BY6`; the [static reply template](https://github.com/dstl/BSI-Flex-335-v2-Test-Harness/blob/6a2999d70d761d224914ddd8fa3f663af5005be0/SapientAsmSimulator/SapientAsmSimulator/Default.TaskAck.ModeChange.json) says ACCEPTED with task id ending `BY8` and unchanged source/destination direction. The developer's [TaskAckGenerator](https://github.com/dstl/BSI-Flex-335-v2-Test-Harness/blob/6a2999d70d761d224914ddd8fa3f663af5005be0/SapientAsmSimulator/SapientAsmSimulator/Common/TaskAckGenerator.cs) may rewrite IDs at runtime, but **no run occurred here**. **A response that accepts another task is not an accepted response for this request**. The shared mode word alone is insufficient; a current mode remains UNKNOWN without a real associated status report at the relevant time.

**Still-open source-edition discrepancies** ([workbook §18](#18-step-14--sapient-v2-oms-30-w3cogc-sosa-ssn-and-web-of-things-observation-action-time-and-representation)): BSI Table 15 `config_data` tag 9 vs pinned Protobuf tag 10, and BSI wrapper content-time definition vs Protobuf sent-time comment. Future converters must distinguish source editions and native timestamps rather than retroactively select a presumed winner. No inferred installed hardware or SAPIENT system is inserted into the four-case source corpus.

### 32.5 Conditional field-level control example — documentation versus an actual action

**Dragino S31-LB, documentary reference only.** One manufacturer-specified supported command is `AT+TDC` for setting a transmit interval; the manufacturer documents a **20-minute default** (1200 seconds), while a printed example `AT+TDC=60000` expresses an interval in **milliseconds** (60 seconds). If a future, individually identified **real node** and genuine CSAPI control implementation were independently established, **one candidate** source→DIM→Part 2 mapping could use:

| Evidence/purpose | Native candidate carrier | What an unknown client may claim only if supported |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Documented 20-min default + supported command/parameter | Manufacturer type-level SensorML `Procedure` parameter/capability description | “Model supports setting uplink interval; documented default 1200 s.” No installed state or physical command exists. |
| Identified actual node and format/schema/permissions established *later* | CSAPI `ControlStream.system@link` to verified unit, `controlledProperties`, `inputName` if source output/input named, `schema?cmdFormat=application/json` returning `parametersSchema` | “A recognized control channel/schema exists for this target; actual write and per-user authorization must be checked.” Cannot claim available now from documentation. |
| Actual verified user request *if ever issued later* | `Command.parameters` holding source-declared interval with explicit units, server `issueTime`, sender/Command ID | “This command was actually requested at the recorded time.” Printed AT commands are not this transaction. |
| Native response and any subsequent evidence *if captured* | `CommandStatus.command@id`, `reportTime`, `statusCode`, optional `CommandResult`; separate device status/readback | “The receiver reported ACCEPTED/COMPLETED/etc.” Independent evidence of a physical effective 60-second interval would be an additional claim. |

**No example CSAPI resources were generated in this step** because any actual target URI, command ID, status/report times, access-control state or reported applied value would be fictional for our reference-only Dragino case. A hypothetical payload is **not needed** to prove the doctrinal separation. Nor may a model-level SensorML parameter default be encoded as actual `DataStream.observedProperties` without observations.

### 32.6 T28 negative contract checks — outcomes at source level

These checks were performed by comparing **original documentary source meaning**, published OGC requirements/companion JSON and previously pinned SAPIENT examples. They are **NOT** actual REST API/firmware/security/runtime tests.

| ID | Tempting but false conversion | Assessment |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **T28-01** | Product default `20 min` or manual `AT+TDC=60000` is an actual unit's active interval. | **REJECTED:** design source/default vs example vs no verified unit state. |
| **T28-02** | All devices must have two modes because SensorML Req39 mentions “two or more.” | **REJECTED:** constraint on a present `modes` list, not mandate for a one-mode model to invent settings. |
| **T28-03** | Readable `ControlStream` and GET schema mean the requesting user can POST and is authorized to move hardware. | **REJECTED:** separate `/req/create-replace-delete` Req71–72, authenticated privilege and target evidence. |
| **T28-04** | `ControlStream.live=true` means unit is healthy, authorized and operation guaranteed to succeed. | **REJECTED:** reports whether control stream accepts commands at that time; not permission or result. |
| **T28-05** | Printed AT command `OK` or source-declared setting selection proves completion and effective state. | **REJECTED:** manual example ≠ execution, accepted request ≠ applied readback/physical effect. |
| **T28-06** | BSI original camera TaskAck with missing mandatory `task_status` is an ACCEPTED or PENDING CSAPI command status. | **REJECTED:** original reply correlated, native outcome UNKNOWN; no source statusCode invented. |
| **T28-07** | Dstl mode request ID `BY6` and ACK ID `BY8` with unflipped sender/recipient are a successful matched exchange. | **REJECTED:** wrong request/ACK correlation despite printed ACCEPTED in independent file. |
| **T28-08** | CSAPI `CommandStatus=COMPLETED` automatically proves independently observed physical actuation or device calibration. | **REJECTED:** source-reported command completion ≠ separate effect evidence; causal attribution would need its own readback/source. |
| **T28-09** | Campbell logger example `PanelTemp` or AirGradient cloud `offline` is a real, current physical condition for another identified target. | **REJECTED:** original sample/subject/scope and time required; source examples not matched field history. |
| **T28-10** | Operator Bennett 16 Apr maintenance and 17 Apr logger OS update are received CSAPI commands, precise event timestamps or a successful live firmware update via our API. | **REJECTED / EVENT HOLD:** actual historical operator servicing differs from a command exchange or a machine-readable `SystemEvent` with supported exact time/type. |
| **T28-11** | Feasibility or linked CommandResult is a guarantee the request was issued and resulted in verified effect. | **REJECTED:** feasibility, issued command, source status, result data and physical effect are different claims. |
| **T28-12** | Prior historical Bennett data GET/HTTP 200 proves a Part 2 control transaction and security model or production support for all encoding classes. | **REJECTED:** no original Command creation, status/result, feasibility, authorization or conformance test in that bounded data demonstration. |

**Interpretation:** These are source-supported rejection decisions, not a nine-or-fifteen-class domain ontology and not vendor pass/fail conformance. A client can still truthfully show that a model has available controls, even when **every actual-unit execution/effective state is UNKNOWN**. The separate Step 26 physical Deployment and Step 27 producer/observation ambiguities must remain visible rather than “fixed” by a command or SystemEvent.

**Completed in one prompt:** inspected original OGC Part 2 §§10–12, 14.5–14.8 and 16.1 including published distinct transaction requirements and nine status codes; SensorML 3.0 §8.8/Req39–43; BSI SAPIENT original §§6.5.7–.9 and visually confirmed Tables 86–87, previously pinned Dstl Task/TaskAck test fixtures; rechecked Dragino/AirGradient/Campbell source sheets and actual Bennett operator service facts. Recorded [P28-01–15 source→DIM→OGC proposals](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#step-28--source-qualified-configuration-command-and-lifecycle-binding-proposals), this direct original-source register, four-case examples, [T28-01–12](#326-t28-negative-contract-checks--outcomes-at-source-level), and the explicit authorization, format, endpoint, missing-source, timestamp and event-type limitations. **No source PDF/CSV, original case manifest, candidate DIM, live application, server or deployment was changed.**

**Not established:** any verified target's actual configurable running state, completed SAPIENT/CSAPI command, live write permissions, actual device readback/physical effect, trustworthy new `CommandStatus` or `SystemEvent`, conformance, or a finished Profile. **Gate E remains OPEN** and the existing older candidate Profile language remains subordinate to these newer, qualified Step 28 proposals; neither is adopted normative text.

**On the owner's next “proceed,” STEP 29 ONLY:** review/decide available **native Observation time, `phenomenonTime` / `resultTime`, original `F_ATemp` QA, source-to-produced-result provenance and the 52°North JSON/SWE JSON format limitations**, using original OGC OMS 3.0, CSAPI Part 2, SWE Common 3.0 and the frozen Bennett actual source packet. Do not execute Step 30 Profile SHALL consolidation, Step 31 contract testing, Glaux code changes, create any CSAPI server resources or trigger a deployment.


## 33. Step 29 — Observation time, quality and provenance binding decision

**2 November 2026 · STEP 29 COMPLETE; STEP 30 NEXT; Gate E OPEN.** This source/standards/implementation comparison supports [candidate Profile Step 29](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#step-29--source-faithful-observation-time-quality-and-provenance-binding-decision), which records **12 P29 candidate decisions, five option dispositions and 14 T29 negative controls**. The decision is **CONDITIONAL / FULL BENNETT NATIVE OMS-CSAPI OBSERVATION FIDELITY ON HOLD**. It does not rewrite an earlier source packet, declare OGC errata, select Bennett's final producing `System`, run the old offline SWE experiment, make new HTTP calls, add normative Profile SHALLs or change production/client code.

### 33.1 Why this matters to the whole-System goal

Our intended generic client needs to tell a real sensor System's actual evidence from a manufacturer's possible output, and to report a measured result without changing what it means. The Bennett source is unusually valuable because it contains **an actual historical station and two actual NOAA/CDMO station means** separated by a documented physical-probe replacement. But its original archive **does not provide the exact result-availability time or a native per-measurement physical probe serial**, and its station timestamp denotes the **end of an interval mean**, not a direct instantaneous sample.

Thus simply placing the mean inside a valid-looking `Observation.result` does not preserve **what period was averaged, when the result became available, what the source QC meant, who originally observed/processed it and whether the observation is a new act or another digital representation**. Those are precisely the cross-vendor whole-System distinctions the DIM was created to protect. A profile-aware client must be able to say **unknown** or **derived** without coercing the source to satisfy a JSON property.

### 33.2 Original field/time/quality trace — no replacement source invented

**Grounded 31 Oct original snapshot**: [Bennett evidence JSON](../Candidate_Specification/examples/bennetts-point-ee181.json) retains two original NOAA/CDMO 2024 CSV selections (5,260,824 bytes, SHA-256 `3f3ee3e5458c00adee770dc9b18a6246e590937a3f0c6c5f48cfc73cd8ae4950`); **that source CSV was not freshly downloaded 2 Nov**. The original [CDMO SWMP parameter reference](https://www.nerrsdata.org/data/parameters.cfm) states meteorological `ATemp` is an **average of air temperature** in °C, `Frequency` 15 minutes, and the summarized period is **prior to** each reported timestamp. The [CDMO QC policy](https://nerrsdata.org/data/qaqc.cfm) states `F_param=0` means **passed initial QA/QC only**; `-3` means rejected, and flags are parameter-specific. The [operator's 2024 ACE Basin metadata](https://cdmo.baruch.sc.edu/waf/YearlyFiles/ACE%20Basin/meteorological/metadata/acemet01-12.24m.pdf), previously read/attributed in workbook §§12, 23 and 26, states **fixed EST (UTC−05:00) year-round**, 5-second inputs, fifteen-minute station averages and **two individual EE181 use histories**. **No new PDF visual inspection or source download was performed in Step 29.**

| Retained field | CSV line 10130 | CSV line 10418 | Claim/evidence standing |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `StationCode` | `"acebpmet  "` | `"acebpmet  "` | SOURCE_FACT **with two original trailing spaces**. This is station code, not probe serial. |
| `DatetimeStamp` | `"04/15/2024 12:00"` | `"04/18/2024 12:00"` | SOURCE_FACT **station local fixed EST**, not independently result availability. |
| `Frequency` | `"15"` | `"15"` | SOURCE_FACT **15-minute** averaging/report context. |
| `ATemp` / unit | `"29.1"` / °C | `"27.3"` / °C | SOURCE_FACT original selected historical station **air mean**, not logger or sensor internal temperature. |
| `F_ATemp` | `"<0> "` | `"<0> "` | SOURCE_FACT original parameter-specific QC **with one trailing space**: passed initial checks only. |
| `F_Record` | `""` | `""` | SOURCE_FACT **independently blank** record-wide flag; not substitute for `F_ATemp`. |
| Converted stamp | `2024-04-15T17:00:00Z` | `2024-04-18T17:00:00Z` | DERIVED fixed EST → UTC using source time basis. |
| Nominal averaging interval | `16:45:00–17:00:00 UTC`, 15 Apr | `16:45:00–17:00:00 UTC`, 18 Apr | DERIVED preceding 15 minutes, **not independently recorded sample-window endpoints**. |
| Likely physical EE181 contributor | serial `222016000109CE` | serial `21281600077814` | DERIVED by station + air-temperature role + date-qualified original operator equipment-use history. **Not in original CSV**. |
| Original `resultTime` | UNKNOWN | UNKNOWN | Neither CSV nor selected operator metadata establishes instant result **became available**. |
| Source measurement identity | NOAA/CDMO source CSV line **10130** | NOAA/CDMO source CSV line **10418** | TWO original selected measurements; four earlier server Observations are **copies/alternative representations**. |

**Related negative evidence:** the 17 April 2024 12:00 EST original CSV line **10322** has `F_ATemp="<-3> [GPR] (CSM)"`, rejected during the logger operating-system update. A numeric original `ATemp` in that row is **not** a third normally accepted result. The actual 16 April broad 09:45–13:30 EST maintenance period did not timestamp exact EE181 exchange, and the 31 Dec 2024 “still in use as of” is not a current/2026 station status. These facts stay separate from per-measurement phenomenon time, QC, unit service and availability.

### 33.3 Approved original standards: precisely where conformity ends

| Approved OGC source (not our choice) | Actual native authority checked against source | What does not follow |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **OMS 3.0 OGC 20-082r4 §§8.2.3–.4** ([approved HTML](https://docs.ogc.org/as/20-082r4/20-082r4.html), [original PDF preservation](../Primary_Sources/OGC_20-082r4_OMS_3.0.0.md)) | `phenomenonTime:TM_Object` **exactly one**; Note 2: average of observations from different times applies to the **sampled interval**. `resultTime:TM_Object` **exactly one**, instant when result **became available**; postprocessed availability can differ from environmental phenomenon. | A period-ending source stamp is not itself both fields; an API server copy time, NOAA archive revision, or plausible GOES slot is not independent original result availability. |
| **OMS 3.0 §9.2.4–.5 and §§8.2.6–.10** | Source-incident `parameter` relevant to actual activity, instance-specific `resultQuality`, separately identified observed feature/property, result, method and Observer. `resultQuality` is a conceptual OMS property whose original semantics differ from universal equipment health. | `F_ATemp` initial QC does not mean instrument calibrated/healthy, all station fields passed QC, or physical EE181 directly identified as final Observer in each row. |
| **CSAPI Part 2 OGC 23-002 §9.7 Table 6, §16.1 Req96–98** ([approved HTML](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html)) | Part 2 Observation information-model Table 6 lists both `phenomenonTime` and `resultTime` DateTime required. For `application/json`, [companion `observation.json` at pinned 2026-09-21 OGC repository](https://github.com/opengeospatial/ogcapi-connected-systems/blob/fb858be23aa03e2b824ec50b5a97ac2c2a1d3fec/api/part2/openapi/schemas/json/observation.json) uses **TimeInstant** for each, requires `resultTime`, and documents `phenomenonTime` default if omitted; format schema may declare `resultSchema` and optional `parametersSchema`. | The companion's omitted-phenomenon default is not authorization to mislabel an interval-average as an instant or manufacture a missing original resultTime. Document the official **Table 6 / encoding-specific default distinction**, without declaring a correction/erratum. This mutable companion branch was **not byte-verified as the exact immutable 2025 release schema**. |
| **CSAPI Part 2 §16.2 Req107–111** | Real `application/swe+json` requests return true SWE JSON observations under **`obsFormat`, `recordSchema`, `JSONEncoding`**; Req110: at least one `Time` component with `phenomenonTime` or `resultTime` definition URI; Req111 payload matches schema. | A source-stated end stamp mapped as `SamplingTime` plus a private `TimeRange` field **does not automatically make that `TimeRange` the normative phenomenonTime**. A correct format does not provide the still-missing original resultTime. |
| **SWE Common 3.0 OGC 24-014 §8.2.14/§9.1.14/§10.2.2–3** ([approved HTML](https://docs.ogc.org/is/24-014/24-014.html)) | `TimeRange` is a scalar range of two time values. JSON encoding of `TimeRange` is a two-value array; `DataRecord` can carry separate typed `Time`, `TimeRange`, `Quantity` and `Text` fields. | A legal SWE range component is not by itself an approved CSAPI mapping into the standard `Observation.phenomenonTime` field. A `DataRecord` field label/URN may preserve original source context without standard-level equality. |
| **SWE 3.0 §7.4.1, §8.2.3 and §8.2.15** | Native **simple/dynamic quality can apply only to the individual scalar**. Optional simple-component `quality` permits Quantity/Category/Text etc; type `Text` can preserve an original flag or a comment. | The prior fixture's `qc` side field does not automatically prove a **native quality attribute association** or `OMS.resultQuality` mapping. Need exact candidate schema, relation/edition agreement and real format round-trip test before a Profile requirement. |
| **CSAPI Part 2 §9.2 single producer DataStream rule; earlier candidate Step27** | One stream has one source-defined producing `System` and common result properties/schema; per-record physical contributor and downstream publishers are distinct possible provenance claims. | An old **probe-owned** live stream is not a normative producer selection for a logger-aggregated station mean. H27-A station/H27-B logger remain competing sources; this time/QA review does not decide them. |

### 33.4 Five real alternatives, not interchangeable promises

| Option | Evidence/standard support | Honest preservation/conformance consequence | Step 29 disposition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **A. Prior scalar `application/json`** | Earlier 52°North readback stored source value in ordinary JSON with scalar `phenomenonTime/resultTime`. | Point timestamp and original value, **not** full interval; source `F_ATemp` and distinct physical attribution not native scalar. `resultTime` used original station stamp as **disclosed surrogate**, not known source availability. | **Retain historical baseline, reject as fully source-faithful.** Do not modify. |
| **B. Parallel archival-report JSON `DataRecord`** | Earlier scalar + extra archival-report copies (four test Observation resources for two source measurements); extra records retain nominal window, `F_ATemp` and DERIVED probe. | Better original content **preservation**, but window is **nested project context** not native outer `phenomenonTime`; original resultTime still surrogate, duplicate copy/producer semantics unresolved. | **Retain limited historical bridge, not a final Profile binding or justification to duplicate more.** |
| **C. Native JSON `parametersSchema` / `parameters`** | Approved Part 2 supports optional schema and parameters, including source-defined quality/context. Tested 52°North parser did **not preserve `parameters`**. | Could carry original QC/window after repair **but** cannot turn JSON top-level TimeInstant into true averaged interval or create original resultTime. | **Conditional context option; not selected for full fidelity**. |
| **D. Proper SWE `application/swe+json`** | Req109–111 permit a declared SWE `Time` and more typed fields; [offline Bennett fixture](../Candidate_Specification/examples/bennetts-point-wire/example.json) has `stamp`, `window` (`TimeRange`), `air` (`Quantity`), `qc` (`Text`), optional `probe` (`Text`). [Offline reader/check](../Candidate_Specification/examples/bennetts-point-wire/README.md) previously demonstrated two original rows plus five deliberate altered inputs. | Proper per-format server format/schema/payload **unverified**; `TimeRange` not established as normative `phenomenonTime`, original resultTime absent, and project `probe` field is DERIVED not original. Offline check was a custom source comparator, **not a standards conformance run**. | **Best future conditional format experiment**, but no Step 29 implementation and no final binding claim. |
| **E. Full OMS result** | OMS requires interval phenomenonTime and instant result availability; SWE can represent range values; later CSAPI interpretation may be clarified. | **Historical result availability is absent** even if a server is upgraded; needs independent appropriate source evidence and verified standard/implementation mapping. | **Release goal BLOCKED at original evidence and standards binding levels.** |

**Decisive result:** **more powerful encoding cannot create missing history**. Separate (1) `resultTime` **SOURCE-EVIDENCE BLOCKER**, (2) interval average `phenomenonTime` **STANDARD-MAPPING GAP**, (3) SWE quality link/OM resultQuality **SCHEMA/SEMANTIC TEST GAP**, (4) current 52°North `parameters/SWE` **IMPLEMENTATION GAP**, (5) old dual-stream count/producer **PROVENANCE AND PRODUCER GAP**. No one bug or field workaround fixes them all.

### 33.5 Earlier 52°North observations — explicitly historical, not freshly retested

The [31 October decision document](../Candidate_Specification/Observation_Encoding_Decision_31OCT2026.md) contains six documented read-only HTTP probes. The `application/json` Observation path returned **200 with an ordinary JSON observation**; a request for `application/swe+json` returned **200 and a SWE MIME header, but still ordinary JSON Observation body**. The `schema?obsFormat=application/swe+json` path returned `obsFormat=application/json` + `resultSchema`, rather than SWE `recordSchema` + `JSONEncoding`. A shorthand `f=swe+json` request gave HTTP 400. Earlier `GET /conformance` exposed only Common core. These results **do not prove actual SWE JSON support**; they do not prove permanent absence of every feature. The deployed instance has documented local adaptations.

Original upstream code at pinned [52°North parser 6ad75aa7](https://github.com/52North/connected-systems-pygeoapi/blob/6ad75aa7fbfc76517c8a24c21446471845007855/connected-systems-api/provider/part2/formats/om_json_scalar.py) uses `datetime.fromisoformat` for `resultTime` and `phenomenonTime`, and the parser does not retain `parameters`; the pinned [provider](https://github.com/52North/connected-systems-pygeoapi/blob/6ad75aa7fbfc76517c8a24c21446471845007855/connected-systems-api/provider/part2/part2.py) stores instantaneous timestamp columns for those fields. A column named `parameters` does **not** mean the inspected normal JSON parser successfully round-tripped it. The pinned code is **not proven byte identical to local deployed modifications**, nor were new endpoint calls made today.

The earlier offline [Bennett SWE JSON cache/reader](../Candidate_Specification/examples/bennetts-point-wire/README.md) is likewise an executed **historical semantic preservation check**, not a full OGC schema-validation or live write/read conformance. Neither previous test turned its `TimeRange` into normative Observation `phenomenonTime` or discovered original resultTime.

| **T29-05** | Extra SWE `TimeRange` automatically equals CSAPI `phenomenonTime` despite Req110 `Time`. **REJECTED/OPEN**: preserving nominal period does not establish mapped normative field. |
| **T29-06** | SWE response media header means the body/schema are SWE JSON. **REJECTED:** prior read-only mismatch proved otherwise for tested service. |
| **T29-07** | `F_ATemp="<0> "` should be trimmed, numeric 0, complete station health or calibration certification. **REJECTED:** source token, spaces and limited initial-parameter QA. |
| **T29-08** | Blank `F_Record` or other parameter flag equals `F_ATemp`. **REJECTED:** source distinct. |
| **T29-09** | Source-defined `qc` `Text` is automatically SWE per-component quality/OMS `resultQuality`. **REJECTED/OPEN** pending explicit valid linkage/schema. |

| **T29-13** | 17 Apr `F_ATemp="<-3> [GPR] (CSM)"` numeric value passes initial QC. **REJECTED:** source rejected measurement. |
| **T29-14** | Station coordinates or day-qualified probe service defines exact probe Deployment geometry/timestamps. **REJECTED:** date/role/site scope is different. |

**All checks are documentary comparisons, NOT a newly run schema or runtime validation.** The imported earlier sample `F_Record` remains blank separately; no original source bytes, software or historical result data were modified.

## 34. Step 30 — Integrated candidate CSAPI Profile and Gate E handoff

**2 November 2026 · STEP 30 COMPLETE; STEP 31 NEXT; GATE E OPEN.** The deliverable is **one coherent candidate application Profile** at the [front of the existing candidate Profile manuscript, Step 30](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#step-30--integrated-candidate-profile-source-qualified-whole-system-interpretation-through-csapi), with **21 PR30 proposed rules**, original-source anchors and examples **for each**, producer/client interpretation, all four source cases and **nine G30 unresolved obligations**. This workbook entry records what was integrated and checks its boundaries; it does not execute Step 31 contract tests, implement the Profile or claim OGC adoption. Its detailed input studies remain [Step 25 §29](#29-step-25--original-csapi-and-ogc-standard-requirements-evidence-qualified-profile-binding-inventory), [Step 26 §30](#30-step-26--identity-kind-composition-host-and-deployment-binding-proposals), [Step 27 §31](#31-step-27--interface-output-datastream-and-producing-system-binding-review), [Step 28 §32](#32-step-28--configuration-control-status-feasibility-and-lifecycle-binding-review), [Step 29 §33](#33-step-29--observation-time-quality-and-provenance-binding-decision), and the [integrated DIM Step 24](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#step-24--integrated-source-bounded-whole-system-dim-and-relationship-falsification).

### 34.1 What the unified draft is for, in plain language

Our intended client should be able to explain different sensing Systems **without being taught manufacturer-specific interpretations for each**. It should identify what physical equipment is documented, what is connected or installed, which functions and interfaces are supported, what produced which reported data, and whether a statement describes design, an actual historical event, a derived association, or missing information. It should not silently transform a product datasheet into live equipment, a response field into a physical sensor, an ACK into a physical effect, or an archive copy into a new Observation.

The new integrated draft is **not** a domain name or root taxonomy imposed ahead of the evidence. It begins with the **source-attested focal subject/arrangement** from qualified Gate C/D, then describes *only evidenced relations* using suitable published OGC structures. The intended first success is **descriptive, source-qualified interoperability** across unlike systems. Claims of complete OMS observation semantics, real command/control interoperability, and universal deployed-unit data remain conditional or excluded.

**Draft behavior statuses:** **R** = proposed required when the applicable subject/resource is claimed under this candidate Profile, **C** = conditional on source evidence plus claimed OGC class/format, **O** = optional additional source-supported description, **H** = held or excluded where facts or interoperable representation are unresolved. These are **proposed project semantics**, not newly adopted normative OGC SHALLs. Requirements already in approved CSAPI/ SensorML/SWE/OMS apply to relevant *claimed original requirements classes*; a descriptive-only producer does not inherit every Part 2 command/encoding obligation.
