### 34.4 The nine explicit unfinished conditions for a testable Profile

The integrated candidate [G30-01–09 gap register](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#6-unfulfilled-obligations-conditional-release-scope-and-gate-e-dependencies) contains the following independently consequential issues. They should **not** be collectively dismissed as “more research needed.”

- **G30-01:** discoverable machine-readable provenance/source/DERIVED/UNKNOWN and identical-original-event representation association (a paper ledger does not make this portable).
- **G30-02:** single station-vs-CR1000X **DataStream.system** producer decision for the original averaged report, without treating input probe or publisher as automatically equivalent.
- **G30-03:** Bennett's original calendar-day-only probe service versus date-time **Deployment.validTime** encoding and 2024 as-of bound.
- **G30-04:** original historical **resultTime is UNKNOWN**; can't produce a fully evidenced mandatory result availability field for the selected original NOAA records.
- **G30-05:** source average's interval-valued **phenomenonTime** versus CSAPI format mappings; extra SWE `TimeRange` has not been shown to be native Observation phenomenonTime.
- **G30-06:** native, actually validated parameter-specific SWE quality association / OMS `resultQuality` from original `F_ATemp`; a source field in a result isn't automatically mapped quality.
- **G30-07:** real 52°North format/schema and source-fidelity support; 31 October SWE media response was conventional JSON body/schema despite MIME request.
- **G30-08:** same genuinely generic interpreter tested against at least two unlike source arrangements *at their actual evidence scope*, with original source-to-display comparison.
- **G30-09:** verified authenticated permission, current configuration and request/ack/status/physical-effect evidence if the Profile ever claims executable management.

**Potential release posture:** only a **properly scoped descriptive/reference candidate** may be supportable with current case evidence; full observational/temporal and command interoperability cannot be promised. A future requirement may be narrowed or a gap made an explicit exclusion rather than filled with unsupported values.

## 35. Step 31 — Candidate Profile contract scenarios, falsification and Gate E decision

**2 November 2026 · STEP 31 COMPLETE; STEP 32 NEXT ONLY AS GATE-REPAIR INPUT-CONTRACT WORK; GATE E REVIEW COMPLETE BUT NOT CLOSED FOR PORTABLE INTEROPERABILITY.** The principal test design and complete 21-pair contract coverage are in [Candidate Profile Step 31](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#step-31--contract-challenges-source-oracle-tests-and-bounded-gate-e-judgment). [Step 30's 21 PR30 rule index](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#step-30--integrated-candidate-profile-source-qualified-whole-system-interpretation-through-csapi) is the candidate under challenge; [Step 24's integrated DIM R24](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md#r24-relationship-register--one-connected-model-individually-testable-meanings) defines the source meanings. This is the intended **Phase E contract/release-gate review**, not Phase F implementation or an OGC conformance audit. **No actual new CSAPI System/Procedure/DataStream/Observation/Command/Deployment was written**. No Glaux code or Cloudflare/Oracle service was touched.

### 35.1 The result in ordinary language

We can write sensible rules for a generic client to distinguish a **manufacturer product design** from a **real physical unit**, a **physical part** from an **output field**, a **permitted command** from an **executed action**, and an **actual measurement** from a **second representation of the same original event**. The published CSAPI Part 1/Part 2, Sensor Model Language (SensorML) 3.0 and SWE Common 3.0 provide many correct original resources and fields when their **applicable conformance classes and required source facts are actually satisfied**. Our four original case descriptions contain enough evidence to **falsify many misleading source-to-model equivalences**.

However, our current Profile is not yet a portable **producer-to-unknown-client** contract for the most distinctive source-truth qualifiers. Our manuscript tells a human that an individual equipment association is **DERIVED**, a 2024 state is **as-of**, a manufacturer packet is a **SOURCE_EXAMPLE**, a 15-minute result's original **resultTime is UNKNOWN**, and two copies belong to **one source event**. Those labels appear in **our research ledger**, not in an established, deployed, discoverable generic CSAPI evidence association. If the client must learn those facts from a special Bennett's Point parser or a manually authored UI, we have **not achieved the user's goal**, regardless of how well the diagram reads.

**Gate conclusion:** Step 31 provides positive/negative **contract oracles** and checks a small existing source fixture, but **Gate E is NOT CLOSED for source-independent Profile interoperability**. A bounded descriptive candidate remains viable for further source-packet/encoding work. Full historical Observation interoperability, exact-time Deployment and executable command control are explicitly held/excluded; their gaps are not filled by declaring a qualified success. The next numbered work item, **Step 32**, is permitted **only as the planned repair/test preparation** for this open gate, not as proof of approval.

### 35.4 Higher-risk source-negative contracts: how a real client could fail

The full [21 C31 scenarios](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#c31-source-positive-and-mutation-negative-contracts--coverage-of-every-pr30-rule) each name source-positive input/expectation, a deliberately false mutation and an honest documentary/held/untested status. For later Phase F testing, the **accept/reject oracles** most relevant to real user harm or misleading technical claims are these:

| Scenario IDs | Controlled input context | Expected generic client decision vs failure to reject |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **C31-01/02/03** | Product-kind Dragino/AirGradient/Campbell docs vs actual Bennett station/two serials, source system identifiers and kind links. | Reference-only shows **kind/design**, not an installed unit. Actual Bennett's returning probe retains its unique physical identity. Do **not** invent serial/UID or call project-authored model Procedure manufacturer-issued. |
| **C31-04/05/06** | External cabled sensor, separately built logger/sensor, Bennett returning probe and date-only service, source station point. | Show **connection versus constituent versus Host/time-scoped service**; preserve original day granularity/as-of; no `validTime` midnight/time guesses or per-probe site-point copied location. |
| **C31-07/08/09** | S31-LB one probe/two properties, AirGradient PM1/PM2 sockets vs particulate keys, Campbell one bus address/14 response values/logger PanelTemp. | Hardware identity, native interface/channel, source report field and environmental medium are independent. **Reject** 14 invented physical sensors or presenting logger panel temperature as ambient air. |
| **C31-10/11/12** | Bennett original station mean and separate logger/probe/relay; manufacturer schema/example output; Part 2 DataStream one producing System and common properties/schema. | Do **not** assume original source mean is directly probe-produced, merge cloud/example schema families into one actual stream, or portray manufacturer test bytes as actual source observations. |
| **C31-13/14/15** | Two 2024 source air-temperature 15-minute averages with derived window, original raw `F_ATemp` (trailing space), source UNK original resultTime, two source rows versus four old server Obs. | **Reject** instant-only mean, fictional result availability, stripped QA, false instrument-health inference and duplicate original measurement count. A complete native OMS/CSAPI claim remains **HOLD**. |
| **C31-16/17/18/19** | Dragino `AT+TDC` default versus example, AirGradient possible calibration, Campbell default bus address, BSI camera TaskAck missing status, Dstl ACK with mismatched task ID, Bennett actual maintenance. | Supported ≠ as-applied; command availability ≠ authorized CREATE; correlated native Task status ≠ effect; source operator maintenance ≠ digital command or exact-time SystemEvent. |
| **C31-20/21** | Model battery/communications/software/security statements; generic client expected to see original source/evidence labels, source representations and unknown state. | Source-defined nameplate/firmware ≠ live condition. **The client may only claim this generically when the evidence labels/links are actually supplied in a portable machine-readable source packet**, not hard-coded by manufacturer or document title. |

**No false positive was declared “passed” merely because the requirement could be written in a paper.** Step 31 shows the negative conditions are clear enough to turn into test cases, while several positive runtime outcomes remain deliberately unimplemented and source-incomplete. At least **C31-01/15/21** cannot honestly be marked portable until provenance/evidence standing has a discoverable carrier that the unchanged client reads.

### 35.5 Gate E decision: meaningful progress, no premature interoperability pass

The [controlling Gate E criterion](../PLAN_DIM_Profile_V2_Whole_System_Recovery_to_Delivery_v0.1_31OCT2026.md) demands a Profile saying what **the same unknown client** can recognize without vendor-specific knowledge. We can state candidate outcomes and correct native semantics, but we **cannot yet transmit and consume every essential source-specific evidence qualification through one demonstrated contract**. The following source gaps have been preserved rather than silently “fixed”:

| G30 original gap | Gate E disposition after actual Step 31 contract check |
| --- | --- |
| **G30-01 portable provenance/uncertainty** | **RELEASE BLOCKER even for descriptive full interoperability.** Offline ledger and bespoke fixture `urn:example:` evidence kinds are not a discoverable, verified generic wire contract. |
| **G30-02 Bennett source producer** | **HOLD native completed station mean stream.** Station or CR1000X logger remain alternatives; EE181 is the physical input, not a source-certified complete-mean producer. |
| **G30-03 historical Deployment time** | **HOLD precise Part 1 Deployment.** Original day-level use and 2024 as-of are source facts, not evidence of minute/instant, station/probe coordinates or ongoing `now`. |
| **G30-04 original result availability** | **SOURCE BLOCKER** for full native historical OMS Observation; original `resultTime` is UNKNOWN. |
| **G30-05 averaged phenomenon interval mapping** | **STANDARD/ENCODING HOLD** for faithful native `phenomenonTime`; external `TimeRange` context not confirmed standard equivalent. |
| **G30-06 source quality / resultQuality** | **HOLD native relation.** Original `F_ATemp="<0> "` (incl. trailing space) preserved, but SWE scalar quality/OMS resultQuality linkage not validated. |
| **G30-07 live backend conformance** | **HISTORICAL MISMATCH / UNTESTED HERE.** Prior 52°North SWE content-type/body/schema disagree; no new HTTP or server patch. |
| **G30-08 one common interpreter** | **NOT RUN.** 21 source-positive/negative contract oracles and read-only Bennett consistency are necessary but insufficient; Phase F tests one reader with unlike evidence inputs. |
| **G30-09 command authorization/effect** | **EXCLUDE for bounded descriptive release**; no verified real command/ACK/effect/actor permission. Source control syntax may be described only as capability. |

**Gate E: NOT CLOSED for a portable, source-independent, implementable candidate application Profile.** **Step 31 is COMPLETE as a planned challenge iteration**, because it produced explicit positive/negative test oracles, checked original records and identified these gates. This gate decision is not a wholesale failure of the DIM or a declaration that OGC standards are deficient; it is a precise answer to whether **our current source evidence and proposed bindings** already support the stronger interoperability claims. A deliberately narrower **descriptive/research subset** remains meaningful, but cannot be labeled *already generically interoperable*.

**Next step — Step 32 ONLY, conditional gate repair:** the plan already assigns Step 32 to define a **small source packet / example input contract** that can carry the required evidence standing and expected DIM meanings for all four different source scopes, **without inventing individual Systems for the reference-only cases**. Treat that as an evidence-carrier/prototype test needed to **revisit Gate E**, not an unconditional green light to develop a vendor-specific UI and declare Profile conformance. Step 32 should define exactly the source-level packet fields and negative/unknown behavior that could make **PR30-01, PR30-15 and PR30-21** genuinely consumable without manufacturer-name branches. It must **not** on its own claim OGC schema conformance or implement Step 33's shared Glaux reader.

**What this Step 31 did NOT do:** create a Step 32 input schema, implement a generic interpreter, change the Glaux app/Resource Details, modify GitHub Actions, deploy Cloudflare, alter the 52°North server or Oracle data, load a new Observation, perform full official OGC schema validation, create a qualified machine-readable Deployment, issue Commands or acquire new vendor hardware. Existing full original documentation, DIM/Profile candidate and frozen evidence remain intact.

## 36. Step 32 — Source-independent evidence packet and falsification-input contract

**2 November 2026 · STEP 32 COMPLETE AS INPUT-DESIGN/TEST-PREPARATION ONLY; STEP 33 NEXT AND NOT STARTED. Gate E remains NOT CLOSED.** The controlling [43-step plan](../PLAN_DIM_Profile_V2_Whole_System_Recovery_to_Delivery_v0.1_31OCT2026.md) permits Step 32 as a **bounded G30-01 Gate E repair investigation**. The substantive, human-readable decision and full test design are in the [one Step 32 contract document](../Candidate_Specification/Step32_Source_Packet_Contract.md); linked JSON input files are under [Candidate_Specification/examples/step32-source-packets/](../Candidate_Specification/examples/step32-source-packets/packet.schema.json). The earlier [Step 31 Profile contract review](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#step-31--contract-challenges-source-oracle-tests-and-bounded-gate-e-judgment) and this workbook §35 remain unchanged original evidence, not reclassified as software tests.

### 36.1 What this improves for the owner's actual goal

The source-first DIM and candidate Profile already identified a genuine danger: an ordinary client may see a device name or observation timestamp but cannot know whether it describes **a published reference design, a real instrument, a manufacturer sample, a source-authored historical result, or our derivation**. Nor can it infer when multiple API Observation resources are copies of the **same** originally reported measurement. Instead of adding Bennett-specific UI heuristics, Step 32 gives the proposed **same future reader** one small versioned input structure with explicit source standing, claim referent, original token, evidence link, derivation and event/copy relationship.

This is **an input-contract design**, not a new information-model ontology or an interoperable CSAPI wire binding. The schema and four packets are machine-readable **within the research workspace** and give later Steps 33–37 a common test target. An independent client still cannot be expected to discover these records through the 52°North API or any native OGC class. No current API resource, equipment state, command, deployed package or client display was changed.

No performed original event. |

No actual installed unit or live event. |

Original publication times and snapshot/edition scope remain explicit. No complete original manual or annual CSV was republished into the packet folder.

### 36.3 Original Bennett evidence, identity and uncertainty were not coerced

The original NOAA source fields still supply just **two** accepted 15-minute station air temperature means: 15 April 2024 **29.1 °C** and 18 April **27.3 °C**, both with `F_ATemp="<0> "` retaining the trailing space and independently blank `F_Record`. Both original station stamps were **12:00 EST**, with 16:45–17:00 UTC period projections and contributor probe assignments **DERIVED** from the time convention and separately documented role service. The first and returning EE181 serial **21281600077814** remains **one individual**, while interim **222016000109CE** is the second, across three source calendar-day service records. **31 December 2024** remains an as-of statement, not a removal date, ongoing 2026 status, exact exchange instant, original resultTime or probe location.

The packet's `originalEvents[]` contains **two source report anchors**, each scoped to the original archive edition, CSV row and `ATemp` field. Its `representations[]` maps **two old scalar plus two old archival-report** server resource IDs to those same two anchors and explicitly marks them `HISTORICAL_PROJECT_EXPERIMENT` from the [previous verified bridge](../PITON_DIM_Profile_V2_Continuity_v1.9_31OCT2026.md). This **project-owned association** makes a future duplicate-count falsification test possible; it **does not establish** a native wire-level equivalence link or actual consumer deduplication. The original completed-mean DataStream producing System is still an open **station versus CR1000X** hypothesis.

### 36.4 Bounded actual input checks, including one corrected checker assumption

The four committed JSON fixtures and candidate schema were **read back from GitHub**. A **transient local JavaScript schema-keyword/shape check** reported no structural errors for the four inputs; cross-reference checks required resolvable source, referent, assertion, derivation, source-event and copy IDs. The retained source JSON was used to compare both original mean values, station stamps, exact `F_ATemp` strings, separately blank `F_Record`, identities and event/copy count. No independent third-party JSON Schema library, actual OGC schema-conformance suite, Glaux interpreter, CSAPI server, or fielded sensor was exercised.

**Final bounded input/source-oracle record: 21/21 checks consistent** — **11 positive (S32-P01–P11)** and **10 deliberately corrupted/mutated expectations (S32-N01–N10)**. Negatives covered QC whitespace trimming, swapped derived probe, invented third physical serial, as-of converted into a removal date, a duplicated source-event compound key, broken representation event link, invented resultTime on UNKNOWN, converting a manufacturer example to a performed event, upgrading model identity to individual-unit identity, and interpreting 14 output fields as 14 actual sensors. These are source-packet/fixture consistency tests **only**, not the earlier 21 PR30/C31 documentary contract pairs, not the Step 31 17/17 checks, and not runtime OGC conformance tests.

The first attempt at **S32-N09** did **not** reject a fake AirGradient actual individual because the initial checker looked for *any nonempty identity string*. That was a **checker weakness**: an original **model designation** cannot, by its existence alone, establish an **installed-unit serial**. The revised source-identity oracle distinguishes identifier scheme/standing and correctly rejects that false promotion. This did **not** alter any original manufacturer facts or prove a unit identity; it exposes a specific later-reader acceptance obligation. A cross-document identity check remains necessary even if an input conforms to a JSON shape.

**Step 32's bounded deliverable is complete: there is one candidate source-independent input format, four source-honest example packets, original event/derivation/UNKNOWN expressibility, and an explicit falsification-harness plan.** The Step 32 packet does **not** solve the underlying **G30-01 producer→client discovery, OGC-standard or justified project carrier, encoding mapping and independent consumption** question. **Gate E remains NOT CLOSED**, and G30-02–09 retain their prior specific HOLD/EXCLUDED dispositions. The candidate [DIM](../Candidate_Specification/DIM.md) and [Profile](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md) normative standing were not upgraded.

**NEXT on a new owner “proceed”: STEP 33 ONLY.** Implement the **first small typed, source-independent interpretation path** with a source-positive and deliberately negative test, consuming the Step 32 packet contract **without branching on Dragino/AirGradient/Campbell/Bennett name or resource ID** and retaining raw unrecognized source fields. The app branch is still separate, and future comparisons of all individual cases, cross-vendor runtime results, integrated Resource Details UI, server-conformance tests and publication steps **must not** be credited until their later numbered prompts. No Oracle/52°North writes, authenticated commands, Cloudflare deployment, or Step 33 code change occurred in Step 32.

## 37. Step 33 — First typed, source-independent packet interpreter; strictly bounded runtime tests

**3 November 2026 (Europe/Amsterdam) · STEP 33 COMPLETE AS FIRST ISOLATED GLAUX READER INCREMENT ONLY; STEP 34 NEXT. GATE E REMAINS NOT CLOSED.** Work was performed on the separate application branch [work/details-view-20261023](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/tree/work/details-view-20261023), not by merging research and app branches. The current plan's [Step 32 input/schema study](../Candidate_Specification/Step32_Source_Packet_Contract.md), the candidate Profile's [PR30-01/15/21](../Candidate_Specification/CSAPI_Profile.md#3-candidate-pr30-rule-index--one-cross-case-contract-with-examples-and-original-authority) and the source-fidelity [§36](#36-step-32--source-independent-evidence-packet-and-falsification-input-contract) governed this small implementation. No Phase F case-specific steps 34–37 were executed, and the existing application pages, live Oracle/52°North service and Cloudflare deployment were not touched.

### 37.1 What improved for the user's whole-System objective

Step 32 only **defined** a way to express the most important source-qualified distinctions. Step 33 creates the **first executable read-only path** in Glaux that can consume that same *project-authored* packet shape without knowing the brand, system label, file name or Bennett's Point resource IDs. It outputs declared focal/reference/actual standing, source evidence links, exact raw values, distinct original facts/printed examples/DERIVED/UNKNOWN assertions, typed relationships and source-event-to-experimental-copy groupings. It never invents a physical serial, observation, live condition, exact swap clock time, resultTime or Command from absent source evidence.

This is **not a native CSAPI Profile parser**. It receives a packet already supplied to the function, rather than discovering the evidence packet through an OGC conformance class, media negotiation, CSAPI link or real service. The underlying packet claims are *declared* by its author; local structural consistency does not prove an issuer truly supplied the data. The approved source meanings and future standard bindings are separate decisions, and a client cannot claim interoperability simply because the decoder accepts a test JSON object.

### 37.2 Committed app sources and boundary

| App artifact | Verified work | Source/code authority and limits |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [First shared reader: src/profile/source-packet.ts](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/src/profile/source-packet.ts) | Pure typed \`interpretSourcePacket(input: unknown)\`, output \`accepted\`, focal subject, subjects, evidence-qualified claims, original source events/copies, explicit issues and unchanged complete raw input. It handles packet-local cross-references, exact literal string preservation, \`SOURCE_FACT\`, \`SOURCE_EXAMPLE\`, \`DERIVED\` inputs and \`UNKNOWN\` without value, and marks unrecognized concepts as unrecognized. | The \`dim-csapi-source-packet.s32.v0.1\` discriminator is **local project research vocabulary**, not an OGC content type, schema-conformant CSAPI resource, complete semantic proof or independently authenticated physical inventory. |
| [Synthetic test suite: tests/source-packet.test.ts](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/tests/source-packet.test.ts) | **11 positive/negative source-neutral unit cases**: model/reference standing; literal spacing and source examples; unknown extension/foreign concept; identifier/label independence; invalid UNKNOWN with value; false actual unit without source-scoped identity; broken evidence; printed example disguised as actual event; one synthetic original source event/two experimental copies; broken copy event; supported DERIVED method/time; rejected missing derivation input. | Tests use **fictional neutral evidence**, not the Dragino/AirGradient/Campbell/Bennett source packets. They do **not** certify original source truth, discoverability, whole-System semantics or original OGC standards. |
| [Profile implementation boundary](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/src/profile/README.md) and [app CURRENT_WORK](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/Docs/Plans/CURRENT_WORK.md) | Where this reader belongs, why it remains separate from \`src/data\` and the existing Resource Details renderer, and exact local test/rollout limitations. | App documentation must not supersede this research branch's numbered plan or earlier PITON; no client UI route is made Profile-aware by this isolated increment alone. |

The module uses only a declared *candidate* list of Step 32 concept keys, **not manufacturer-specific string matching**. Unrecognized keys and additional source detail stay available through the original \`raw\` reference and assertion-level \`raw\` rather than being fabricated as familiar fields. The decoder preserves original time objects as given and **does not** normalize a date-only statement into a precise temporal event, or infer an observation from a printed example. Copy grouping is by explicitly provided original-event association, **not** heuristic time proximity or a claim that the API already publishes \`sameAs\`.

### 37.3 Exactly what actually ran — not overstated

The exact committed reader and test bytes were compared to the locally tested files by Git object identity. The reader passed an isolated **strict TypeScript \`tsc --noEmit\`** check with available TypeScript **5.8.3** (repository package currently specifies 5.9.3), and **11/11** of the new test suite's logical checks passed an **isolated Node 22.16 built-in test harness** after transient TypeScript transpilation. The repository's normal target is Node **24** and **Vitest**; the project dependencies and runner were not installed in this restricted workspace, so \`npm ci\`, \`npm run typecheck\` across all code, \`npm test\` through Vitest, \`npm run build\`, browser validation and GitHub Actions **were not executed here**. The committed Vitest test file is prepared for a later normal project verification run, **not falsely reported as having executed under Vitest**.

The 11 local checks include **positive interpretation** of one fictional externally connected reference component with source-native literal sample \`" 0 "\`, explicit \`SOURCE_EXAMPLE\` and \`UNKNOWN\`; **negative source assertion corruption** that attempts to populate an UNKNOWN value, invent a physical individual without original identity, break the evidence pointer, turn a reference description into a performed source event or orphan a representation; neutral *identifier and label renaming* retaining exactly the same derived semantic dispositions; explicit \`DERIVED\` method/source-input preservation and missing-input rejection; and one **fictional** event with two project copy references grouped under that event rather than counted as two originals. The unknown nested extras/foreign concepts and original input remained inspectable and unmodified.

**Proof scope:** a pure, source-independent first interpreter function and bounded test logic exist. The actual four evidence packets have **not** been interpreted by this function as source-based case evaluations, and no standards-conformant or live-resource producer→CSAPI→generic-consumer path was exercised. This is the intended, deliberately modest Step 33 implementation checkpoint—not an early Step 34, Gate E closure or general-purpose vendor adapter.

### 37.4 Known limitations and retained gate decisions

- **G30-01 (BLOCKING):** the source packet is **not discoverable or validly negotiated through an OGC/native CSAPI binding**. Independent producer authority, provenance semantics and interoperability require explicit documented carrier decisions and follow-on tests. An author-assigned \`standing\` does not prove the original source supports it. An actual unit identifier's *scheme and issuer* also need original source corroboration; a nonempty model ID alone is insufficient.
- **G30-02–07:** Bennett completed-average producing System, historical calendar-day Deployment, original UNKNOWN \`resultTime\`, valid interval-valued \`phenomenonTime\`, native \`resultQuality\` and real 52°North SWE JSON behavior remain precisely as in Step 31. The new reader does not normalize or repair them.
- **G30-08:** common **reader code now exists at isolated input-function level**, but **the exact same code has not yet passed the four source-case evaluations**, actual vendor-equipment fidelity checks, integration into Resource Details or real CSAPI end-to-end tests. A single neutral synthetic test is *not* a verified cross-vendor interpretation.
- **G30-09:** executable device control/permissions/physical effect remains excluded. Neither new commands nor control endpoints were used.
- **Input completeness ceiling:** the reader checks a bounded subset of source-packet member types/cross-references and retains raw unknown fields; it is **not** an independent JSON Schema Draft 2020-12 validator or a domain-independent source-auditing engine. Temporal semantics, source authority, unknown original facts and real deployed unit status are never manufactured.

**Gate E NOT CLOSED; Gate F not yet demonstrated.** Step 33 completed the planned first reader/code/test increment; it did not satisfy the original gate's unknown compatible client + standardized producer discovery or source fidelity obligations.

## 38. Step 34 — Dragino S31-LB source-packet challenge and fidelity limits

**3 November 2026 · STEP 34 COMPLETE as a bounded manufacturer-reference case test; STEP 35 NEXT, NOT STARTED; Gate E remains NOT CLOSED.** This used the exact unchanged [Step 33 generic interpreter](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/src/profile/source-packet.ts), the existing [Step 32 Dragino packet](../Candidate_Specification/examples/step32-source-packets/dragino.reference.packet.json), the itemized [original-source C sheet](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Dragino_S31_LB.md), original C02 manufacturer manual and pinned C03 decoder. A byte-identical copy of the research packet was committed under the application's [test fixtures](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/tests/fixtures/source-packets/dragino.reference.packet.json), confirmed as Git blob **5d7931846ff88b0752dab81402a4df1703fa8077**. The shared interpreter itself stayed at blob **346d682cbff6a2e97770feba088452df6a067c4e**; there is **no manufacturer-specific branch** in it.

### 38.1 Human result — what the first real manufacturer case tells us

Dragino **S31-LB** is a **reference product/model**, not an authenticated deployed individual (do not borrow S31B or LS construction). Original **C-I02** describes **one external SHT31 with a three-metre cable**; C-F05 and C-F06 give two environmental output properties, temperature and relative humidity, **not two physical probes**. Original **C-F05** supplies a printed scalar 0x0105 → **26.1 °C** as a *manufacturer example*, not independently observed field telemetry. C-I04 states a rated nonrechargeable **8,500 mAh** battery capacity, not remaining charge. C-I15 provides no installed individual identity or actual setting; C-O03 documents a design **20-minute default** while actual effective interval remains unknown.

The unchanged reader returned **one REFERENCE_ARRANGEMENT**, **one REFERENCE_COMPONENT**, the single external-link relation, the two native field names and units, the **SOURCE_EXAMPLE** raw 26.1, rated capacity as design SOURCE_FACT, and two explicit **UNKNOWN** claims (installed identity and actual effective interval). It returned **zero original performed events** and zero experimental copies. It did not invent a serial, Device EUI, physical installation, Observation, current battery percentage, actual mode or Command.

| Source → candidate DIM | Input packet → unchanged reader | Candidate CSAPI/Profile limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C-I01/C-I15; R24-01/02: product kind distinct from actual individual | Model designation S31-LB is reference standing, installed identity UNKNOWN | Kind/Procedure/SensorML description is a conditional future carrier, **not** an actually published and validated CSAPI Procedure or actual System |
| C-I02; R24-03/04/06: one external physical probe | Single link from reference node to reference SHT31; no second part | SensorML design/connection proposal only; no exact deployed Subsystem or Deployment |
| C-F05/06; R24-07/08: two source fields | TempC_SHT31 (°C) and Hum_SHT31 (% RH) are two values of one SHT31 reference | Potential output/SWE fields, **not** actual DataStream/Observation or a second device |
| C-F05; R24-09: printed example not performed act | 26.1 °C stays SOURCE_EXAMPLE, original event count zero | No native Part 2 Observation, actual result time, location or live data |
| C-I04; R24-10/14: rated battery versus health | SOURCE_FACT design rating 8,500 mAh, not current level | No charge percentage, remaining battery life or measured condition |
| C-O03/C-C01; R24-10/11: default versus effective and available versus issued command | Effective setting is correctly UNKNOWN, **but 20-minute documented default is absent from packet** | **Source→packet coverage gap**, not reader loss; no current interval or authorized ControlStream |

### 38.2 Informative Resource Details presentation — not deployed (Step 38 retains integration)

| Display area | Honest result from this source packet | Necessary qualifier |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Identity and Equipment** | “S31-LB — manufacturer reference”; one externally cabled three-metre SHT31 | No known individual installed unit, physical serial, EUI, deployed coordinates, status |
| **Outputs / capabilities** | Temperature TempC_SHT31 (°C) and relative humidity Hum_SHT31 (% RH) | Both properties from one reference probe; **no actual observation stream** |
| **Power / condition** | Rated nonrechargeable 8,500 mAh battery | Not present battery charge, voltage measurement, health or remaining autonomy |
| **Activity** | Optional “manufacturer decoding example: 26.1 °C” | **Not live or historical field telemetry**; cannot be plotted as actual Observation |
| **Configuration** | “Actual reporting interval unknown” | Source itself says **20-minute default**, but the packet does not contain it; cannot truthfully render it from this input |
| **Commands / source** | Links to documented C02/C03 source, with reference/evidence qualifications | Printed AT commands do not establish actual request, authorization or effect |

This is a **source-grounded preview** of a correctly qualified common Resource Details view, not a new UI route, code renderer, normative tab arrangement or live browser test. Actual UI integration remains Step 38.

### 38.3 What actually ran: structural PASS versus real source-truth failures

The actual committed reader was fetched from the app branch and its **JavaScript logic executed in a transient V8 harness after stripping TypeScript-only syntax**, with the exact project Dragino source packet as input. The [13 committed source-case tests](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/tests/source-packet.dragino.test.ts) passed **13/13 through that compatibility harness**, not through Vitest. A second transient screen checked **18/18 expected source-positive, malformed-input, source-false and omission outcomes**. The latter count **does not** mean 18/18 fidelity success.

**Direct positive behavior:** the reader accepted the original reference packet and preserved model versus reference component, one cabled probe/two fields, exact printed example label, rated capacity, UNKNOWN installed/effective state and no actual source-event records. **Five mutated malformed packets** (broken component target, missing source reference, fabricated UNKNOWN literal, event fabricated from reference design, duplicate assertion identifier) were correctly **rejected**.

**Three source-false mutations were structurally accepted by the reader** but **rejected by the separate source-specific test oracle**:

| Mutation tested | Reader result | Original source fidelity result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Promote S31-LB **model** designation to SOURCE_IDENTIFIED_EQUIPMENT, still using the model scheme | **ACCEPTED** as a well-formed packet | **FAIL:** C-I01/C-I15 have no authenticated installed individual serial |
| Change printed 26.1 °C **SOURCE_EXAMPLE** into SOURCE_FACT | **ACCEPTED** | **FAIL:** C-F05 is a printed scalar, not an actual reported measurement |
| Add a second fictional SHT31 with a valid internal reference | **ACCEPTED** | **FAIL:** C-I02 documents **one** external SHT31 probe |

**One source-to-input coverage failure:** C-O03's **20-minute documented default uplink interval** is absent from the actual Step 32 packet. The generic reader cannot recover a fact never supplied to it. This is a source→packet/qualification gap, not a reason to fabricate the device's present configured interval. The correction belongs in a deliberate profile/packet repair decision after the bounded case challenges, not in a vendor-specific rendering branch.

**Test ceiling:** no full repository Vitest or Node 24 run, app-wide TypeScript/build, browser rendering, actual CSAPI media/schema conformance, production service, Cloudflare or physical equipment was exercised. “Accepted by source-packet reader” means **structurally consistent input**, not authenticated original manufacturer meaning. Three false claims currently pass that limited acceptance layer; these are **retained negative findings, not passing interoperability**.

### 38.4 Gate result and exact Step 35 handoff

**Step 34 is COMPLETE as a source-falsifiable Dragino exercise**, even though some fidelity results are **FAIL**. It demonstrates that one unchanged common interpreter can represent this manufacturer-reference packet *when the source qualifications are accurately declared*, and identifies where source falsity still slips through. Gate E **remains NOT CLOSED** because **G30-01**—portable producer→independent-client source identity, assertion standing and discoverable provenance—remains unresolved. G30-08 also stays open until **more than one unlike real source case** passes the same path, and other Bennett/management/time/quality gaps remain unchanged. Do not insert a Dragino-specific rule into the shared interpreter or claim the packet alone supplies an adopted OGC carrier.

**NEXT on one future “proceed”: STEP 35 ONLY.** Apply the **same unchanged reader** to [AirGradient Open Air Max manufacturer-reference packet](../Candidate_Specification/examples/step32-source-packets/airgradient.reference.packet.json) and [source sheet A](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/AirGradient_Open_Air_Max.md). In particular, test two physical PMS5003 reference modules at **PM1/PM2 sockets** versus PM1.0/PM2.5/PM10 *data properties*, source examples versus actual measurements, and unsupported calibration/installed firmware/current unit evidence. Preserve the Dragino default omission and the three source-authenticity failures for a later targeted repair/gate audit. **Do not** proceed into Campbell, Bennett, UI integration, Oracle/Cloudflare changes or publication steps in the same turn.

## 39. Step 35 — AirGradient Open Air Max source-packet challenge and fidelity limits

**3 November 2026 · STEP 35 COMPLETE AS ONE BOUNDED MANUFACTURER-REFERENCE CASE CHALLENGE; STEP 36 NEXT, NOT STARTED. Gate E remains NOT CLOSED; Gate F not yet demonstrated.** This is the second actual manufacturer-documentary case exercised against **the same unchanged Step 33 Glaux source-packet interpreter** (after Dragino Step 34), but **not** a cross-vendor source-authenticated or original CSAPI-compliant Profile PASS. The original [AirGradient source sheet A](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/AirGradient_Open_Air_Max.md), its [A01 hardware README](https://github.com/Open-Air-Foundation/pcb-openair-max-main/blob/89aacf5013fc0ffe4b988a2e3c5a227bce3c4983/README.md), [A02 firmware README](https://github.com/Open-Air-Foundation/firmware-openair-max/blob/84e479a415d81151e7a81aea9a2f7449a15e98cc/README.md), retained [Step 32 evidence packet](../Candidate_Specification/examples/step32-source-packets/airgradient.reference.packet.json), and the unaltered [generic interpreter](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/src/profile/source-packet.ts) govern this result. The exact research input was copied into [the application test fixture](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/tests/fixtures/source-packets/airgradient.reference.packet.json) without byte changes; both share Git blob **f3684d6e1e8285f9f766420a379cef8c0dc60201**.

### 39.1 Original-source distinction: hardware, output properties and example values

The selected **O-M-1PPSTON-CE** is the manufacturer-described **gas-equipped Open Air Max reference design**, not an individually verified field installation. The original pinned hardware source A01 describes **two physical Plantower PMS5003 modules** connected to separately named **PM1** and **PM2** hardware sockets, plus additional sensing, processing, communication and power modules. The pinned firmware README A02 publishes separate source-native fields `pm01`, `pm25` and `pm10` referring to **PM1.0, PM2.5 and PM10 particulate reported properties**. Socket names and reported property names are **not interchangeable**, and the selected sources do not prove which actual individual sensor produced any particular historical or current `pm25` reading.

The retained A02 annotated **Wi-Fi payload example** gives `pm25 = 27.7 µg/m³`, but this is **SOURCE_EXAMPLE**, not an independently acquired real physical-unit Observation. A02 also contains distinct cellular sample groups and different scaling, and A09 publishes other cloud examples; these should **not** be merged into one synchronized, calibrated installed-unit record. Source A01 hardware snapshot, A02 firmware snapshot and the separately documented A07 firmware-era behavior are **independent sources/editions**, not evidence of one deployed firmware/hardware pair. The exact selected physical-unit identity, installed firmware, site, selected power/network mode, active configuration, delivered observation history, gas calibration and authorized controls remain unverified.

### 39.2 What the unchanged shared interpreter actually recovered

The unchanged reader accepted the existing small project packet with **one REFERENCE_ARRANGEMENT focal subject and two REFERENCE_COMPONENT subjects**, both explicitly **design-level** and separately identified by reference socket PM1/PM2. The two source-qualified `equipment.reference_component` relationships point to these two modules; neither was promoted into an actual serial-numbered installed unit. The three `output.native_field` claims preserve `pm01`, `pm25` and `pm10` as reported field names in **µg/m³**, scoped to the reference assembly rather than improperly attributing a result to a particular PMS5003. The `a-example` claim keeps `27.7` as `SOURCE_EXAMPLE`; `a-installed` remains `UNKNOWN`. The packet contains **zero actual original performed events and zero API representation copies**.

| Original source/equipment meaning | Implementation-independent DIM relation | Project evidence packet / unchanged reader | Candidate OGC/CSAPI binding and current limit |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A-I01 / A-I16: reference gas-equipped assembly, no deployed individual | R24-01/02: **reference kind ≠ original physical individual** | Focus `a-max` is reference `O-M-1PPSTON-CE`; installed identity UNKNOWN | Reference-kind **Procedure/SensorML** is conditional if actually published with correct class/link; **no actual physical `System` or installed serial established** |
| A-I06: **two PMS5003 modules at PM1/PM2** | R24-03/06/07: physical constituent versus equipment interface/role | Two separate reference components and `equipment.reference_component` assertions; original `PM1/PM2` tokens retained | SensorML components and physical interface descriptions possible at design level; **not proof of a populated deployed Subsystem or an observed channel** |
| A-F04/05/06: **PM1.0/PM2.5/PM10** properties | R24-07/08: output field/property ≠ hardware socket | Three native `output.native_field` records about focal reference; no hardware count by number of fields | SWE Common field definitions possible as bounded design schema; **no actual DataStream/Observation or per-sensor producer evidence** |
| A-F05: printed Wi-Fi `pm25 = 27.7 µg/m³` | R24-09/15: **source example ≠ performed act** | `SOURCE_EXAMPLE`, raw `27.7`, native field `pm25`; event count **0** | Do not create an actual Part 2 Observation, current environmental claim, live meter reading, timestamp or verified calibration |
| A-I03/04, A-G01–06: hardware/firmware snapshots and context differ | R24-01/10/14: edition/source, declared choices versus installed state | Packet explicitly holds installed identity UNKNOWN, does **not** assert actual firmware, active power/network or gas calibration | A code commit is not installed firmware or a manufacturer-issued physical serial; **stronger native state/command claims excluded** |
| A-I07–15 / A-P01–07 / A-C01–13: other physical modules, power, communication and settings | R24-03/06/10/14: supporting whole-System facts may be relevant independently | **Most of these source-supported design facts are absent** from the selected small packet, beyond the two PMS5003 reference modules | **Source→packet coverage limitation**, not data a generic reader can recover without input; defer selection/repair rather than filling with invented fielded data |

**Important evidence specificity:** PMS5003 is a physical reference component type in the documented design; `PM1/PM2` are its hardware socket positions. `pm01` and `pm25` are firmware-reported **particle fraction/size property fields**, not alternate spellings of those ports. Even a faithful producer cannot infer a unique emitting physical installed module simply from similar strings, and the source packet deliberately does not assert one. The distinction enables a useful future common UI but has not been proven through a real served CSAPI/SensorML chain.

### 39.3 Informative Resource Details preview — not a new live Glaux page

| Shared Resource Details area | Source-faithful information a future common client could show | Required qualifier or withheld fact |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Identity** | “Open Air Max O-M-1PPSTON-CE — manufacturer reference design” | No verified individual serial, installed device, deployment, geographical position, live condition or authenticated firmware version |
| **Equipment** | Two **PMS5003** reference modules connected at hardware **PM1** and **PM2** sockets | Socket labels are not PM concentration/size properties; other source-known modules **were omitted from this small packet** |
| **Outputs/capabilities** | `pm01` (PM1.0), `pm25` (PM2.5), `pm10` (PM10), in µg/m³, as documented source fields | Three output fields do not imply three physical sensors or proven per-module observation attribution |
| **Activity / measured values** | “Manufacturer Wi-Fi example: `pm25=27.7 µg/m³`” | **SOURCE_EXAMPLE**, not present telemetry, observed environmental condition, validated gas concentration or a native CSAPI Observation |
| **Communications / power** | Manufacturer original source documents Wi-Fi/cellular and USB-C/solar/battery design options | These details are **not present** in the small Step 32 packet and cannot be displayed from that packet alone; no active mode or actual battery state |
| **Configuration / condition** | Installed identity and actual configuration unavailable in this evidence packet | Distinct source-era firmware, different network/interval examples, uncertain calibration and installed state must **not** be reconciled into one operating unit |
| **Sources / provenance** | Pinned A01/A02 originals, manufacturer-attributed examples and explicit UNKNOWN standing | Source assertion types are currently **project-declared**, not authenticated or discoverable through a published, implemented OGC Profile |

**This table is an informative source-to-display design target only.** We **did not implement** a new Resource Details renderer, deploy a browser page, create a System/Procedure/DataStream, update the existing Codex map, or claim a new UI acceptance test. The user-facing rendering and its actual source-fidelity scorecard belong to later Steps 38 and 40.

### 39.4 Exact test layers and falsifications

The preserved [app copy of the evidence packet](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/tests/fixtures/source-packets/airgradient.reference.packet.json) was confirmed to match the research input by Git blob; the existing generic reader remains unmodified at **346d682cbff6a2e97770feba088452df6a067c4e**. A [new app case-test suite](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/tests/source-packet.airgradient.test.ts) uses the **same source-independent reader**, with a **separate test-only source oracle** informed by pinned A01/A02 and maintained research sheet A. The source oracle includes **source-specific expected physical-count/output-property/standing checks**; it was not installed as a new vendor switch in the shared application code.

**16/16 test expectations passed in a transient V8 compatibility harness** after stripping TypeScript-only annotations from the exact fetched module/test files. This was **not** the repository's Vitest runner, full application TypeScript/build, browser test, official JSON Schema, live 52°North API, sensor hardware or independent source authentication. The observed categories include six positive/label-independence tests, **five malformed-input cases correctly rejected** by the generic reader, **four source-false but structurally accepted cases caught by the separate source oracle**, and one original-source-to-packet coverage-limit test.

| Input challenge | What the unchanged reader did | Original-source fidelity decision |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Original correct packet: two hardware sockets, three source output fields and one printed PM2.5 sample | **ACCEPTED** with appropriate declared SOURCE_FACT/SOURCE_EXAMPLE/UNKNOWN, zero source events | **SOURCE-SUPPORTED AT DOCUMENTARY REFERENCE LEVEL**, not an actual installation or CSAPI conformance proof |
| Broken socket target, nonexistent original firmware source, UNKNOWN with invented actual value, fake event from reference design, duplicate assertion ID | **REJECTED** structurally | Appropriate generic malformed-input rejection |
| **F35-01:** model `O-M-1PPSTON-CE` promoted to `SOURCE_IDENTIFIED_EQUIPMENT` while identifier scheme remains `model designation` | **ACCEPTED** structurally | **FAIL original-source identity**; A-I01/A-I16 describe only product design, not physical serialized installed unit |
| **F35-02:** `pm25` field converted to `equipment.reference_component`, linked to PM2 hardware socket | **ACCEPTED**; internal target resolves | **FAIL semantic category**; A-F05 is an output property, not PM2 hardware or evidence of a direct physical source |
| **F35-03:** printed `pm25=27.7` promoted from SOURCE_EXAMPLE to SOURCE_FACT | **ACCEPTED** structurally | **FAIL source evidence standing**; A02 is an annotated published example, not acquired actual telemetry |
| **F35-04:** a fabricated third PMS5003 design module at fictional PM3 and a coherent valid reference link | **ACCEPTED** structurally | **FAIL original hardware configuration**; A-I06 documents two PMS5003 modules at PM1/PM2 only |
| A-I07–15 and A-P/A-C source items omitted from selected packet | Generic reader appropriately cannot invent those fields | **SOURCE→PACKET COVERAGE GAP**, not interpreter parsing loss; remains unrepresented within this selected study slice |

**Interpretation of “16/16” is critical:** all locally scripted test scenarios reached their **expected verdicts**; the **four source-false accepted cases are not source-fidelity PASSes**. This is the same unresolved authenticity distinction exposed in Step 34, now encountered in a second, substantially different manufacturer-reference structure. The result strengthens the case for a source-authority/producer obligation and a genuinely discoverable Profile contract, not for hard-coded AirGradient exceptions in the client.

### 39.5 Gate standing and precisely what Step 36 will do

**Step 35 is COMPLETE as a bounded, falsifiable AirGradient source-to-reader experiment**, with retained negative outcomes and explicit source→packet gaps. The source packet is readable through an **unchanged generic interpretation function** also used in Dragino Step 34, which establishes a limited useful common parsing behavior across two differently organized manufacturer designs. It **does not** prove a portable, source-authenticated, standard-conformant, end-to-end CSAPI Profile; moreover both cases exposed ways that a coherent self-declared input can carry false original-source assertions. **Gate E remains NOT CLOSED (G30-01)** and **Gate F not yet demonstrated** as a complete faithful client. No new source class, universal PM ontology, production UI change, server data write, software deployment or authenticated control was executed.

**NEXT on one subsequent owner “proceed”: STEP 36 ONLY.** Exercise the **same unchanged reader** against the original [Campbell CR1000X + first-generation ClimaVue 50 source sheet D](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Campbell_CR1000X_ClimaVue50.md) and [existing reference packet](../Candidate_Specification/examples/step32-source-packets/campbell.reference.packet.json). Challenge the two separately documented products and SDI-12 connection, **14 ordered `aR7!` response positions versus physical sensors**, ClimaVue ambient `Air temperature` versus logger `PanelTemp`, and unrelated manufacturer Status example versus an authenticated joined installed station. Retain missing source facts and negative oracles without altering the generic interpreter by manufacturer name. Do **not** run Bennett (37), UI integration (38), service conformance (39), publication (40–43), Oracle/52°North writes, Cloudflare deployment or actual device Commands in the same prompt.

## 40. Step 36 — Campbell logger-ClimaVue 50 source-packet challenge and fidelity limits

**3 November 2026 · STEP 36 COMPLETE as one bounded original-manufacturer REFERENCE case evaluation; STEP 37 NEXT AND NOT STARTED. Gate E remains NOT CLOSED; Gate F not yet demonstrated.** The same **unchanged** [Step 33 Glaux evidence reader](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/src/profile/source-packet.ts) was tested on the third differently structured manufacturer source arrangement: [Campbell CR1000X plus first-generation ClimaVue 50](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Campbell_CR1000X_ClimaVue50.md). Source paper D is an audit of the publisher's separate original D02 ClimaVue 50 manual (08/2024, Table 7-1 and Table 8-1), D07 CR1000X MQTT Status example, relevant D01/D05/D06 documentation and historical D03/D04 examples; the last group was **not** authenticated as one installed active release. The prior paper records original PDF visual verification; this step did not reverify the original PDF independently. The input was the preserved [Step 32 source evidence packet](../Candidate_Specification/examples/step32-source-packets/campbell.reference.packet.json), copied **byte-for-byte** as the app's [Step 36 test fixture](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/tests/fixtures/source-packets/campbell.reference.packet.json), shared Git blob **3671babc4d415d480022d9372851eeb5f1c82ae3**.

### 40.1 Source meaning in plain language

The subject is **two different kinds of manufactured equipment in a documented possible logger–sensor arrangement**, not a single authenticated deployed weather station. The CR1000X performs data logging/processing/reporting roles; the first-generation ClimaVue 50 is a separate sensing assembly with an SDI-12 interface. The manufacturer describes how an SDI-12 signal is connected to a logger terminal, and the sensor's original factory default address is 0. **Neither the proposed pairing, the default address nor a historical example program proves an actual currently wired station, applied address or active program.** A CR1000Xe or second-generation ClimaVue 50 would require different evidence and was not imported into this case.

ClimaVue's **`aR7!` response is one native response with 14 ordered numerical positions** (D-R01–14), not 14 physically identified sensor units and not an original sampled observation acquired during this research. **Position 8 is ambient *air temperature*** (D-R08, °C); **position 12 is *humidity-sensor temperature*** (D-R12, °C), a different observed object even though both use degrees Celsius. The same original D02 source says certain command responses can contain a previously reported value, so merely receiving a response would not prove a fresh environmental sampling act.

By contrast, **CR1000X `PanelTemp` is temperature of the LOGGER WIRING PANEL**, not the ClimaVue ambient air or humidity sensor. Manufacturer D07's independent MQTT Status example reports `PanelTemp = 25.91098 °C` (D-S02). This is **SOURCE_EXAMPLE** from a separately published logger Status message, not a numerical `aR7!` sensor response, no authenticated reading of a particular installed Campbell unit, and no evidence that the ClimaVue produced it. The original 31-field Status example includes a separate logger `Battery` (13.2368 Vdc) and many diagnostics, but no source-backed equivalence of those fields to ClimaVue conditions or whole-station readiness can be invented.

### 40.2 Exactly what survived source → candidate DIM → project packet → unchanged interpreter

The reader accepted a source-accurate packet with **three subjects**: one `REFERENCE_ARRANGEMENT` focal pairing and two `REFERENCE_COMPONENT` types (CR1000X logger and first-generation ClimaVue 50). It retained two reference-kind relation assertions and **one `equipment.design_connection` SDI-12 link**, rather than treating wiring documentation as a performed command, registered Deployment, historical service fact or permanent physical integration. It retained **eight selected claims**: two reference equipment links, one design connection, one 14-field count, one ClimaVue air-temperature field definition, one separately sourced logger PanelTemp field definition, one `SOURCE_EXAMPLE` PanelTemp literal and one explicit `UNKNOWN` actual installation. **No genuine original Observation events or previous project CSAPI copy representations are in the manufacturer-reference packet.**

| Original source and physical/functional distinction | Integrated DIM proposition | Step 32 packet and unchanged reader | Proposed CSAPI/Profile boundary and exact limitation |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **D-I01/I02:** logger and separate sensing assembly; two reference kinds, no installed station | R24-01/02/03/04: reference product ≠ actual individual; physical-part ≠ connected-equipment role | `d-pair` reference focus, `d-logger` and `d-climavue` as two reference kinds | **Potential** SensorML `System`/component/connection and type-`Procedure` description, **not** verified original CSAPI published physical-unit resource/Deployment |
| **D-I05/I06/I08:** proposed power and SDI-12 signal wiring; factory default address **0** | R24-04/07/10: physical/interface design versus effective installed connection/address | `d-connection` has `equipment.design_connection` and original `SDI-12` text; installed config remains UNKNOWN | May describe interface design; no actual running bus address, logger terminal, individual deployment or issued Command can be asserted |
| **D-R01–14:** `aR7!` describes fourteen ordered response fields | R24-06/08: sensing role, output form and each field/property are distinct from physical equipment | `d-field-count` retains raw `14` and `aR7!`; does **not** instantiate 14 sensors or source observations | SWE Common Record schema is a **candidate** for source-defined output structure; no complete native 14-field mapping or actual physical sensor count by field |
| **D-R08:** ClimaVue **ambient air temperature**, position 8 | R24-08/09: correct observed property/object versus performed act | `d-ambient-field` about `d-climavue`, `aR7! position 8`, temperature field definition, **no numeric weather result** | Could describe source-supported response property; **no actual Observation/phenomenonTime or verified producing System** |
| **D-R12:** humidity sensor's own temperature, position 12 | R24-08: two quantities with same unit may still have different observed objects | **NOT included in small Step 32 packet** | Source→packet **coverage gap**; do not automatically treat it as ambient, panel temperature or nonexistent |
| **D-S02/D07:** logger `PanelTemp` **25.91098 °C** from separate 2026 published Status example | R24-05/08/09/15: reporting equipment, native property/object, and source example standing | `d-panel-field` about **logger** with original D07 source; `d-panel-example` retains `SOURCE_EXAMPLE` raw `25.91098` | **Not** a ClimaVue air-temperature observation, not a measured installed state, not a confirmed station connection |
| **D-S01–31:** 31 published logger Status fields; other D03/D04 historical programs separate | R24-08/10/14: diagnostics/software/communications/status attach to correct referents and editions | **30 of 31 Status fields NOT selected**; program/source editions and active runtime not represented | **Source→packet coverage gap**, not reader loss; a source name `Battery` does not imply remaining charge or physical ClimaVue voltage |
| **D-G01/G02/G05/G07:** actual identity, configuration, sensor result and controls absent | R24-01/09/10/11: no evidence upgrade to individual, effect or performed observation | `d-installed` is explicit `UNKNOWN`; `originalEvents=[]`, `representations=[]` | No actual station serial, firmware, status, Command approval, native Part 2 Observation or verified remote action |

**No new model class, OGC `SHALL` or wire binding was adopted by this mapping table.** The source packet was read directly from project research files, not independently discovered through an approved native CSAPI conformance class, machine-readable Profile link, media type or source-provenance endpoint. The test oracle uses publisher/audit-specific evidence **only in tests**, not in the shared reader.

### 40.3 Informative Resource Details preview — what a future client could show, not a rendered page

| Common Resource Details area | Source-qualified information a future generic view could display | Must remain explicit or unavailable |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Identity** | “CR1000X + first-generation ClimaVue 50 — documented possible manufacturer reference arrangement” | **No authenticated installed station, hardware serials, site or joined live configuration** |
| **Equipment / connectivity** | CR1000X **logger type** and separate ClimaVue 50 **sensing assembly type**, with source-described **SDI-12 design connection** | Factory default address **0** from original source ≠ a measured active bus address. Do not present a permanent hardware-only part hierarchy from documentary wiring |
| **Sensing output** | ClimaVue `aR7!`: **14 defined response positions**; position 8 **air temperature** | The small packet includes only **one of the 14 position definitions**. No acquired ClimaVue numerical weather values, or 14 identified sensors |
| **Logger Status / condition** | Logger `PanelTemp` as separate property; published **25.91098 °C** Status example | This refers to logger panel only; the value is a **published example, not live operation**. Other 30 Status fields omitted |
| **Other temperature types** | Source D-R12 has **humidity-sensor temperature** at position 12 | **Not in selected packet**; cannot be manufactured, collapsed with position 8 ambient air or conflated with logger PanelTemp |
| **Activity / readings** | Explicit “No acquired actual Observation in this reference corpus” | No invented field event, source timestamps, per-parameter QA, site, historical trend, command acknowledgement or operator availability |
| **Configuration / management** | Source-linked design wiring and limitations | **Actual SDI-12 address, active program, firmware, authorization and effect unknown**; printed `aAb!`/measurement commands are not actual issued Commands |
| **Sources** | Manufacturer ClimaVue D02 design manual and D07 independent logger Status example; source-edition labels | 2019 program, 2021 download, 2024 manual and 2026 status example remain **separate provenance**, not a verified installed release combination |

This is a **documented source-to-display fidelity sketch only**. It is **not an existing, implemented or deployed Glaux Resource Details page**. Actual shared presentation integration remains **Step 38**; source-to-display comparisons remain **Step 40**. Source-preserving interpretation is a necessary first step, not the same as a complete UI or OGC conformance result.

### 40.4 Exactly what was tested and what failed at source-fidelity level

The [Campbell case test suite](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/tests/source-packet.campbell.test.ts) contains **18 independently named test scenarios** and a **test-only original-source oracle**. The **exact committed** generic interpreter, tests and byte-identical source packet were read from GitHub and used for a **temporary V8 compatibility execution** after stripping TypeScript-only syntax. **Final outcome: 18/18 test expectations observed.** The first transient transpilation attempt had a local test-harness stripping mistake (an initializer was removed), corrected in the harness without modifying the reader or original packet. This count is **NOT** 18/18 original-source fidelity, an installed Vitest run, complete app build, user-visible screen test, native CSAPI/SensorML/SWE conformance, or authenticated live operation.

The **seven positive-source/representation checks** covered the two distinct reference products, SDI-12 documentary interface, single `aR7!` output of fourteen positions, separate air position 8 versus logger `PanelTemp`, published `25.91098` as an example, explicit UNKNOWNs and invariance under changing packet-local IDs/labels. **Five structurally malformed mutations** were correctly **REJECTED**: nonexistent SDI-12 relationship target; missing original publisher for Status field; invented literal attached to UNKNOWN; fabricated performed original weather event for a reference-only subject; duplicate assertion ID.

**Five more semantically false source assertions were structurally ACCEPTED by the unchanged reader** but recognized as false by the independent original-source test oracle:

| Adversarial claim | Structural reader response | Original-source fidelity verdict |
| --- | --- | --- |
| F36-01: change CR1000X logger **reference model** standing to a real `SOURCE_IDENTIFIED_EQUIPMENT` while original identifier remains `scheme=model` | **ACCEPTED** as well-formed declaration | **FAIL:** D-I01/17 provides a product kind, not a uniquely verified installed physical logger |
| F36-02: reassign logger `PanelTemp` definition to **ClimaVue** | **ACCEPTED** (both subjects exist) | **FAIL:** D-S02 explicitly concerns logger wiring panel, not ClimaVue environmental air |
| F36-03: promote separately published `PanelTemp = 25.91098 °C` `SOURCE_EXAMPLE` to `SOURCE_FACT` | **ACCEPTED** (declared evidence standing not independently authenticated) | **FAIL:** D07 is a printed publisher Status example, not research-acquired actual unit telemetry |
| F36-04: add 14 fabricated physical reference components from the `aR7!` response positions | **ACCEPTED** with internally valid links | **FAIL:** D-R01–14 are **fourteen output positions**, not fourteen independent physical sensors or equipment |
| F36-05: change potential `equipment.design_connection` into an actual historic `equipment.role_service` relation | **ACCEPTED** (contract contains that concept) | **FAIL:** D-I02/06 only establishes **documented wiring/possible connection**, not an actual station equipment-service history |

**18/18 means 18 expected *test verdicts*, including the five expected failures of source-fidelity authenticity.** It is NOT a measure of 18 faithfully accepted equipment assertions, complete output coverage or cross-vendor Profile interoperability. The publisher/source examples and test oracle have no standing as a generally usable CSAPI source-authentication service; the project must not add Campbell-name branches to make the generic client return green.

**Coverage gaps retained:** only one ClimaVue `aR7!` temperature-field definition is included, **13/14 original response fields omitted** (including D-R12 humidity-sensor temperature). Only `PanelTemp` of the **31 original publisher Status example fields** is in the packet, **30/31 omitted**. The original candidate input does not contain a complete first-gen sensor output record/schema, compiled logger program, installed bus/address/firmware, full power/network/maintenance or any actual numeric ClimaVue weather result. We cannot derive those missing data from the packet; these are **source→packet coverage omissions or unavailable original field evidence**, not values the generic reader lost. Distinguish an omitted source-known datum from an unavailable source-unknown one.

**Gate E remains NOT CLOSED**: G30-01 still lacks a demonstrably portable producer→independent-client mechanism to communicate and verify source issuer/edition, reference/actual standing, DERIVED/UNKNOWN and same-original-event copies through a justified CSAPI or other agreed carrier. G30-08 is no longer “no parser exists” at the isolated function level, but a compliant common cross-source **Profile-aware end-to-end client and fidelity demonstration has not passed**. The G30-02–07 Bennett historical producer/time/Deployment/quality/backend issues and G30-09 executable-management exclusions remain. **Gate F is not demonstrated**, and this packet does not establish operating-unit interoperability or current/real observations.
