## 41. Step 37 — Bennett's Point historical source-packet challenge and fidelity limits

**3 November 2026 · STEP 37 COMPLETE as ONE bounded real historical station/equipment/measurement source challenge; STEP 38 NEXT, NOT STARTED. Gate E remains NOT CLOSED and Gate F's complete source→Profile→common UI claim is not demonstrated.** This step tests the **same unchanged Step 33 source-independent interpreter** previously exercised on Dragino, AirGradient and Campbell reference packets, but Bennett is **a different evidence kind**: the preserved NOAA and operator records identify **one actual continuing station, actual serial-numbered equipment, and two original historical observations**. The [Step 32 source packet](../Candidate_Specification/examples/step32-source-packets/bennetts-point.historical.packet.json) was copied **byte-identically** into the app as a [read-only test input](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/tests/fixtures/source-packets/bennetts-point.historical.packet.json) (both Git blob **83bd92f22b19a1f6a1f9a4c1e3444e64e546bf60**); the preserved [NOAA/operator source selection](../Candidate_Specification/examples/bennetts-point-ee181.json) was also copied exactly as a [separate test oracle fixture](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/tests/fixtures/source-packets/bennetts-point.source-selection.json) (both blob **38d226842d1b88bda9940748ebb1763a34fa2376**). The generic [interpreter](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/src/profile/source-packet.ts) itself remains the original unchanged Git blob **346d682cbff6a2e97770feba088452df6a067c4e**.

### 41.1 What the original sources establish, and what we checked independently

The original [*ACE Basin (ACE) NERR Meteorological Metadata, January–December 2024*](https://cdmo.baruch.sc.edu/waf/YearlyFiles/ACE%20Basin/meteorological/metadata/acemet01-12.24m.pdf), **updated 18 August 2025**, was opened and **visually checked at its printed pp. 2, 6 and 11** for the sampling/time basis, physical serial/use records and NOAA QA flag meaning. This renewed original PDF check confirms that **Bennett's Point (`acebpmet`) is an actual continuing field station**, the source uses **Eastern Standard Time (EST), UTC−05:00, year-round**, and the **station air temperature is a 15-minute mean of 5-second records**. The PDF identifies **CR1000X logger serial 13916** and **TX321 GOES transmitter serial 2335** as different actual equipment from the temperature/relative-humidity EE181 probes. A transmitter/source-data publisher is not automatically the *producer of the completed 15-minute mean*.

The source states these original EE181 equipment-use facts; **calendar-day dates are preserved at calendar-day precision, and adjacent date boundaries do not invent the same-minute swap**:

| Real physical EE181 unit from operator §9 | Source-defined station role/use statement | Correct scope |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **21281600077814** (sensor 3) | **4 May 2022–8 June 2023** | One identified physical probe's first documented period |
| **222016000109CE** (sensor 4) | **8 June 2023–16 April 2024** | A second identified physical probe during an intervening service period |
| **21281600077814** again | **16 April 2024–still in use as of 31 December 2024** | **Return of the original first physical probe**, not a third individual; **31 Dec is an as-of date, not removal or proof of its current 2026 operation** |

The separate preserved [NOAA/CDMO 2024 source-selection JSON](../Candidate_Specification/examples/bennetts-point-ee181.json) identifies the 2024 original annual archive URL, **5,260,824-byte source snapshot, SHA-256 3f3ee3e5458c00adee770dc9b18a6246e590937a3f0c6c5f48cfc73cd8ae4950**, and **two selected** original station-temperature CSV rows. Their original reported data are:

| Original NOAA archive row / `ATemp` | Original `DatetimeStamp` (12:00 fixed EST) | Original mean °C | Original `F_ATemp` / `F_Record` | Normalization and derived inference |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| **Line 10130, 15 Apr 2024** | `04/15/2024 12:00` | **29.1 °C** | **`"<0> "` (one trailing space)** / separately blank `""` | Source stamp normalized to **17:00 UTC**; nominal **16:45–17:00 UTC** mean interval is **DERIVED**; likely contributor EE181 **222016000109CE** is **DERIVED from a *separate* history source** |
| **Line 10418, 18 Apr 2024** | `04/18/2024 12:00` | **27.3 °C** | **`"<0> "` (one trailing space)** / separately blank `""` | Source stamp normalized to **17:00 UTC**; nominal **16:45–17:00 UTC** interval **DERIVED**; likely contributor **21281600077814** is **DERIVED** |

The NOAA CSV original **does not include the probe serial number on those ATemp rows or an independently established result-availability time (`resultTime`)**. The project join from source station stamp, nominal period and separate day-granular operator use history to a likely probe is **DERIVED**, not a NOAA-authored per-row sensor identifier or independently witnessed device reading. The raw `F_ATemp="<0> "` means **the *air-temperature parameter* passed initial QA/QC**, not certified calibration, station health, original sensor attribution or a validated OMS/SWE `resultQuality` binding. The independent original `F_Record` flag is blank, **not the source of the air-temperature flag**. The rejected 17 April row (`F_ATemp="< -3>"` would be an inaccurate transcription; source token is exactly **`"<-3> [GPR] (CSM)"`**) is **not** a third accepted original mean; it is retained as a negative example, not copied into the selected performed-event inventory.

**Original-source re-verification boundary:** the metadata PDF was reopened and visually inspected; the preserved NOAA source-selection JSON and its original archive SHA were checked as **frozen project evidence**, but **the complete annual CSV was NOT newly downloaded, rehashed or independently re-extracted in Step 37**. Thus do not conflate the renewed operator-PDF review with a fresh proof of raw CSV bytes; prior extraction, source hash and row locators remain the original selected evidence. The frozen selection **does not separately re-extract the TX321 transmitter** even though the full operator PDF confirms **TX321 / serial 2335 on printed p. 11**; the Step 32 packet's transmitter identity is **direct-PDF-grounded**, not proved simply by rereading the reduced JSON selection. The operator's separate **16 April 2024 station maintenance episode (09:45–13:30 EST)** and **17 April CR1000X operating-system update** are **not** exact EE181 installation times or a Command execution history.

### 41.2 What the unchanged Glaux reader actually understood

The unchanged generic `interpretSourcePacket` consumed the full original **project-authored source-evidence packet**, returned `accepted=true` with no **packet-shape/reference** issues for the original, and preserved **five named source subjects**: one source-identified station, **two physically distinct actual EE181 individuals**, one CR1000X logger and one TX321 transmitter. It preserved **23 selected evidence assertions**: **three** calendar-day `equipment.role_service` declarations, two original station temperature values and their source-time/QA strings, **four DERIVED claims** (two nominal averaging windows and two physical contributor joins), **five UNKNOWN claims** (two original `resultTime`, two unchosen completed-mean producers, one missing individual probe position), original source locators and **two original source-event identifiers with four historical experimental representation links**. The per-event source values remain original fact claims, but the derived links are **not silently promoted into source facts**.

| Source-grounded meaning and DIM relationship | What actual project packet/reader preserved | Candidate CSAPI/Profile boundary and unresolved evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **R24-01/02/12:** actual named continuing station and two independently serial-numbered EE181s with date-only use history | `b-station` `SOURCE_IDENTIFIED_STATION`; `b-probe3` and `b-probe4` as two `SOURCE_IDENTIFIED_EQUIPMENT`, with **three distinct day-level service assertions**; same first serial occurs in two service records | Source-supported System/Host/role information, but **no proven exact `Deployment.validTime` or native CSAPI Deployment resource**, no per-probe surveyed geometry |
| **R24-05/09:** actual completed 15-minute station mean, logger processing versus EE181 sensing input versus GOES relay | Two original station `ATemp` rows, separate logger/transmitter identities; contributor assignments qualified as DERIVED | The **one** Part 2 `DataStream.system` producer of the completed mean remains **station H27-A versus logger H27-B**, not the EE181 by convenient default; **G30-02 HOLD** |
| **R24-09:** source station stamp, averaging interval and result availability are different | Original fixed-EST textual station stamps, converted UTC display and separate **DERIVED** 15-minute windows; **both historical original `resultTime` remain UNKNOWN** | Original OMS period-valued phenomenonTime/actual resultTime semantic mapping **G30-04/05 HOLD**; the packet does not provide new source availability evidence |
| **R24-09/14:** result quality belongs to the original measurement parameter, not overall equipment health | Two exact `F_ATemp="<0> "` strings including trailing space and two separately blank `F_Record` fields; issuer/source field preserved | **G30-06 HOLD:** not yet validated as standard SWE/OMS resultQuality, source QA flag `0` means **initial parameter QA** only |
| **R24-15:** original source event ≠ each API digital representation, and original source attribution ≠ project join | `originalEvents` has two original annual CSV `ATemp` report anchors, with **two separately named old experiment copies per original**; source-to-probe associations visibly **DERIVED** | Four old experimental 52°North CSAPI Observation resource IDs **are not four original measurements**; their packet-local link is **NOT a validated native CSAPI same-event relation** |
| **R24-01/13/14:** original source issuer and other status/maintenance context | PDF-supported logger 13916 and GOES TX321 2335; 2024-as-of knowledge, original source locations and nine explicit G30 holds | Not a full whole-station Profile; packet omits separate maintenance/OS event, station-site geometry, most other weather instruments, actual native permissions/command state and other source-described functions |

**Crucial independent consumer limit:** these results show that an unchanged reader can *parse and preserve correct declarations* about a historical case. The same reader **does NOT autonomously verify whether source standing, issuer provenance, original event/representation links, time precision, original observed object or probe serials are factually correct**. Source-specific identities/NOAA row IDs live in **tests**, not hidden branches of the shared parser. A project `originalEventId` or `HISTORICAL_PROJECT_EXPERIMENT` value is not a published, discoverable standard CSAPI source-event equivalence field. We made **no native OGC/SWE mapping or server data repair** in Step 37.

| **Equipment / history** | **EE181 serial 21281600077814** used 2022–23 and returned 16 Apr 2024; **EE181 222016000109CE** interim 2023–24; **three day-qualified role statements describing two individuals** | No invented third probe, exact replacement minute, instant Deployment `validTime`, or individually surveyed EE181 coordinates. Station latitude/longitude in the PDF describe station/site context |
| **Activity / historical measurements** | Two **NOAA historical station 15-minute mean** temperature rows: **29.1 °C** (15 Apr), **27.3 °C** (18 Apr), both stamped **12:00 fixed EST** | These are **two original measurements**, even if the earlier experimental server has **four Observation resources**; no live trend, fabricated third row, or inferred measurement on rejected 17 Apr |
| **Time / provenance** | Nominal **16:45–17:00 UTC** averaging windows clearly marked **DERIVED**; physical probe contribution shown as **DERIVED from date-qualified equipment history** | Original `resultTime` **UNKNOWN**; station clock mark ≠ demonstrated result availability; no native standard period mapping validated |

This is **an informative data-to-view fidelity sketch**. **NO user-visible Glaux UI was built, integrated, tested or deployed during Step 37.** Step 38 is the first separately authorized UI-integration step; this preview does not assert a rendered screen, working map, or authentic CSAPI Profile transport.

### 41.4 Exactly what tests ran, and the eleven SOURCE-FALSE interpretations accepted structurally

The [committed 30-case Bennett test file](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/tests/source-packet.bennetts-point.test.ts) uses **two byte-identical frozen source fixtures** and calls the unchanged generic interpreter. The **30/30 expected test outcomes** passed when the exact fetched TypeScript reader/test logic was run in a **transient V8 JavaScript compatibility harness** by removing TypeScript-only syntax and providing a small assertion adapter. This is **NOT** the repo's installed **Vitest** suite, a complete `npm run typecheck`, production build/browser run, 52°North HTTP read, schema-conformant SWE/CSAPI test, or new physical field verification. The tests are source-historical/descriptive and self-contained; no server/API write, remote device request or deployment occurred.

**13 source-positive/protection checks** confirmed real station/equipment identity, two probes/three date-use statements, two source-original values/four experimental copies, exact raw QA tokens and independently blank record flags, derived UTC windows and physical attribution, UNKNOWN original result availability and producer, unchanged source input, local key/label invariance, original transmitter/operator geometry/maintenance scope and the excluded rejected row. **Six malformed-input mutations were rejected by the generic reader:** nonexistent evidence source; missing physical target; a value on explicit UNKNOWN; orphan copy without original event; duplicate event from exactly the same original source record/field; and a derived assertion referencing nonexistent evidence input.

**Eleven more semantically false packets were structurally ACCEPTED by the generic reader but correctly rejected by the independent, TEST-ONLY original-source oracle:**

| Source-false mutation | Structural parser | Correct original-source disposition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Invent **third actual EE181** with a well-formed serial identity | **ACCEPT** | FAIL; 2024 operator §9 supports only the **two selected real EE181 individuals** for this 2022–24 study window |
| Turn “still used as of 31 Dec 2024” into a **removal date** | **ACCEPT** | FAIL; day-qualified **as-of** cannot become removal event or 2026 status |
| Assign **09:45 EST exact swap** from 16 Apr station maintenance window | **ACCEPT** | FAIL; operator multi-instrument maintenance interval is not a probe-specific clock boundary |
| Exchange first-row contributor from probe 4 to probe 3 | **ACCEPT** | FAIL independent source-use-history join; physical attribution remains **DERIVED** |
| Relabel derived second-row probe attribution as **NOAA SOURCE_FACT** | **ACCEPT** | FAIL; NOAA CSV does not name a physical EE181 serial |
| Trim `F_ATemp="<0> "` or substitute it for separately empty `F_Record` | **ACCEPT** | FAIL; original parameter QA token has **significant trailing space** and is distinct from record-wide flag |
| Invent `resultTime=17:00Z` by changing UNKNOWN to SOURCE_FACT | **ACCEPT** | FAIL; original 12:00 fixed EST is a **station period stamp**, not an independent result-availability record |
| Shift nominal **derived** averaging interval by one minute | **ACCEPT** | FAIL; supported 15-minute projection is 16:45–17:00 UTC and explicitly DERIVED |
| Add **third original NOAA** source event with invented new row locator | **ACCEPT** | FAIL; original selected source supports only **two accepted ATemp originals** |
| Attach 18 Apr archival-report experimental copy to the 15 Apr original event | **ACCEPT** | FAIL; wrong original-event/copy relationship changes provenance despite valid ID pointers |
| Declare the EE181 probe the **verified completed-mean producing System** | **ACCEPT** | FAIL; station-versus-logger producer still unresolved and source physical contributor ≠ final DataStream producer |

**This is the decisive negative result:** a wholly self-consistent project packet can encode false dates, provenance, event counts, observation producer, or historical equipment. Those errors will survive if the downstream client trusts assertion labels without an independently justified original-source/producer contract. **30/30 means all expected test outcomes occurred, including these 11 known semantic FAILs**. It does **not** mean 30/30 source-fidelity PASS, original standards conformance or complete historical truth.

### 41.5 What remains unsupported and exactly what Step 38 may do

**Step 37 is COMPLETE as a bounded, original-source-challenged Bennett case experiment**, not a generic source-authentication success. We have now exercised **one unchanged shared parser** against **three unlike manufacturer reference arrangements and one genuinely source-identified historical station**. The correct original historical statements can survive in the project packet at their proper evidence standing, **but G30-01 is still BLOCKED**: no interoperable, independently discoverable producer→unknown-client original source issuer, reference/individual identity, DERIVED/UNKNOWN, calendar-day precision or source-event/copy equivalence carrier has been accepted and executed in a native CSAPI context. The risk is no longer merely theoretical: the source-false negative mutations demonstrate why a producer's self-reported standing cannot be accepted as independent source proof.

**Other held/excluded obligations remain explicit:** G30-02 the one completed-mean producing `DataStream.system` (station H27-A or logger H27-B, not auto-selected), G30-03 exact original day-only historical Deployment/time and individual probe geometry, G30-04 original result availability, G30-05 native averaged `phenomenonTime` period mapping, G30-06 standard resultQuality from raw NOAA `F_ATemp`, G30-07 historical 52°North SWE media/schema mismatch and currently untested backend behavior, G30-08 complete cross-vendor native CSAPI-to-shared UI fidelity (the parser is only one piece), and G30-09 real authenticated command/physical-effect chain (excluded). Source→packet omissions include additional station sensors, original source events, site position, station maintenance/firmware updates and full source-native program history; do not solve by fabricating the missing system state.

**NEXT ON OWNER'S SEPARATE “PROCEED”: STEP 38 ONLY.** Integrate the common packet interpretation into the **existing Glaux Resource Details working views** without diminishing the generic standard/Raw source inspection or inventing a new full client structure. **Reuse the existing Codex shared Map renderer**, only for trustworthy subject-specific geometry; site location is not an individual probe's position. Preserve a clear local research-packet versus live-native-CSAPI provenance distinction and the evidence standing/UNKNOWN/DERIVED source labels. Keep the existing application branch separate from the research branch; use selective, source-checked fixtures rather than wholesale merging. One positive and negative UI interpretation check is appropriate only if the view truly consumes the same reader. **Do not automatically begin Step 39 real service validation, release audit/publication (Steps 40–43), Oracle/52°North writes, actual Commands or Cloudflare deployment.** If a proposed UI would misrepresent a source-false or unknown field, show it as unverified/unsupported and retain the raw input instead of hiding the failure to achieve a green screenshot.

The working [application branch](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/tree/work/details-view-20261023) now extends the **already existing** [`resource-details-profile-test` Resource Details page](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/src/presentation/resource-details-profile-test.ts), rather than inventing a replacement app or template. The prior generic-versus-candidate rendering controls, Smart Citizen/product-design, earlier published examples, TRSS/OmniSense synthetic cases, and the independent live CSAPI/Bennett pages remain available; original `src/data`/upstream OGC-client behavior was not weakened.

The **same unchanged** [Step 33 `interpretSourcePacket`](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/src/profile/source-packet.ts) (Git blob **`346d682cbff6a2e97770feba088452df6a067c4e`**) now feeds a single [typed source-packet presentation projection](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/src/profile/source-packet-presentation.ts) and a single [generic Resource Details evidence renderer](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/src/presentation/source-packet-details.ts). The code maps to views by the *declared DIM-related concept* (`equipment.*`, `output.*`, `power.*`, `observation.*`, `configuration.*`), evidence standing and original-event/copy relationships, **never** by a matching manufacturer label, known station ID, firmware name or backend URI. This is a **project-authored packet UI mapping**, NOT a newly adopted vocabulary, native CSAPI media type, authenticated provenance envelope or independent source-authority validation.

The existing page's Source case selector accepts four new read-only local research inputs, with direct local routes shown here (these are **application routes, NOT verified deployed URLs**):

### 42.2 Source-to-display meaning, including what the client refuses to assert

| Existing Resource Details view | Evidence-qualified behavior implemented by the one shared renderer | Qualification / remaining source or Profile problem |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Operational → Overview** | Displays one declared focal subject and its standing, selected subjects/claims, original source-event count versus previous experimental API copy count, and UNKNOWN/DERIVED/SOURCE_EXAMPLE counts | Labels are **locally declared in a research packet**; even `accepted=true` is only internal structure/reference validity, NOT independent source authenticity or native CSAPI resource discovery |
| **Operational → Equipment** | Distinguishes reference product kinds from source-identified historical individuals and renders equipment relationships with original source locators and date precision | Two output fields are **not** two physical sensors; a documented cabled design relation is not an actual historical installed Deployment; day-only role statements are not precise time instants |
| **Operational → Communications** | Preserves source-native `output.native_field` and `output.field_count` assertions, plus a documented `equipment.design_connection`, without inventing observed producer associations | A source message/property schema is not an independently witnessed actual DataStream or synchronized physical-device value |
| **Operational → Condition** | Shows original source-rated properties such as a design battery with explicit standing and source references; effective state may remain `UNKNOWN` | Not a current battery charge, sensor health, physically performed command/diagnostic, or measured environmental observation |
| **Activity → Table** | Groups **original source events** by their declared original-event identity; each Bennett original shows its two *previous experimental* copy references; exact NOAA original tokens, DERIVED intervals and probe attribution and UNKNOWN resultTime remain separate | A coherent but source-false packet might misassociate events; the reader does **not authenticate the declared copy link**. This is NOT a native OGC observation response or an automatically validated historical aggregation |
| **Activity → Charts** | Does **not** manufacture a time-series plot from examples, project-derived windows or unsupported actual source result times | No silently inferred `phenomenonTime`, validated resultQuality, per-probe history continuity or current statistical series |
| **Commands → Available / Requests** | Shows why no authenticated ControlStream/permission/issued Command/physical outcome is available in these research packets | No control affordance, endpoint write or device action is enabled |
| **Management → Record** | Shows **the complete unmodified JSON research packet**, including unknown fields and lexical tokens; retaining `F_ATemp="<0> "` and blank `F_Record` is an acceptance target | **Raw project source packet**, not the server's native standard response; existing live/native CSAPI inspector remains a separate unchanged route |
| **Management → Sources / Configuration** | Lists original issuer URL/edition, claim source locators, G30 holds, unrecognized assertions, transformations, reference/actual standing and effective-state unknowns | The **source issuer/edition is packet-declared**, not independently authenticated; raw/source preservation is not OGC Profile adoption |
| **Operational → Map** | Calls the unchanged [shared Codex Resource Details Map renderer](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/src/presentation/resource-details-map.ts) with **null geometry**; reuses its safe **no-position** fallback | None of the four selected packets contains an independently validated **subject-specific GeoJSON Point**. Bennett station-site coordinates from external operator metadata MUST NOT be assigned to a specific EE181 probe |

**The generic/raw mode remains available.** For these new packets the generic button is clearly labeled “Raw packet · not native CSAPI,” preventing the prior generic UI comparison from masquerading as actual CSAPI metadata support. The legacy generic experimental mode and original Standard/raw behavior in the independent live resource paths remain untouched. Original source strings and future/unrecognized fields are rendered safely as text or exposed in complete raw JSON, and invalid packets reveal their parser issues plus raw data instead of populating apparently valid equipment or activity cards.

### 42.3 Exact checks performed and checks NOT performed

**Verified isolated code/input checks:** [12 source-packet UI-projection tests](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/tests/source-packet.presentation.test.ts) passed **12/12 expected outcomes** by running the unchanged shared reader with the new typed UI projection against the four exact research packets in a transient V8 compatibility harness. [Nine source-packet Resource Details component tests](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/tests/source-packet.details.test.ts) passed **9/9** against the actual source renderer with a **minimal fake DOM** and a stubbed shared Map call, verifying that all four Map invocations used **null geometry**. Both harnesses stripped TypeScript-only annotations from the fetched committed logic; **these are not installed project Vitest runs** or proof the complete interface rendered successfully in Chromium. The tests exercise two original Bennett source reports versus four copies, exact QC whitespace, equipment/output role separation, SOURCE_EXAMPLE/DERIVED/UNKNOWN, source-false-but-accepted warning, invalid packet/raw fallback, arbitrary unknown field retention, generic/raw mode, and no authorized commands.

A [repeatable fourteen-check browser smoke script](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/scripts/verify-source-packet-browser.cjs) was added, with `npm run test:browser:source-packet` in `package.json`. **GitHub work scope:** the application's main page router `src/main.ts`, existing [Resource Details template](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/blob/work/details-view-20261023/src/presentation/resource-details-template.ts), shared Map renderer, live-native CSAPI pages, `src/data`, upstream OGC client, Oracle/52°North API, GitHub deployment trigger and Cloudflare production service were not modified. App design and current-work notes were updated. No new original equipment facts were invented, normalized Observation quality/time encoding revised, actual control issued or server resource written.

### 43.2 Native CSAPI format and Profile advertisement ceilings

The checked **/conformance** endpoint returned only **OGC API – Common Part 1 core** as its single advertised URI; it did **not advertise a Connected Systems requirements-class URI** in that specific response. The System collection and two identified Systems returned **application/sml+json** response media headers when the GET requests asked for **application/json**; the two Systems parsed as PhysicalSystem descriptions. Four existing DataStreams and four Observation collections returned **application/json**, each Observation collection containing **one** entry. The experimental request for archived report Observations with **application/swe+json** returned **HTTP 200** and that MIME label with a JSON object and one member; its **SWE Common normative structure was not tested**.

These facts distinguish verified **HTTP transport, content-type observation and JSON parseability** from unproven **OGC API – Connected Systems Part 1/2 conformance, SensorML/SWE Common requirements, OMS averaged-period phenomenonTime, original resultTime, native resultQuality, Deployment and source provenance**. Do not infer every endpoint is nonconformant merely because the conformance list is incomplete, nor claim the SWE representation conformant solely because the returned MIME starts with application/swe+json. The four old scalar/report API resources are **still four representations of two prior original NOAA mean reports**, without a proven standards-native same-original-event equivalence relation.

### 44.1 What was actually compared

For each case, the study traced **original issuer/edition and native source field → R24 source-bounded DIM proposition → conditional PR30 native CSAPI/SensorML/SWE/OMS mapping → exact Step 32 selected packet → shared Glaux Resource Details interpretation → any independently executed native/browser evidence**. The report explicitly distinguishes **PRESERVED (local), SOURCE_EXAMPLE, DERIVED/QUALIFIED, UNKNOWN (source-limited), OMITTED FROM SELECTED PACKET, NATIVE BINDING HOLD, TESTED NATIVE — LIMITED, SOURCE-FALSE counterexample and NOT TESTED/EXCLUDED**. These are report dispositions, not new standardized Profile tokens or a new mandatory DIM taxonomy.

The same four original source situations were maintained without forcing one fixed physical or API chassis:

| [AirGradient Open Air Max](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/AirGradient_Open_Air_Max.md) manufacturer **reference** | 3 referents, 7 claims, **zero real original events** | Two PMS5003 reference physical modules on **PM1/PM2 sockets** kept separate from **pm01/pm25/pm10 properties**; printed 27.7 μg/m³ is SOURCE_EXAMPLE; installed firmware/identity unknown | Other CO₂/SGP41/SHT4x/gas modules, solar/battery and transport/firmware source details not selected; no calibrated or fielded unit inferred |
| [Campbell CR1000X + first-gen ClimaVue 50](../Controlled_Corpus/System_Data_Sheets/Campbell_CR1000X_ClimaVue50.md) **two separate reference kinds** | 3 referents, 8 claims, **zero real original events** | Potential SDI-12 design link, **14 response fields ≠ 14 physical sensors**; one ambient field versus separately published logger PanelTemp source example | **13/14 ClimaVue response field definitions and 30/31 logger Status fields omitted**; no verified installed station or live observations |
| [Bennett's Point](../Candidate_Specification/examples/bennetts-point-ee181.json) **actual historical station** | 5 source-identified referents, 23 claims, **2 original source reports**, **4 project experimental API copies** | Two actual EE181 serials in **three calendar-day service assertions**, two original NOAA **29.1/27.3 °C** means; original F_ATemp **"<0> "** (with trailing space), separate empty F_Record; DERIVED periods/probe associations, original resultTime UNKNOWN | Additional site/meteorological/maintenance details omitted; native exact Deployment, completed-average producing System, original resultTime, OMS/SWE period/quality mapping and source-event-copy carrier **HELD** |

This is a **13-subject / 45-assertion selection**, not an original corpus completeness percentage or a physical device count. **23 deliberately source-FALSE but internally coherent mutated test packets** (3/4/5/11 per case) are still **accepted by structural parsing** and correctly identified as contradictions by the separate *original-source test oracles*. The source-false mutations are **not defects alleged in the unchanged original manufacturer/NOAA record or four original selected packets**. A source-approved producer/trust/evidence contract, not an AI-invented set of manufacturer branches, must decide how to deal with them.

The prior [Step 39 actual read-only validation](Step39_ReadOnly_E2E_Validation_03NOV2026.md) **really passed** full Node 24 TypeScript, **198/198 installed Vitest**, Vite build, **14/14 local source-packet Chromium tests**, **14/14 native 52°North CSAPI GETs HTTP 200 JSON**, and **10/10 specialized live Bennett browser checks with ten cross-origin GETs HTTP 200**. There was **no application code change**, original source rewrite, Oracle/52°North server mutation, device Command or production deployment as part of Step 40.

### 44.3 Cross-case fidelity judgment, not a gate pass

**Meaningfully preserved:** original reference-vs-actual standing; one probe/many output properties; socket-vs-field namespace; logger PanelTemp-vs-ClimaVue air; source FACT-vs-example-vs-derived-vs-UNKNOWN; two genuine NOAA source reports versus four experiment API resource copies; correct date-only history; exact QA string and safe no-position Map behavior. **Not yet represented comprehensively:** many genuine original manufacturer capabilities, other equipment, actual historical station events/site metadata, complete output/status schemas and supporting whole-System context. **Genuinely source-unknown:** fielded serial/active mode and actual telemetry for manufacturer examples, plus Bennett original result availability and exact probe switch time. **Standards/interface HOLD:** source/authenticity/discovery **G30-01**, Bennett mean producer/Deployment/time/quality/backend **G30-02–07**, complete common native client **G30-08**, authenticated physical management **G30-09 EXCLUDED**.

**Gate E NOT CLOSED**, and **Gate F NOT CLOSED** for a full interoperable unknown-client Profile. This is a bounded falsifiable evidence result, not a declaration that the overall research is worthless or that software tests failed. The scorecard documents both the honest progress and the precise reason an unknown client still cannot rely on the full source-to-native-to-UI chain.

**3 November 2026 · STEP 41 COMPLETE as a decision-only audit · STEP 41R-01 NEXT, NOT STARTED · Original plan 41/43 complete plus six separate pending repair increments. Gates E/F NOT CLOSED; Gate G NOT REACHED.** The complete [source-linked Step 41 release-blocker register and six bounded repair definitions](Step41_Release_Blocker_Dispositions_and_Bounded_Repair_Sequence_03NOV2026.md) governs this summary; do not substitute this short section for the evidence clauses and acceptance criteria in the full decision. The [controlling 43-step plan](../PLAN_DIM_Profile_V2_Whole_System_Recovery_to_Delivery_v0.1_31OCT2026.md#inserted-step-41-repair-sequence--each-requires-a-separate-owner-proceed-all-pending) now records the extensions explicitly rather than pretending the original 43 steps or substantive Profile claim are complete.

### 45.1 The actual judgment

The four original source situations and the Step 40 scorecard remain authoritative: three manufacturer design/reference cases (Dragino S31-LB, AirGradient Open Air Max, Campbell CR1000X + first-generation ClimaVue 50) with **no actual observed field events**; one historically source-identified Bennett station with **two actual EE181 probes**, three day-qualified service statements, **two** original NOAA 2024 mean air temperatures and **four project API representations of those two original reports**. The selected Step 32 fixtures describe **13 subjects / 45 assertions**, not complete vendor operating systems. **Twenty-three** intentionally source-FALSE but structurally valid mutations were detected by original-source test oracles, not by the generic packet shape checks. Those false mutations are *not* errors in the unchanged original source/packet files.

**Actual Step 39 positive software evidence is retained but bounded:** [Actions run 41609815744](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/actions/runs/41609815744) passed **198/198 Vitest**, **14/14 local source-packet browser**, **14/14 native Node read-only GETs**, and **10/10 specialized live Bennett Chromium checks** with ten 200-OK native browser GETs. The source-independent Resource Details page reads **local project JSON**; the Bennett live page is a **separately specialized, manually curated native CSAPI reader**. We did not test an unknown client recovering arbitrary producer-issued source standing/DERIVED/UNKNOWN/original-event identity through a server-advertised Profile. **Gate E G30-01 and Gate F remain open.**

**Original-standard checks for Step 41:** [OGC API – Connected Systems Part 1 (23-001 v1.0), §19.2.7 Requirement 100](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-001/23-001.html) distinguishes source-backed **Procedure hardware datasheets** from actual Systems, and its associated requirements classes cannot be claimed from a lone Common-core /conformance URI. [SensorML 3.0 §8.2.2.1 Requirements 9–10](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-000/23-000.html) permits separately namespaced extensions but does **not** require every ordinary SensorML client to interpret them. [CSAPI Part 2 (23-002 v1.0), §9 and §§16.1–16.2](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) has separate DataStream producer, required Observation time, JSON schema and SWE Common mapping obligations. A custom packet, MIME label and HTTP 200 **do not** prove conformance; nor does a missing advertised class alone prove that every actual server endpoint fails. Complete [OMS 3.0 original and preserved text](../Primary_Sources/OGC_20-082r4_OMS_3.0.0.md) remain available for any future historical Observation claim.

### 45.2 Eight issue groups receive actual, distinct decisions

| F40 group | Decision reached in Step 41 | Future acceptance and scope |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **F40-01: portable producer/Profile carrier** | **BLOCKER.** Project packet is not an OGC-published/discoverable source-standing/derivation/event-equivalence contract. | **41R-01** decide standards-grounded native or explicitly project-defined carrier and client discovery, **41R-05/06** validate actual producer→generic consumer; else HOLD. |
| **F40-02: true source/producer assertions** | **BLOCKER to source-authentic claim.** All 23 source-false tests correctly show structural parser cannot authenticate sources; an issuer string is not an original publisher signature. | **41R-02** establish producer accountability, verifiable source locators/editions and source-oracle negatives, separately label unverifiable external assertions. |
| **F40-03: requirements class/media/schema** | **BLOCKER to native CSAPI/Profile conformance claims.** Step 39 checked Common core advertising, JSON media variation and SWE header without body certification. | **41R-01/05** select exact required class/format and validate real advertised schema/body; actual live 52°North requires separate proof. |
| **F40-04: averaged history** | **HOLD full native Bennett mean Observation.** Producer station vs logger undecided; original resultTime unavailable; nominal interval, raw QC and native quality mapping qualified. | Preserve original **29.1/27.3 °C**, exact F_ATemp token with trailing blank, blank F_Record, DERIVED probe/time and UNKNOWN original resultTime; no false surrogate claim. |
| **F40-05: dated service/geography** | **HOLD exact-time native Deployment and per-probe coordinates.** Evidence is two probes, three **date-only** assignments, 2024 as-of and a site point. | Preserve role history and station geometry in qualified description; no invented replacement clock, removal date or individual probe position. |
| **F40-06: source selection completeness** | **BLOCKER to broad whole-System coverage.** Omitted Dragino 20-minute default/FPort/status, AirGradient modules/power/transport, Campbell **13 other ClimaVue fields and 30 Status fields**, Bennett site/instrument/maintenance context. | **41R-03/04** make a source inventory→selection→client crosswalk for two cases per turn, with high-value repairs and explicit remaining omissions. Do not call missing source detail “parser loss.” |
| **F40-07: native generic consumer** | **BLOCKER.** Local generic research packet and specialized live Bennett paths have never been connected through discovered standards/Profile semantics. | **41R-05** native served read-only test producer, **41R-06** one unchanged Glaux discovery/interpretation path and source truth/browser challenges; no hard-coded vendor/resource IDs. |
| **F40-08: actual commands/permissions** | **EXCLUDED EXECUTABLE MANAGEMENT** from present descriptive candidate. | Retain documented capabilities but no authenticated ControlStream, Command write, correlated outcome or physical effect claim without a separate authorized evidence program. |

**Totals are issue counts, not percentages:** **five repair-dependent groups** (01/02/03/06/07); **two historical native source/format HOLDs** (04/05); **one executable control exclusion** (08). These are different dispositions. The source facts and source limits that motivate them are linked in the [Step 40 scorecard](Step40_Four_Case_Source_to_Client_Fidelity_Scorecard_03NOV2026.md#8-candidate-step-41-blocker-handoff--no-repairs-performed-here). **Excluding unsupported stronger behavior does not itself satisfy the remaining descriptive-profile interoperability gate.**

## 46. Step 41R-01 — Original-standard Profile-discovery and source-evidence carrier decision

**3 November 2026 · STEP 41R-01 COMPLETE as a documentary, source-linked carrier-choice iteration ONLY; STEP 41R-02 NEXT; no native/server/client implementation and no new interoperability conformance PASS.** The full [standards-to-Profile carrier decision](Step41R01_Standards_Profile_Carrier_Decision_03NOV2026.md) is authoritative for the comparative options, normative-versus-project boundary, candidate Web discovery path and twelve machine-testable positive/negative future acceptance scenarios. Its native OGC and RFC sources were inspected, and the saved complete OMS 3.0 searchable text was checked for the actual average `phenomenonTime`, independent `resultTime`, Observer, Deployment and resultQuality constraints. This was **not** a complete rerun of all historical source or executable software tests.

### 46.1 Human finding, with correct original authority

**SELECTED CANDIDATE:** native, actually valid **OGC API – Connected Systems (CSAPI) `Procedure` or `System` representation** + **registered RFC 8288/RFC 6906 `profile` link** + **registered `describedby` link** to a separately **project-defined, versioned and resource-bound source-evidence document**. The required additional evidence vocabulary is an explicit **proposed Profile convention**, not an invented original OGC member. An unknown **Profile-aware** client must have learned the project Profile contract, but need not know any manufacturer name, model string, source-packet fixture path or private Bennett resource IDs. A regular CSAPI client without this Profile must continue to interpret valid native resource content without being forced to understand our evidence extension.

**Original-source reasons:** [OGC API – Common – Part 1 v1.0.0 §§6.3–6.4, 9.1–9.3](https://docs.ogc.org/is/19-072/19-072.html) permits additional landing-page typed links, uses `describedby`, and recommends HTTP `Link` header mirroring. [RFC 8288 §§2–3,5](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc8288.html) defines link context and headers but warns that blindly following linked sources is not secure. [RFC 6906 §§1,3–4](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc6906.html) registers `profile` to announce additional constraints/semantics **without altering media-type-native meaning**; the URI itself is an identifier and does not prove conformance. [SensorML 3.0 §8.2.2.1 Req9–10](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-000/23-000.html) explicitly permits non-SensorML namespaces but **does not require non-Profile-aware SensorML readers to understand them**. [CSAPI Part 1 §13/§19.2.7 Req100](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-001/23-001.html) governs `Procedure` source design/datasheet representations and their no-position constraint; [CSAPI Part 2 §§9.2,16.1–16.2](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-002/23-002.html) retains actual DataStream producer and Observation format/time/schema requirements. None of those standards already supplies the **complete project SOURCE_EXAMPLE/DERIVED/UNKNOWN and original-event-copy source-attestation vocabulary**.

**Options compared:** (A) native CSAPI/SensorML metadata/extensions only — **insufficient alone** for a required common provenance/unknown/event-copy interpretation; (B) registered links plus separate project source-evidence sidecar — **selected for bounded falsification** without corrupting native resource bodies; and (C) full mandatory W3C PROV-O/JSON-LD graph — **deferred**, as additional RDF structure does not by itself settle project source standing, missing facts, original-event equivalence or truth and would substantially increase complexity before necessity is proved. W3C provenance terms remain eligible for targeted future reuse.

### 46.2 Specific selected boundary and still-open obligations

A genuine native `Procedure` source model must not be represented as an actual installed `System`. A `System` with original identity may link to a valid type Procedure **only when independently published/resolvable**. The **per-native-resource** `profile` link identifies an exact Profile edition, while `describedby` retrieves a schema-validated, read-only project evidence description **for precisely that resource**. A link only on the API root is not sufficient to assert all nested resources support the Profile. HTTP `Link` response headers allow this to be proposed **without illegal new native JSON fields**; the future actual endpoint must allow Glaux to read headers in a browser, including `Access-Control-Expose-Headers: Link` for cross-origin access where necessary.

The evidence document must identify its **integrating producer separately from original issuer**, original document/edition/location/units/lexical value and time precision, real *kind/reference vs actual individual/station* standing, **SOURCE_FACT/SOURCE_EXAMPLE/DERIVED/UNKNOWN** as **project vocabulary**, original source-event/copy links and honest source-verification status. This **does not certify** the original publisher's assertions or make a fourth API copy a fourth NOAA event; the 23 source-false mutations remain source-oracle failures even if valid structurally. Profile ID, host/route, final schema and independent attestation policy still require next-step work and actual tests. The chosen contract must not be put into native `/conformance` as a falsely adopted OGC requirements class.

The twelve **C41R-P01–P12** positive and **C41R-N01–N12** negative test contracts in the [decision document §5](Step41R01_Standards_Profile_Carrier_Decision_03NOV2026.md#5-machine-testable-producerconsumer-acceptance-contracts-for-future-steps) cover exact version discovery, native-to-evidence identity matching, actual native class/media validation, truthful source/producer separation, example-vs-event, physical component/field differences, original temporal precision, Bennett **2 actual NOAA originals/4 experimental copies** and QA raw text, the 23 false source mutations, honest control/Observation exclusions, browser header visibility and independent reproducibility. They are **planned test definitions only**, not passed endpoint or browser checks.

**Decision state:** **41R-01 COMPLETE as a bounded original-source selection and planned contract**, but **G30-01/F40-01, Gates E and F NOT CLOSED**, and Gate G not reached. Original source or existing Profile rules were **not** revised in this step. The 198/198 Vitest, 14/14 local browser, 14/14 native GET and 10/10 live Bennett browser evidence remain **earlier Step 39 actual results**, not new tests. 41R-02 must handle issuer, source verification and all 23 original-source counterexamples. Until then source claims are producer **assertions**, not independently authenticated manufacturer/NOAA issuance.

**NEXT ON ONE OWNER “PROCEED”: STEP 41R-02 ONLY.** Source-assertion authority, original issuer versus integrating publisher, source-document/edition/locator verification and negative source-oracle acceptance criteria for all four cases, preserving 23 source-FALSE mutations without vendor name branches. Do not start coverage repair, native producer, Glaux integration, actual Commands, Oracle writes, Cloudflare deploy or publication in that same prompt.


## 47. Step 41R-02 — Producer-side source assertion accountability and source-false truth oracles

**3 November 2026 · STEP 41R-02 COMPLETE as source-scoped producer claim/verification contract and actually executed bounded tests · 41R-03 NEXT. Original plan 41/43 complete; inserted repairs 41R-01 and 41R-02 of six complete; 41R-03–06 and original 42–43 pending.** The full source-linked [producer assertion contract, all 23 negative cases, four new issuer controls and exact execution record](Step41R02_Producer_Source_Assertion_Contract_and_Oracles_03NOV2026.md) governs this summary. Its runnable, read-only [producer/source-oracle program](step41r02-source-oracles.cjs) sits **only on the research branch**, outside the generic Glaux decoder. It is **not a new source packet, OGC Profile schema, signed publisher statement, native CSAPI implementation or deployment**.

### 47.1 Why this matters, in ordinary language

The Step 41R-01 source-evidence sidecar approach may eventually let an unfamiliar, Profile-aware client *discover* source claims, but **discoverability does not make the claims true**. We must keep **original manufacturer/NOAA issuer** separate from the **integrating producer/CSAPI API provider**. A producer should not be allowed to claim that a published sample was performed telemetry, that a manufacturer reference product is an installed serial, that a logger status reading is ambient air, or that NOAA recorded a precise time/result/serial that its archive never actually supplied.

The bounded producer source-oracle contract therefore demands **exact original URL, edition/commit/captured snapshot, native field/row locator, original lexical value/unit/time precision, claimed real referent, SOURCE_FACT/SOURCE_EXAMPLE/DERIVED/UNKNOWN standing, transformation inputs, and the person/process/test making the integration assertion**. Independently verified selected-source consistency is **not** the same as cryptographic original issuer authentication. The contract distinguishes **CONTROLLED_SOURCE_CONSISTENT**, **PRODUCER_ASSERTED_UNVERIFIED**, **CONTROLLED_SOURCE_CONTRADICTED**, and **SOURCE_INSUFFICIENT / NATIVE_BINDING_HELD** as **newly proposed project source-audit dispositions only**, not native OGC vocabulary or publisher signatures.

### 47.2 Exact checks run and original source standing

The isolated JavaScript V8 test execution read the **four committed Step 32 research JSON fixtures plus frozen Bennett original selection** directly from their research branch paths, then exercised [the committed source-scoped oracle functions](step41r02-source-oracles.cjs). Four **unchanged positive packet-versus-controlled-original checks passed (4/4)**. **All 23 historic source-FALSE mutations were identified (3 Dragino + 4 AirGradient + 5 Campbell + 11 Bennett), 23/23.** Four **additional producer-issuer/authority mismatch controls** were also detected, **4/4**, without retroactively changing the historic 23 count. These mutations were generated transiently in memory and do not change original packet files.

Source audit corroboration was refreshed **only to the extent described** in the [41R-02 main record §2](Step41R02_Producer_Source_Assertion_Contract_and_Oracles_03NOV2026.md#2-verified-source-population-edition-and-independent-source-access-status): original Dragino model manual and pinned decoder; **original pinned AirGradient hardware README and separate original pinned firmware README**, both directly retrieved by their repository/commit IDs; Campbell first-generation **08/2024** manual Table 8-1 text and earlier source-indexed logger Status; **previously preserved** NOAA/ACE Basin Bennett selected rows and serial-use history. The entire NOAA annual 2024 CSV and ACE Basin 2025-updated metadata PDF were **not freshly downloaded/rehashed**, and the original Campbell PDF page-image screenshot was not available via the page service. Neither a material hash nor a source's issuer label provides a signature or owner authorization.

The new test was run **in isolated V8 with actual fetched JSONs**, **not** a new Node CLI, GitHub Actions workflow, installed Vitest, live browser, native server GET, Cloudflare deployment or device command. The script is reproducible with `node Docs/Research/DIM_Profile_V2/Whole_System_Recovery/step41r02-source-oracles.cjs` from a research-branch checkout, but that CLI was not newly executed in this ChatGPT turn. Historical [Step 39 GitHub Actions 41609815744](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/actions/runs/41609815744) remains the independently executed full app/build/browser/native transport evidence for the **earlier** app revision and separate purposes.

### 47.3 Disposition and exact next step

**F40-02 has a concrete bounded producer-side test method and known-false classification**; **cryptographically authenticated original issuer/unknown arbitrary producer source truth is NOT CLAIMED**, so the larger source-authenticity/release risk is **not fully closed**. The source-oracle program and author contract are *not* injected into Glaux or the project Profile's previously proposed PR30 semantic rules. The selected source packets remain intentionally narrow and **F40-06 whole-System coverage omissions remain**. G30-01/F40-01 portable native producer Profile, F40-03 actual conformance/schema and F40-07 generic native client still BLOCK release; Bennett native history F40-04/05 remains HOLD and executable control F40-08 EXCLUDED. **Gate E NOT CLOSED, Gate F NOT CLOSED, Gate G NOT REACHED.**

**NEXT ON ONE OWNER “PROCEED”: STEP 41R-03 ONLY.** For **Dragino S31-LB and AirGradient Open Air Max**, define and implement a bounded original-source inventory→selected packet→unchanged generic reader coverage improvement, including **source-known 20-minute Dragino design default/FPort/status information** and **AirGradient other modules/power/transport original distinctions**. Preserve reference-only standing and leave effective state/actual telemetry UNKNOWN. Do not begin Campbell/Bennett 41R-04, native server 41R-05, generic client 41R-06, publication 42–43, Oracle writes, actual Commands or Cloudflare deployment in that same iteration.

## 50. Step 41R-05 — Actual read-only native CSAPI GeoJSON producer, linked source evidence, and HTTP/schema checks

**3 November 2026 · Step 41R-05 COMPLETE only at the demonstrated isolated source-to-native-HTTP producer scope; 41R-06 NEXT, NOT STARTED.** The [complete original-standard- and source-linked Step 41R-05 report](Step41R05_Read_Only_Native_CSAPI_Producer_and_Profile_Evidence_03NOV2026.md) and [reproducible Python producer/test/schema directory](Step41R05_Native_Producer/README.md) govern this short cumulative summary. ### 50.1 Human explanation

Previously all four locally curated source packets demonstrated **one shared Glaux interpretation** but not a producer transmitting standards-native source identity/standing through a discoverable wire path. We now have a *real, separately executable, read-only local HTTP producer* which publishes **two unlike original-source situations in genuine OGC API – Connected Systems Part 1 GeoJSON**. **Dragino S31-LB** is a **Procedure** representing the manufacturer's **reference/datasheet model**, not a made-up installed System. **Bennett's Point** is a **System** representing the original 2024 source-identified continuing station, not an invented model or new instantaneous Observation.

For each native resource, both the actual **HTTP Link header** and native resource body's \`links\` carry the registered RFC \`profile\` and \`describedby\` relations. The latter is a **separately read-only served project evidence document**, with exact resource URI and integrating UID binding, original issuer/edition/locator, 21 source-qualified Dragino assertions or 37 Bennett source assertions, plus Bennett's two original NOAA means/four older project API copies and source raw QA. The published source-evidence vocabulary is **our proposed project Profile convention**, not an OGC native member, original producer signature, original instrument serial or new permitted Observation.

### 50.2 Actually verified CI and original schema requirements

[GitHub Actions 41652689569](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/actions/runs/41652689569) ran **12/12 actual loopback HTTP/URI/media/schema/negative integration checks successfully** on Python 3.12 with test-only \`jsonschema==4.26.0\`. A subsequent documentation-triggered [run 41653054720](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/actions/runs/41653054720) also **COMPLETED SUCCESS**, preserving that tested producer implementation. The test checked the real native \`application/geo+json\` \`Procedure\` and \`System\` response bodies against pinned **original OGC 23-001 v1.0.0** \`geojson/procedure.json\`, \`system.json\` and feature collection schemas plus their published GeoJSON transitive references. It checked real source-link header/JSON parity, CORS header exposure, GET evidence and schema validation, exact source standing/UID/canonical URL, different resource kinds, deliberate wrong subject/issuer binding, unsupported Accept/IDs/versions and HTTP write 405 rejection.

The original [41R-02 read-only source-oracle program](step41r02-source-oracles.cjs) was run in the same successful workflow: **4/4 original frozen packet source-ledger positives**, all **23/23** historical structural-but-original-source-false mutants flagged and **4/4** new issuer/authority false declarations detected. Those checks do **NOT** cryptographically authenticate an arbitrary original publisher. The first [test workflow 41652011442](https://github.com/OS4CSAPI/glaux-client/actions/runs/41652011442) detected a Python JSON Schema resolver bug (three references wrongly resolved after an external GeoJSON \`$id\` scope), not false original measurements or native body structure; the harness then canonicalized only relative references **in-memory**, leaving official vendored schemas unchanged, and subsequent CI passed.

**Documented original normative sources:** [OGC 23-001 Part 1 §§9,13,19.1 GeoJSON Req 77,81–82,85–86](https://docs.ogc.org/is/23-001/23-001.html), [OGC API Common Part 1](https://docs.ogc.org/is/19-072/19-072.html), [RFC 8288](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc8288), [RFC 6906](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc6906). Original published OGC/GeoJSON schema files are copied unchanged with source release/sha citations in [Step 41R-05 README](Step41R05_Native_Producer/README.md#original-standard-clauses-and-pinned-schemas-not-generated-project-syntax). The new sidecar schema is **project authored** and distinct from those originals.

## 51. Step 41R-06 — Actual native CSAPI producer→one generic Glaux Resource Details client, with Gate E/F HOLD

**3 November 2026 · 41R-06 COMPLETE at tested read-only local native producer + actual Chromium end-to-end scope; all SIX targeted 41R repairs executed. Original 43-step plan: 41/43 complete; original Step 42 NEXT and Step 43 PENDING.** The complete [Step41R06 source, code, actual CI, source-falsification and Gate E/F report](Step41R06_Generic_Native_CSAPI_Profile_Client_03NOV2026.md) governs this workbook summary. Original [Step40 F40-01–08 release issues](Step40_Four_Case_Source_to_Client_Fidelity_Scorecard_03NOV2026.md), [Step41 disposition register](Step41_Release_Blocker_Dispositions_and_Bounded_Repair_Sequence_03NOV2026.md) and [41R01–05 source and native producer evidence](Step41R05_Read_Only_Native_CSAPI_Producer_and_Profile_Evidence_03NOV2026.md) remain separately attributable.

### 51.1 What changed in terms of the project's actual goal

Earlier source-based Glaux Resource Details demonstrations **received local project packets chosen by our own code**. They did not establish how an unfamiliar native Connected Systems API (CSAPI) publisher could tell a Profile-aware client what specific equipment/resource referent it was, which original source asserted an equipment/field/example/event, what had been inferred, what was genuinely unknown and which apparent API observations were digital copies of one original source event. The old specialized Bennett native viewer was a *different* route.

Now **one real read-only native producer and one generic Glaux client path actually cooperate over HTTP**, using **two unlike original-source cases**. The client is given a native `Procedure` or `System` resource URL, not a special “Dragino” or “Bennett” case identity. It GETs an actual native `application/geo+json` feature, reads that resource's registered RFC `profile` and `describedby` links in the **actual HTTP header and native body**, GETs the exact versioned **project-owned source evidence** for that resource, validates its linked UID/href, source referent and internal source locators, and then hands the source-qualified assertions to the **SAME Step33/38 Glaux reader and Resource Details renderer**. No special manufacturer-string or preselected source UUID branch was added for native interpretation.

The original source semantics survive. The **Dragino S31-LB manufacturer reference Procedure** keeps one externally cabled SHT31/two properties and a printed `AT+TDC=60000 ms` example distinct from the 20-minute documented default and **UNKNOWN effective installed setting**. The **real Bennett's Point historical station System** preserves two physical EE181 serials/three date-only service uses, station-only source geometry, other historical equipment, **two NOAA original 2024 mean air-temperature source acts versus four old experimental CSAPI copies**, exact `F_ATemp="<0> "` original trailing space and blank `F_Record`, DERIVED mean interval/probe attribution and **UNKNOWN original resultTime/actual completed-mean producing System**. No fake performed Dragino/Campbell/AirGradient Observation, native Part 2 quality mapping, exact Deployment, per-probe coordinate or device Command was produced.
